# Item mechanics you can give more items

Some items needed a mechanic of their own, which the module now has. Each mechanic finds its items by their wording. When an item's wording differs, you can still give it the mechanic by adding it to that mechanic's **EXTRA** table, keyed by the item's PF2e slug (the `system.slug` field, such as `burial-oil`). A table entry takes precedence over the wording.

After adding an entry, run `node --test --test-isolation=none test/*.test.js` and `node tools/census.mjs` (or `tools/census-permanent.mjs` for a permanent item's activation), and the item's row should change.

## A weapon lent a property rune for a while

- **What it does:** the item card lists its user's weapons. Apply gives the user an Effect with PF2e's own AdjustStrike rule, which adds the rune to the weapon picked for the item's time. This is how PF2e's own Wounding Oil works. For a weapon that lends itself the rune, there's no picker.
- **Items now:** Burial Oil, Burial Oil (Greater) (Vitalizing, Greater Vitalizing), Oil of Swiftness (Quickstrike).
- **Wording read:** "… the benefits of a Vitalizing rune for 1 minute", "gains the effects of a Greater Frost rune", and "Until the end of your turn". Items that link PF2e's own Effect for it keep that Effect (Wounding Oil, Ghost Oil, Oil of Keen Edges).
- **Add an item:** `src/compiler/rune-loans.js`, `EXTRA`:

  ```js
  export const EXTRA = Object.freeze({
      "my-oil": { name: "Ghost Touch", duration: { mode: "fixed", value: 1, unit: "minutes", expiry: "turn-start" }, own: false }
  });
  ```

  `name` is the rune's English name as PF2e shows it. Live play finds PF2e's key from it (Vitalizing is PF2e's `disrupting`, Quickstrike `speed`). `own: true` gives the item itself the rune when it's a weapon.

## One of several PF2e actions, picked on the card

- **What it does:** the card picks the action. Apply rolls PF2e's own action, with the item's bonus and with a skill in place of the usual one, then puts that action's result on the target.
- **Items now:** The Bear (Grapple, Shove or Trip, +2 status), Blade of the Rabbit Prince (Trip or Disarm using Thievery, its potency as an item bonus).
- **Wording read:** "You attempt to Grapple, Shove, or Trip the creature …", "You gain a +2 status bonus to your check", "using Thievery instead of Athletics", "add the …'s item bonus to the check".
- **Add an item:** `src/compiler/item-actions.js`, `EXTRA`:

  ```js
  export const EXTRA = Object.freeze({
      "my-whip": { slugs: ["trip", "disarm"], bonus: { value: 1, type: "item" }, statistic: "acrobatics" }
  });
  ```

## An action at the item's modifier, when that's higher than its user's

- **What it does:** PF2e's own action, plus an untyped modifier of the difference when the user's skill is lower than the item's.
- **Items now:** the third-party Splitting Tree Splatters and the Lesser and Greater Ravenous Flytraps.
- **Wording read:** "use a +N Athletics modifier for your <action> instead of your Athletics modifier if it is higher than your own" (the item's modifier for that action, in place of the user's when higher).
- **And its bonus to the user's own:** a later sentence giving an item bonus to the check when the user's own modifier is used (`minimum.ownBonus`). The user's own with the bonus when that's higher, else the item's modifier.
- **Add an item:** the same `EXTRA` in `src/compiler/item-actions.js`:

  ```js
  "my-vine": { slug: "grapple", minimum: { value: 7, skill: "athletics" } }
  ```

## A condition until the end of its user's rage

- **What it does:** while its user is raging, the item card gives them an Effect that grants the condition (PF2e's GrantItem) for at most a minute. When the user's Rage Effect ends, the module removes it. If the user isn't raging, the card says so.
- **Items now:** the Barbarians+ Instinct Crowns (Wildblood, Elemental, Bloodrager): quickened.
- **Wording read:** "You become quickened until the end of your rage". What the extra action may be used for stays a note.
- **Add an item:** `src/compiler/rage-time.js`, `EXTRA`:

  ```js
  export const EXTRA = Object.freeze({
      "my-crown": { conditions: [{ slug: "quickened", value: null }] }
  });
  ```

  A valued condition takes its value (`{ slug: "frightened", value: 1 }`).

## All of an item's own linked PF2e Effects at once

- **What it does:** when an item links several PF2e Effects named for itself, the item card usually applies only the one matching the item and lists the others as notes. With this mechanic it applies all of them.
- **Items now:** Thorn of Milani (Lesser): its Spirit Damage and Unholy Weakness Effects.
- **Wording read:** every link is "Effect: <item> (…)", none names a degree or a choice, and the text says one, then the other ("You then gain …").
- **Add an item:** `src/compiler/consumables.js`, `EXTRA_ALL_LINKS`, a list of slugs:

  ```js
  export const EXTRA_ALL_LINKS = Object.freeze(["my-talisman"]);
  ```

## The module's own items: a spell on a spellgun's hit, a firearm merged with a staff

These two belong to the module's own items (`packs/_source/items`), not to PF2e's, so no wording is read: each item asks for what it is when it first goes into an inventory (`scripts/generic-items.js`).

- **A spell's results on a spellgun's hit** (`spellgunFromSpell` in `src/compiler/generic-spellgun.js`): the spell module's plan for the spell, heightened to a rank, moved under an attack against AC. A save spell's failure is the hit, and its critical failure the critical hit. A spell attack spell keeps its own rows. A spell with neither deals its data's damage (not a healing spell's healing). The spell is its 2-action version (`ACTIONS`, a spellgun's Activate): PF2e's action variants through the core's `planWithActions`, and Force Barrage's shards through its `missileCount`. The plan is kept on the item, so compileItem, live play and the Combat Simulator need neither module again. Another item that holds a picked spell could use the same function: `spellgunFromSpell(spellPlan, { spell, rank })`, then `loadedUpdate(spell, rank, plan)` for its name, level, price and description. The spell's own "@item.rank" and "@item.level" (in its rows' Effect rules and its text's inline rolls) are written as the rank loaded (`withRankResolved`, 2026-10-20): PF2e would read them from the gun, its level and not the spell's rank, and from nothing once the gun is used up, so the card's rolls failed. A gun loaded before is read the same way (`loadedPlan`).
- **A spellgun from PF2e's maker of scrolls and wands** (`scripts/generic-items.js`, 2026-10-20): a spell dropped on a sheet's Inventory opens PF2e's maker, whose kinds come from `CONFIG.PF2E.spellcastingItems`. The module adds a Spellgun there (one for a spell of a rank, one for a cantrip), with the Generic Spellgun as its item. PF2e's crafting crafts only a scroll, a wand or a spell gem from an item at one of those uuids, so the maker gets the gun's uuid without "Item." (Foundry reads it the same), and the Generic Spellgun itself is still crafted. The maker's item is stopped as it's created (`fromMaker`: the gun's flag, the maker's spell, PF2e's name for it) and the spellgun is made loaded instead at the maker's rank, mystified if asked. Two of the same stack (its traits sorted and its HTML as Foundry keeps it). A spell a hit can't carry makes nothing. A spell dropped on a Generic Spellgun's sheet or its inventory row opens its picker with that spell; loading another spell clears the old plan first, since an update merges flags.
- **A firearm merged with a staff** (`gunStaffUpdate` in `src/compiler/gun-staff.js`): the firearm's data, the staff's spell list, traits, rules and runes (at least +1), and a Reinforced Stock attached that keeps the runes. `isBaseFirearm` holds the limit to two-handed firearms; to allow others, widen it and `firearmProblem` together.

## Runes

- **Etched runes' activations** (`src/compiler/runes.js`): a rune key on a weapon or armor (`system.runes.property`) is matched to PF2e's rune item by its slug ("extending-greater" → "greaterExtending"). The runes PF2e renamed keep their old keys in `RENAMED` (Quickstrike `speed`, Vitalizing `disrupting`, Spell Reservoir `spellStoring`, Animated `dancing`): add a rune there if its key doesn't match.
- **Runes on a critical hit** (`src/compiler/rune-strikes.js`): each "On a critical hit …" sentence of a rune's text is read by the ability compiler. Who it's for is in `FOR` ("the undead", "the giant", a bleeding target): add a pattern there for another rune that names its creatures.
- **Etching** (`src/compiler/etching.js`): the fundamental runes by slug are in `FUNDAMENTAL`; which items a rune fits, by its usage, in `fitProblem`.

## Read from the wording only

These mechanics have no table yet. An item gets one when its text uses the same words. To give one a table, follow the pattern above, or ask me.

- **Spellguns** (`src/compiler/spellguns.js`): "making your choice of a spell attack roll or a firearm attack roll against the target's AC" (or a defense's DC), then the damage the text links and its degree rows. The card offers the user's spell attack and firearm attack. Reaper's Spellgun's damage in its critical success's row is read too.
- **One of two maneuvers at the item's modifier** (`src/compiler/own-checks.js`): "attempting to Disarm or Trip (your choice) … with a total of +9 to the Athletics check" (Twisting Twine).
- **Warriors that Strike at the item's modifier** (`src/compiler/own-checks.js`): "Two warriors manifest with a +11 attack modifier", each "makes a Strike for 2d6 force" (Spiritual Warhorn) With fewer enemies targeted than warriors, the rest Strike them again in turn, a card each, every Strike's damage its own roll (2026-10-25).
- **Damage to a creature that touches the drinker** (`retaliationPlan` in `src/compiler/direct-damage.js`): "a creature that touches you … takes fire damage. The aura deals 1d4 fire damage" (the Retaliation potions). The card stays open for the item's time.
- **Damage a successful save negates** (`negatingSavePlan` in `src/compiler/direct-damage.js`): "dealing 1d6 persistent poison damage to all creatures …; a successful … save negates the damage" (Mycoweave Shield).
- **Effects for allies or another creature** (`grantsOthers` in `src/compiler/consumables.js`): "You and all allies within a 60-foot emanation gain …", "a creature you touch", "Choose an adjacent creature …".
- **Conditions lowered on others, one of several, or to 0** (`src/compiler/reduce.js`): "Reduce the ally's frightened condition by 1", "your clumsy, enfeebled, frightened, or stupefied condition by 1", "Reduce your doomed value to 0".
  - Each one its user has: Flickering Aeon Stone's Enter Stasis, "If you have the clumsy, drained, enfeebled, or stupefied condition, the value of each of these conditions is reduced by 1", beside its healing. Each is lowered after the healing. That sentence isn't a note once it is read whole. The resonant power after it belongs to the wayfinder, not the activation.
- **A spell cast at the creature the Strike hits** (`castOnHit` in `src/compiler/strikes.js`): "the target is also affected by a 4th-rank Vision of Death with a spell DC of 28" (Tiger's Claw), "A creature hit by an activated stone bullet is subject to the effects of a 6th-rank Petrify spell (DC 34 Fortitude)" (Stone Bullet). On the hit, the spell module casts it at that creature, with the spell's own save at the item's DC. A save of the item's own before the spell ("… unless it succeeds at a DC 25 Will save", Silencing Shot) uses castFromItem's `saveFirst`. The hit standing in for the spell's attack ("subject to a Disintegrate spell requiring a … save", Disintegration Bolt) uses its `attackDegree`. Both options are in the spell module's working tree, not its release yet; until a release has them, the card says to do it by hand.
- **Runes for the Strike that fires it** (`src/compiler/strike-damage.js`): "For the Strike with which you consume the ammunition, the weapon is a +1 striking weapon" (Weapon Shot).
- **Splash from any Strike** (`scripts/bombs.js`): a Strike whose damage has PF2e's splash-only rolls (Elemental Ammunition) gets the bomb card's splash.
- **An item's attack at its modifier or its user's, the higher** (`HIGHER_ATTACK` in `src/compiler/own-checks.js`): "Attempt a spell attack roll against the target's AC, using a modifier of +15 or your own spell attack modifier, whichever is higher" (Urn of Ashes). The card uses the user's spell attack when it's higher, else the item's.
- **One check at every creature of a kind in an area** (`src/compiler/item-actions.js`): "Make an Intimidation check to Demoralize against all creatures with the undead or unholy trait in a 30-foot emanation" (Horn of Exorcism). The card rolls once against each targeted creature's DC.
- **A condition only for creatures with a trait** (`traitGated` in `src/compiler/guards.js`): "if the creature has the ghost trait, it becomes Stupefied 1" (Lacquered Waist Drum). This isn't applied to every creature that saves. A note on the row says who it's for.
- **An Effect's opening flavor** (`src/compiler/flavor.js`): when the ability compiler can't read an Effect, it tries again without the first sentence, if that sentence has no link, number or rule word ("You strike an intimidating pose …", Suit of False Impression).
- **Amounts that grow with the owner's level** (`byOwnerLevel` in `src/compiler/item-data.js`): "The damage increases to 8d6 … if you're at least 15th level, to 9d6 … if you're at least 17th level" (The Desert, The Trumpet, The Midwife's healing). Live play uses the step for the owner's level.
- **An activation named for one of the item's PF2e toggles** (`src/compiler/option-on.js`): when the item has a toggle named like the activation (Glacier Hammer's Grow Icicles → `grow-icicles`), the toggle card switches it on. It turns off at the end of the turn ("Until the end of your turn"), or after the item's next damage roll ("The next attack … deals"). If the text says "extend or shorten", it flips instead.
  - For these jumps (2026-10-27): Kinburi's Sandals of Bounding's Light-Footed Bound turns on PF2e's own "light-footed-bound" toggle until the end of its user's turn. That toggle raises the sandals' item bonus to +2 for its High Jumps and Long Jumps. The two jumps are its user's own actions, a line of the card.
- **A damage type picked by what's loaded** (`src/compiler/type-choice.js`): "the damage type depends on the type of coins you've loaded. Copper coins deal acid damage, silver coins deal electricity damage …" (Fortune Cord). The card picks the type.
- **The owner's own numbers** (`withOwnerNumbers` in `src/compiler/item-data.js`): "@actor.abilities.str.mod" and "@actor.level" in an item's text become the owner's values in live play (Wolf Fang's Strength damage). An action's damage on a success ("If you succeed, … dealing … damage. If you critically succeeded, the damage is equal to double …", Bracers of Strength's Bear Hug) gets its own card after PF2e's roll.
- **A Requirements line set aside** (`src/compiler/permanent.js`): an activation the ability compiler can't read is tried again without its Trigger and Requirements lines ("Your last action was a successful Strike …", Catoblepas Maul).
- **"gain the X condition for a time"** (`selfConditions` in `src/compiler/rows.js`): "You stomp three times and gain the Quickened condition for 1 minute" (Propulsive Boots).
- **One check at each enemy within a distance, with a degree's extra condition** (`src/compiler/item-actions.js`): "Attempt a check to Demoralize each enemy within 30 feet of you. On a critical success, the target is also Fleeing for 1 round" (Lion's Armor). The condition is added to that degree's row of PF2e's action.
- **A worse condition for a creature already in one** (`alreadyGated` in `src/compiler/guards.js`): "on a critical failure, if the creature is already slowed, it's permanently petrified" (Stony Hag Eye) becomes the core's escalate on that row's Slowed. Live play applies Petrified to a creature that's already slowed.
- **An area in words, a save made rather than succeeded at** (`withItemData` in `src/compiler/item-data.js`): "All enemies within a 40-feet emanation must make a … save or be Dazzled for 1 minute (or Blinded … on critical failure)" (Golden Gloves). What the save's text also does and no row holds (a penalty for some creatures, a counteract on the user) goes in the notes (`saveExtras` in `src/compiler/guards.js`).
- **Healing for the owner's level in words** (`withOwnerNumbers`): "You regain Hit Points equal to twice your level" (The Rakshasa).
- **A save link with no DC after its user's spell** (`withItemData`): "Your last action was to cast a spell …, who must attempt a @Check[fortitude] save" (Maw of Hungry Shadows) is against the user's spell DC.
- **Drained, doomed and wounded last until reduced** (`lastingConditions` in `src/compiler/guards.js`): "Blinded for 1 round and Drained 1" doesn't give Drained the round, unless its own words give it a time.
- **A degree row's damage by its type** (`withItemData`): "The target takes half the fire damage", "takes the full fire damage" (Blast Lance) are read as half and full damage.
- **A degree row's own linked Effect** (`src/compiler/degree-effects.js`): "Effect: Crackling Bubble Gum (Failure)" after its row goes on that row as PF2e's Effect. This only happens when the Effect lasts as long as the row says (PF2e's equipment-effects durations, `equipmentEffectIndex`). Stonethroat Ammunition's "For 1 round" doesn't match its Effect's unlimited duration, so it stays with the GM.
- **Temporary Hit Points for another creature, or with their time after** (`tempHitPoints` in `src/compiler/rows.js`): "You touch an adjacent creature, granting them 3d8 temporary Hit Points for 1 hour" (Razmiri Wayfinder) goes to the creature picked on the card. In "You also gain 15 temporary Hit Points. You lose any remaining temporary Hit Points after 1 minute" (Bangles of Crowns), the later sentence gives the time.
- **A penalty "imposed" on its user** (`modifiersOf`): "the calming urges impose a -1 status penalty to your attack rolls" (Aeon Stone (Olivine Pendeloque)). The rest of that sentence stays a note.
- **A bonus to a listed skill and to one its user picked** (`modifiersOf`): "+1 item bonus to Diplomacy checks and checks of the skill associated with your phrase" (Words of Wisdom). Diplomacy is applied; the other skill is a note.
- **Resistance to the type its Trigger names** (`ownRow`): "Trigger You would take mental, poison, or spirit damage Effect You gain resistance 5 to the triggering damage type" (Safeguarding). The card picks one of those types.
- **What waits for something later** (`withoutLater` in `src/compiler/flavor.js`): an activation the ability compiler can't read is tried again without a "whenever …" sentence ("Additionally, for the next minute, whenever a creature touches you …", Thunder Mail). That sentence becomes a note.
- **Healing a feat of its owner's changes** (`withItemData`): "you regain 2d6 Hit Points. If you have the Battle Medicine feat, you instead regain 2d6+7 Hit Points" (Lover's Knot) uses the owner's feats in live play (`ownerOf` in `scripts/foundry.js` gives them). The census's stand-in owner has none.
- **A lowered condition beside a note** (`src/compiler/index.js`, `src/compiler/permanent.js`): when an item lowers a condition and its other words are only a note (Serene Smelling Salts' immunity), the plan is partial instead of note-only. The card lowers the condition.
- **A damage type its user writes** (`src/compiler/type-choice.js`): "The tenor of what you write … determines the damage type …: acid for a caustic inscription, cold for an aloof one, …" (Explosive Missive) becomes the save card's damage picker. Damage "of the same type" is a note.
- **Thrown to explode** (`withItemData`): an item that "can be thrown up to 30 feet, causing it to explode" has no Strike. Its area's save is read (Blindpepper Bomb). "Blinded for 1 round and then Dazzled for 1 round" becomes Dazzled for both rounds. A condition that lasts "until it removes the sickened condition" is applied with no time and a note (`untilRemoved` in `src/compiler/guards.js`).
- **A save "or be overcome" with a penalty for a time** (`withItemData`): "must succeed at a … save or be overcome with embarrassment for 1 hour, taking a –1 status penalty to Deception, Diplomacy, Intimidation, and Performance checks" (Stage Fright Missive) becomes that save's failure row.
- **A by-hand row that is one penalty** (`manualPenalties` in `src/compiler/guards.js`): the ability compiler leaves rows like "The target takes a –1 status penalty to Deception … checks for 1 hour" to the GM because its reader doesn't know skills. The item module reads that penalty with its own selectors, and also reads "As success, but a –2 penalty" (Captivating Rosebud). A critical failure with no row of its own copies the failure's.
- **Quickened and what its extra action is for** (`selfConditions` in `src/compiler/rows.js`): in "You become Quickened for 1 minute … and can use the extra action to …" (Mariner's Mien), the clause after "and can use the extra action" isn't the condition's. Quickened is applied and that clause is a note.
- **A banner's ally** (`ownRow`): "An ally within the banner's aura becomes Quickened for 1 round" (Zealous Banner, Quickened Standard) goes to the ally the presser targets.
- **After its user's own action succeeds** (`withItemData`): "On a successful Feint, the target is Dazzled for 1 round" (Glamorous Buckler) goes on the target, with a note to apply it only after the Feint succeeds.
- **A hit's condition after a flavor sentence** (`withItemData`): "On a hit, the creature becomes covered in … The creature becomes Slowed 1 for 1 round" (Growth Gun) is read as one rider.
- **An area that forces a save** (`withItemData`): "… in a 30-foot emanation, forcing hostile creatures in the area to make a … save or be Blinded for 1 round" (Eye of the Moonwarden).
- **A snare that moves its creature a square** (`withItemData`, `src/compiler/snares.js`): "save or be moved into an adjacent square (chosen by you when you craft the snare)" (Expulsion Snare) is a 5-foot push, with a note that the crafter chose the square.
- **A called creature's Strike with its damage in the next sentence** (`readOwnCheck` in `src/compiler/own-checks.js`): a released creature's attack modifier, with its damage in the next sentence ("On a successful Strike it deals …"), is read as one sentence (third-party bottled creatures).
- **A save "or become" with its worse result after it** (`withItemData`): a save to avoid a condition for a time, with a worse value for a critical failure at the end of the sentence, becomes "save or become X (Y on a critical failure)".
- **A flight's line that saves or takes damage** (`withItemData`): creatures along a released creature's flight that save or take damage are a line as long as the flight. Another damage "instead" for some creatures is a note (`saveExtras`).
- **One check at the target and the enemies around it** (`src/compiler/item-actions.js`): "attempt to Demoralize the target as well as each enemy within 30 feet of the target" (Shrieking Skull). The skill-action form also reads "… and attempting an Intimidation check to Demoralize that creature" (Wrestler's Armbands).
- **A banner's damage at each hurt enemy in its aura** (`withItemData`, `whoTargeted` in `src/compiler/guards.js`): "The magical banner deals 1d4 persistent bleed damage to any enemy within the banner's aura that has been dealt damage since the end of your last turn" (Banner of Piercing Shards). The damage goes to each creature targeted, and which ones count is a note.
- **A moment's resistance with its type first** (`readied-bonus.js`): "gaining fire resistance 20 against the triggering effect" (Sparkshade Parasol). A parenthesis is its own sentence, so a later "For the next 1 minute, …, letting you use …" about another activation isn't the resistance's time.
- **A companion's Speed** (`speedsOf` in `src/compiler/rows.js`): "granting your companion a swim Speed of 30 feet for 10 minutes" goes to the companion the presser targets (Waverider Barding's first way since 2026-10-27, `creature-readings.js`).
- **A mount's damage and condition** (`withItemData`, `directDamageOver` in `src/compiler/direct-damage.js`): "Your mount takes 2d6 bleed and gains the quickened condition for 1 minute or until the persistent bleed damage ends" (Heedless Spurs) goes to the targeted mount. The item's outright reading is used over the ability compiler's when it reads more (the quickened beside the bleed), and the bomb rider reader now knows "is quickened for …".
- **Conditions a row gives later** (`delayedConditions` in `src/compiler/guards.js`): "At the end of their next turn, they fall Prone and fall Unconscious for 1 minute" (Nap Gas Dispenser) isn't applied at once. Those conditions come off the row (and off "As failure, but …"), and a note on the creature says when they come. A by-hand penalty beside a condition the row already holds ("takes a –2 status penalty to Perception checks and is Slowed 1 for 1 round") is read too.
- **A condition a row names only under "if", "when" or "after"** (`conditionalOnly` in `src/compiler/guards.js`): Heart of Sarnax's "When it recovers from being stunned, it is Slowed 2 for 1 minute" and Hex Blaster's "If the target falls Unconscious from damage …" aren't applied with the rest of the row. They're notes for the GM. ("Until …, the creature is also Confused" is Confused now.)
- **"As success, but …" rows keep the success's conditions** (`asRowCopies`): The Waxworks' failure is Confused as its success is. Wintershot's copied Dazzled takes the row's own "the light affects the creature for 1 minute", and a critical failure "As failure" gets what the failure got. "The duration of the blinded condition is 1 minute" (Lamentation of the Faithless) gives Blinded that time.
- **A condition's time in its own sentence** (`sentenceTimes`): "For the next minute, it is Stupefied 1 …" (Liar's Bangle) and "Stupefied 4 for 1 day" get those times, not forever. An "As failure, but Clumsy 2" row (Floppy Rag Doll) keeps its failure's time for the condition it names again.
- **One of several runes lent, picked on the card** (`src/compiler/rune-loans.js`, `src/live/card.js`): "granting the weapon the effects of either a Decaying weapon rune, Grievous weapon rune, or Wounding weapon rune for 1 minute" (Boughshatter); also a list, Glaive of the Artist's "the effects of your choice of the corrosive, flaming, frost, shocking, or thundering property runes for 1 round" (2026-10-26). plan.item.rune.choices lists them, the card's "Rune" picker chooses one (`runeChoice`), and the first is the default.
- **One action at every creature in a burst, each its own roll** (`src/compiler/item-actions.js`, `scripts/item-actions.js`): "attempt a ranged Trip against all creatures in a 5-foot burst … Roll a separate Athletics check for each target" (Windlass Bola). action.separate makes PF2e's action roll once per targeted creature, each with its own row.
- **A condition its creature can't lower below a value** (`conditionFloors` in `src/compiler/guards.js`, and Tallow Bombs' rider in `src/compiler/bombs.js`): "can't reduce its frightened value below 1 for 1 round" (Timpani of Panic, and its "As failure" row), "… until it spends an action, which has the concentrate trait" (Terrifying Ammunition), "for the duration of the penalty" (Gleaming Cuirass), "can't reduce its sickened condition until the end of its next turn" (Putrescent Glob) and "while the persistent fire damage lasts" (Tallow Bombs). Each becomes the core's conditionFloor: { slug, value, lifetime fixed | until-lifted | while-persistent }, which the ability module plays as a floor Effect (ability d79b1ab and later).
- **A bonus after "and gain", and "While this effect is active"** (`readied-bonus.js`, `modifiersOf`): "you draw strength from your adoring crowd and gain a +1 status bonus to the triggering check" (Champion's Laurels, the trigger's Acrobatics or Athletics), and "While this effect is active, you gain a +2 status bonus to Deception checks to feint" (Look-Me-Not).
- **"The pads give you a climb Speed …"** (`speedsOf`): Gecko Pads' climb Speed for its time; "The climb Speed lasts for 10 minutes" restates that time and isn't a note.
- **Its holder's resistance beside what it does to others** (`withItemData`): "For the next minute, the creature holding the gadget gains resistance 2 to spirit damage, while all creatures … gains weakness 2" (Maelstromic Destabilizer). The holder's resistance is applied; the others' weakness is a note.
- **A bonus to the triggering save and the rest against the same affliction** (`withItemData`): "+2 status bonus on the triggering Fortitude save and on all Fortitude saves against the same disease for the next minute" (Fulu of the Stoic Ox, Fulu of the Drunken Monkey) is a minute's bonus to Fortitude saves against diseases (poisons), the triggering one among them.
- **An aura that acts at a turn's start or end in it** (`src/compiler/aura-zones.js`): "any creature that ends its turn adjacent to you takes 3d6 cold damage (… basic save)" (Arctic Worm Chitin Shield), "Any creature that begins its turn within 20 feet of you must succeed at a … save or become Fascinated" (Alluring Lantern), and a thrown "5-foot radius. Any creature that starts its turn in the area or moves into the area takes …" (Sandstorm Top). Each becomes the ability module's zone: an emanation attached to its user's token (or a burst placed where it's thrown), the sentence's save at "start" or "end" (and "enter"), for its time. What it gives its user beside the aura is a note.
- **A bonus for this turn** (`timeIn`, `modifiersOf`): "until the end of your turn", "during those Stride actions", "for this movement" end with the turn (PF2e's 0 rounds), "until the end of your next turn" with the next one: Quick Runner's Shirt's "You Stride twice and gain a +10-foot item bonus to your Speed during those Stride actions", Cape of the Open Sky's "+15-foot status bonus to your Speed and fly Speed for this movement" (both Speeds). Flavor's "with greater speed" isn't a Speed.
- **A bonus to some actions' checks** (`modifiersOf`): "grants you a +2 item bonus to attempts to Decipher Writing, Disable a Device, Identify Magic, Pick a Lock, Seek, or Sense Motive" (Eye of the Wise: PF2e's check selector, for those actions), "a +3 item bonus on Perception checks you make to use Sense Motive against that creature" (Spectacles of Inquiry, White Cleome's Eye; the one creature a note). A bonus only "while" something else lasts stays a note (Exploration Lens's "while Investigating"); an aeon stone's resonant power isn't the activation's.
- **Healing after another thing you do** (`textHealing`): "you avoid being knocked out and regain 2d8+5 Hit Points" (Guardian Rose: the healing; avoiding unconsciousness stays a note).
- **A lock's bonus for anyone picking it** (`withItemData`): "Anyone attempting to Pick the Lock within the next minute gains a +1 status bonus to the Thievery check" (Lockpicker's Oil): its user's Thievery bonus to Pick a Lock for that minute; anyone else's a note. Its "the first time during that minute that anyone gets a critical failure on the Thievery check, they get a failure instead" is its user's own Effect (`src/compiler/first-degree.js`, plan.item.firstDegree): PF2e's AdjustDegreeOfSuccess on Thievery checks to Pick a Lock, which the first critical failure it changes removes (`scripts/first-degree.js`).
- **A Deafened condition "to all other noise"** (`withItemData`): Mordant Mask's Pounding Surf, Deafened until the end of its user's next turn.
- **A Strike without its range penalty or a trait's penalty, or with flat splash damage** (`src/compiler/strike-damage.js`, `src/live/strike-card.js`): "You take no range increment penalty on your attack, as long as the attack is against a target within the affixed weapon's first two range increments" (Sniper's Bead: PF2e's `ignore-range-penalty:2` roll option on the readied Strike; Greater four, Major six), "ignoring the penalty for making a Strike within your second or third range increment" and "deals 10 piercing splash damage for this Strike" (Jistkan War Crossbow), "ignore the circumstance penalty of the attached weapon's kickback weapon trait" (Alchemist's Damper: PF2e's kickback modifier suppressed). "You attack with the affixed firearm" is its ranged Strike. A talisman's physical description ("This plain wooden bead dangles …") isn't a note.
- **A change to its user's weapons for a while** (`weaponAdjustOf`, `src/live/card.js` `weaponEffectSource`): "You increase the range increment of the weapon to which the scope is attached by 20 feet until the beginning of your next turn" (Magnifying Scope: an Effect with PF2e's AdjustStrike adding to its ranged weapons' range increment), "until the end of the current turn, the affixed weapon gains the thrown 20 feet weapon trait" (Dragonbone Arrowhead: its melee weapons gain thrown-20; the weapon flying back is a note). Live-checked: PF2e's range increment rises by 20, the shortsword gains thrown-20.
- **Other damage for one kind of creature mid-sentence** (`withItemData`, `saveExtras`): "Creatures in the area take 6d8 spirit damage, or 6d12 spirit damage if they're a dragon (… basic save)" (Lance of Sun's Radiance): the 6d8 and its save are played, and "A dragon takes 6d12 spirit damage instead" is a by-hand note.
- **A spell on a creature nearby, counteracted** (`readCounteract`, the counteract card): "attempt to counteract an active divine or occult spell within 30 feet, with a +7 counteract modifier and a counteract rank of 2" (Etheric and Material Essence Disruptors, all four grades each). The card offers every creature within that distance, foe or friend, and lists that creature's spell Effects (PF2e's "Spell Effect: …"). It rolls the counteract check against the DC and rank shown and removes what it beats. The spell's tradition is a note, for the GM to tick only a fitting one.
- **Back from dying without raising Wounded** (`keepsWounded`, `textHealing`): "You lose the dying condition and regain 1 Hit Point. Your Wounded value does not increase." (Magical Medal (Phoenix's Fire)) and "An ally within the banner's aura with the Dying condition regains 30 Hit Points, does not increase their Wounded condition" (Banner of the Rising Star, at the ally targeted). The healing is the item card's, and the core's recoverFromDying runs with keepWounded, so Dying ends and Wounded stays.
  - Guardian Rose's "When you activate a guardian rose, you avoid being knocked out and regain 2d8+5 Hit Points" (its trigger: you would be reduced to 0 Hit Points) reads the same way. The Dying and Unconscious PF2e gave its user at 0 Hit Points end on the healing, and no Wounded is added for them, because its user was never knocked out. Text that raises Wounded itself ("your Wounded condition increases by 1", False Death Vial) isn't read this way.
  - Left at 1 Hit Point instead of 0 (item.dropToOne): The Survivor's "Instead of being reduced to 0 Hit Points, you drop to 1 Hit Point instead and immediately restore an additional 4d8+16 Hit Points" and Immortal Bastion's "You drop to 1 Hit Point instead of being reduced to 0 HP or dying, and you gain 100 temporary Hit Points …". Before the healing or temporary Hit Points, the card sets its user's Hit Points to 1 and ends the Dying and Unconscious PF2e gave it, with no Wounded for them. The Survivor's larger healing at 15th, 17th and 19th level is its owner's step (`byOwnerLevel`, which reads "The amount of Hit Point restored increases to …" too). With no owner level known, the steps are a note.
- **A damage link and its save with no "damage" word** (`withItemData`): "Each creature in the burst takes 10d8 piercing with a … Reflex save" (Black Hole Armor) is read as a basic save.
- **A missile that hits on its own** (`withItemData`): "The shuriken hits automatically and deals 1d4+1 force damage, or 2d4+2 force damage if the target is your designated offender" (Cape of Justice): damage outright at the creature targeted, the other case a note.
- **Healing in other words, and what goes with it** (`textHealing`): "You recover 3d10+8 Hit Points" (Swarmeater's Clasp); "… and deal the same amount of bludgeoning damage to the swarm" stays on the note.
- **A consumable used in one of several ways** (`src/compiler/ways.js`, `src/live/ways-card.js`, `scripts/ways.js`): each Activate block is compiled as a permanent item's activation. After its Use, the ways card lets its user pick a way; "Use it" posts that way's own card, and the ways card stays open for the next. This covers Toadskin Salve's two reactions (its mucus's persistent poison at the creature hit, or resistance against the triggering attack) and Thrice-Fried Mudwings' meal (a fly Speed) and its wings' reaction (resistance 15).
- **A moment's bonus for another creature** (`readied-bonus.js` bonus.recipient "target", the fortune card): "The target gains a +2 status bonus to AC against the triggering attack" (Resonant Guitar's Chord of Protection), "grants the target resistance 10 to the triggering damage" (Hallajin Key). Apply gives the Effect to the creature the presser targets, within the distance its trigger names. Without a target, Apply is refused. The barrier's damage to an attacker who still hits is rolled and applied to that attacker (bonus.onAttack.hit).
- **Extra damage on its user's Strikes for a while** (`strikeDamageOf`, `src/live/card.js` `strikeDamageEffectSource`): "your melee Strikes deal 1d4 additional precision damage to Off-Guard creatures" (Martial Mayhem Mutagen). The Effect is PF2e's DamageDice on Strike damage (a FlatModifier for the grades' flat 1), with `item:melee` and `target:condition:off-guard` as its predicate, for the mutagen's 10 minutes. Its other words stay a note.
- **A grapple at the item's own modifier, given in a later sentence** (`withItemData`): "The arms share your multiple attack penalty and attempt to Grapple a random enemy within 15 feet of you. … The Athletics modifier is +12" (Octopus Potion: +12, +17, +22). It's the own-check card's Grapple at that modifier against the targeted creature's Fortitude DC, with PF2e's Grapple rows. The random enemy, the d4 added effects and the later actions are notes. Its table roll's "#Added Effect" label and the table's "Added Effect" header no longer read as the Effect's start.
- **An attack at its user's spell or ranged attack modifier, their pick** (`own-checks.js`, `scripts/poisons.js`): "Make a spell or ranged attack roll (your choice) to affect the target. On a hit, the target takes 10d8 fire damage and is Drained 2." (Clockwork Helm). The card offers its user's spell attack and their best ranged Strike, as a spellgun's offers spell and firearm. The damage is doubled on a critical hit and the condition comes on any hit.
- **A ranged attack roll for a beam's damage** (`own-checks.js`): a third-party Serpentine Mirror's Burning Beam. The card picks its user's best ranged Strike against AC, and a hit deals both its fire and its persistent fire (doubled on a critical hit).
- **An item thrown as a Strike that PF2e can't roll** (`thrown.js`, `scripts/poisons.js`): "When you Strike a creature with a sack of rotten fruit, … the creature takes 1d4 bludgeoning damage and must attempt a DC 15 Fortitude save. On a failure, the creature is Sickened 1" (Sack of Rotten Fruit), "This is a simple thrown weapon with a range of 5 feet … If it hits, it deals 2d6 spirit damage" (Lady's Chalice), "using your attack bonus for a ranged simple weapon. On a hit, … becomes Off-Guard …, and on a critical hit, it's also Immobilized" (Halcyon Heart), Life Salt, Oil and Rod of Razors. The card's attack is its user's simple thrown weapon attack: their Dexterity modifier and simple weapon proficiency, or their best ranged Strike when they have no proficiency to read. A hit gives the damage (doubled on a critical hit) and conditions. A save the hit calls for is the same card's second step, at the creature hit. Splash, a flat check to ignite, and who it works on are the card's notes.
  - Its weapon traits are read. Deadly (Rod of Razors' d10) is one die more on a critical hit, not doubled: a component of its own on that row only (componentMultipliers 1). Volley 30 feet is –2 at a target within 30 feet. Its range is a range increment, as a weapon's is: –2 for each increment past the first, and a target past six increments is refused. Apply measures the distance from its user to the creature targeted, takes the penalty off the attack and says so.
  - A precious material, Rod of Razors' "The flechette is an adamantine weapon", goes in the damage's flavor ("3d8[piercing,adamantine]"), which PF2e and the core read for a weakness or resistance to it. A plan with nothing left as a note is no longer partial.
- **A creature the item becomes** (`conjured.js`, `scripts/zones.js`): "This simple onyx statue transforms into a Guard Dog" (Wondrous Figurine (Onyx Dog)), "it becomes a pair of adult Lions" (Golden Lions), "this fragment becomes a Giant Mosquito when activated … for up to 10 minutes" (Fossil Fragments), Beetle Gel, Serpent Oil, Pyrite Rat, Winder's Ring and Marvelous Miniature (Horse), each linking PF2e's stat block. The ability module's conjured tokens appear beside the item's user, on their side (`api.live.startConjure`, ability module 0.8.4 or later; before that, a GM note). They carry the minion trait when the text gives it (a Fossil Fragment's construct and earth too), go at 0 Hit Points and end with their time in a fight. A time in hours lasts until the GM ends it. The Marble Elephant and Brontosaurus last 1d4 rounds once they fight. The Ruby Hippopotamus takes its own turns. Figurines written without the family's Activate block (Bismuth Leopards, Candy Constrictor, Obsidian Steed, Marble Elephant, Ruby Hippopotamus, Stuffed Fox) get it, 2 actions. What the creature can or can't do are the card's notes.
- **A creature whose numbers the item gives** (`conjured.js` statedCreaturePlan): Alchemists+'s Bottle-O-Ooze grades (an ooze of the grade's size that stays put for its minute) and Canopic Canisters (a zombie for a few rounds). Each is a conjured token with the text's AC and Hit Points, and saves at the item's level when the text says so. Its grab save, its Strike at the start of its user's turns and its immunities are notes for the GM. A module's short link to a bestiary creature counts as its stat block. A Fossil Fragment that names its creature without a link (Petrified Wood's awakened tree) is found by that name in the compendiums. Tian Xia+'s Ant Pheromones is an Army Ant Swarm that takes its own turns.
- **Creatures read sentence by sentence** (`src/compiler/creature-readings.js`, 2026-10-27): each reading names its item (or its grades) and has a pattern for every sentence of the effect, so none is dropped. A conjured token as the text has it:
  - Dust of Corpse Animation and Oil of Dynamism, each grade's level the most. Its user first picks a skeleton or zombie, or a mindless construct, from PF2e's rulebook bestiaries (`plan.pick`, `scripts/creature-pick.js` with the ability module's own filter). The pick is made on the using client, as the consumable may be used up by then. The token is a minion for its minute.
  - A Galvasphere's galvaheart zombie: the ways card picks a Medium or Small corpse. It is PF2e's Zombie Shambler with the construct trait in place of undead, no unholy trait, immune to vitality and without the vitality weakness, at the corpse's size.
  - Kotodama Bells' awakened object, with the text's AC 5, 5 Hit Points and Speed 10. An Effect with PF2e's AdjustDegreeOfSuccess makes it fail every save.
  - Cordelia's Construct Keys' Animated Broom with its Strike items gone, for 1 hour (8 hours for the greater key).
  - What the item changes on the made creature is `src/live/conjured.js` conjuredChanges: creature.removeTraits, own.dropWeaknesses, own.noStrikes, and a size for a linked creature, beside replaceType, immunities and own.rules.
- **A companion's senses while Sustained** (`creature-readings.js`): Collar of Empathy's Empathic Link is a note Effect on its user for 10 minutes, PF2e's most for a Sustain with no time of its own. Its user removes it when they stop Sustaining.
- **What a companion's item does to it** (`creature-readings.js`): for the creatures its presser targets (item.recipient "targets"), as the module's other companion items.
  - Hosteling Statuette's merge is a note Effect with PF2e's rules: a GrantItem of Petrified (inMemoryOnly) and CreatureSize Tiny, with no time. Its other activation ends it: `plan.item.endsOwn { label }` (`src/live/ends-own.js`, `scripts/live.js` applyToRecipient) deletes the creature's Effects named "<item>: <label>", as the ability module names a note Effect.
  - Waverider Barding is the ways card: the item card's swim Speed of 30 feet, or a note Effect of +10 feet item to the swim Speed it already has, for 10 minutes.
  - Swarmform Collar's swarm is a note Effect for its minute: CreatureSize Large, the swarm trait, and PF2e's Spider Swarm's resistances and weaknesses (`SPIDER_SWARM`, held to the stat block by the test). Its swarming attack is `plan.again`, the later save card for the companion, kept open for that minute and pressed at each use. Its basic Reflex DC 23 takes its damage type from the card's picker (bludgeoning, piercing or slashing, as its unarmed attacks deal).
  - Whistle of Calling's 10-minute attunement is its card's line; its other activation stays the teleport card.
- **A tattoo with a summon spell's parameters** (`creature-readings.js`): Bound Guardian is PF2e's Tattoo Guardian, and Fauna Guardian is the animal its user picks (a common animal of level 4 or lower, `plan.pick`). Each is a conjured token with the summoned and minion traits for a minute at most, as a Sustained summon. At 0 Hit Points it is gone, a tattoo again.
- **A cast whose changes are its card's lines** (`creature-readings.js`, a reading with `keep`): it goes over the cast read before and keeps it, its notes reworded and no longer partial.
  - Busine of Divine Reinforcements' Summon Celestial: no alignment limits it, and its celestial fights only evil creatures unless its user's deity favours it (a behaviour beside the cast).
  - Sack of Hydra's Teeth's Sow a Tooth: Phantasmal Minion as a visible skeleton.
  - Anchor of Aquatic Exploration's Cozy Cabin: a sunken ship full of air.
- **Reef Heart, Guardian Shield and Bleachguard Doll** are one-offs: the coral's underwater time, the guardian's shield for an ally, and the doll's own spells and its animated self.
- **What a conjured creature's entry says of its calls** (`conjured.js`, 2026-10-27):
  - How often it's called is the activation's frequency for the activations card: "The lions can be called on only once per day", "The onyx dog can be activated once per week", "It can be called upon no more than four times per month". `src/live/activations-card.js` counts a week and a month (30 days) too.
  - A figurine's "If the figurine is slain while in animal form, … cannot be activated again until 1 week has passed" is `plan.slainWait`. When a creature the ability module conjured from the item drops to 0 Hit Points, `scripts/slain-wait.js` sets the item's recharge for that week. Golden Lions' "If either of the lions is slain, that lion cannot be summoned again" is slainWait.each: the slain lion is kept on the item with its time, and one fewer comes while it waits.
  - The Onyx Dog's scent is words on its own Effect (`own.text`). The Brontosaurus's 24 hours out of combat and its structure are its card's lines, and its 1d4 rounds once it fights are its time.
  - The Amber Mosquito's changes to its own abilities are a one-off. `decided.js` counts a conjure plan's notes as shown, since the conjure card shows them.
- **Serpent Oil (Moderate) and Palanquin of Night** (`creature-readings.js`): the ways card's Sea Snake or Python for a Small or Medium object; the palanquin's four Unseen Servant bearers as conjured tokens, its travel and shelter its card's lines.
- **The rest of the creatures group read sentence by sentence** (`creature-readings.js`, 2026-10-27):
  - Bargainer's Instrument's +2 item bonus to Performance checks is a note Effect of PF2e's rule until the contest ends. The contest with the talent its Binding Circle conjures (the GM's) is its card's lines, out of combat.
  - Mourner's Dawnlight Fulu's search within 500 feet is whispered to the GM and its user.
  - Cooperative Waffles (both grades) is a 24-hour note Effect on each of the two who shared them, the creatures its presser targets. PF2e's Follow the Expert doesn't say whom it follows, so its bonus 1 higher (and the greater's Aid) is words.
  - Harrow Spellcards' 1d6 is the table card. Each row is the fortune card's roll twice for its next check of that kind (any Recall Knowledge: a skill check with action:recall-knowledge), for a day, to the next daily preparations. A drawn harrow card's suit picks the row.
  - Doctrine of Blissful Eternity is the ways card, one way per slot rank. Each heals the targeted undead minion 1d8 per rank and spends a slot of that rank (`spend.spellSlot`, as `slot-cost.js` does).
- **Weapon Shot's ghostly weapon** (every grade) is a one-off. Its first activation stays the Strike card with the shot's runes.
- **Sandalwood Fan's Cloud of Leaves** (`creature-readings.js`): the allies' card first, lesser cover as +1 circumstance to AC for a round for the creatures its presser targets (allyArea). Then `plan.again` posts the enemies' card: Revealing Light's Dazzled for a minute, and its rule on invisibility and concealment as a note Effect.
- **Ouroboros Buckles' regeneration**: PF2e's own Effect: Ouroboros Buckles (its FastHealing regeneration, ended by fire) as the row's native Effect. A note Effect for the minute says when to apply or remove PF2e's swarm Effect, at each regeneration. Swarming Bites (4d4 piercing and 2d6 poison, basic Reflex DC 41) is `plan.again`'s later card, kept open for the minute.
- **Bloodstone Dolls** (`creature-readings.js` over the counteract plan): the counteract card lists the creatures within 90 feet so its user can pick the summoned creature. A success doesn't end the spell. Its `counteract.afterFree` is a note Effect on that creature: its user dictates its actions this turn. With `against: "user"` and a penalty, the Effect has PF2e's TokenMark of the user's token and the –2 status to attack rolls only at it (`src/live/counteract-card.js` afterFreeRules, `scripts/counteract.js`).
- **Alicorn Lance, Viper Arrow and Cloning Potion** are one-offs: a charge with the jousting trait's effects, an arrow that becomes a Viper delivering its venom, and a clone sharing its drinker's Hit Points.
- **An unarmed attack it grants** (`rows.js` attacksOf, `src/live/card.js`): "Your hand becomes a tiger's claw, granting you an agile claw unarmed attack that inflicts 1d6 slashing damage, and you Strike with the claw. Then the claw turns back to normal" (Ring of the Tiger, until the end of the turn), and Tentacle Potion (Greater)'s 1d6 bludgeoning sweep limb for its 24 hours. Its user gets an Effect with PF2e's Strike rule, as PF2e's own Bestial Mutagen gives its claw, and PF2e lists the attack among their Strikes. Tooth and Claw Tattoo's "unarmed attack matching the tattoo … the same damage as your best unarmed attack" works the same way. The ways card picks the tattoo's animal (a cat's claw, a bull's horn, and so on), and the attack copies the die and traits of its user's best unarmed attack.
- **A weapon gaining a trait for a while** (`rows.js` weaponAdjustOf): "The chain of command gains the nonlethal trait for 1 minute" (Chain of Command). This is PF2e's AdjustStrike on that weapon alone, like "the weapon loses the nonlethal trait". A trait an attack gains ("the triggering attack gains the nonlethal trait", Possum's Play) isn't the weapon's.
- **A shield changing form until it's activated again** (`permanent.js` shieldFormOf): Sapling Shield's "The buckler becomes a tower shield, gaining the corresponding AC bonus, Speed penalty, and ability to Take Cover". Its user gets an Effect with PF2e's ItemAlteration on that shield, a tower shield's +2 AC and –5-foot Speed penalty. Its Hardness and Bulk are 2 higher, also through ItemAlteration. Activating it again removes the Effect, and the shield is a buckler again. Its Take Cover as a tower shield is PF2e's own: the Take Cover action's Effect: Cover, which its user sets to greater cover with the shield raised. The card says so in its line, not as a note.
- **A save's degrees written as sentences** (`save-or.js` degreeSentencesPlan): "The target must attempt a DC 36 Reflex save. On a failure, the limb holding their weapon is also pulled into the gearblade, and the creature takes 3d12 bludgeoning damage. On a critical failure, they take 6d12 bludgeoning damage and the limb becomes unusable …" (Gearblade). This becomes the save card at the creature targeted, each degree with its own damage. The rest of each degree is a line for the GM.
- **A skill check against the creature that triggered it, written without its link** (`item-data.js`): "Attempt a Stealth check against the triggering creature's Perception DC. If you roll a success, you're Hidden from that creature …" (Shadowmist Cape, Graymist), and Storyteller's Opus's Deception. The card rolls its user's skill against that creature's DC. A condition the check gives its user ("you're Hidden") is the GM's line on the card, not the other creature's condition.
- **Damage as many as the weapon's damage dice** (`item-data.js`): "dealing slashing damage equal to the scizore of the crab's number of damage dice" (Scizore of the Crab). The damage is the weapon's own die count plus its striking rune, at the creature it holds.
- **Fast healing for a while** (`rows.js` fastHealingOf): an Effect with PF2e's FastHealing rule, which heals at the start of each turn. Infernal Health's "For 6 rounds, at the start of your turn each round, you recover 25 Hit Points unless you took spirit damage since the start of your previous turn" was this until 2026-10-27, with the "unless" as a note. It's now its own healing (`turn-heals.js`, ruleEffect turnHeal { at: "start", unless: "spirit" }): scripts/turn-heal.js heals at each turn's start (pf2e.startTurn) for 6 rounds, to the end of the last turn. A spirit damage (the core's damage listener) marks the Effect, so the next start heals none, and the start after heals again.
- **A dragon's breath type** (`type-choice.js`): "deals 25d6 damage (basic Reflex save) … The breath's damage type matches the Breath Weapon of the dragon type associated with the orb" (Orb of Dragonkind). The card picks the type from the dragons' breath types. The other area shape is a note.
- **A named state that's a penalty** (`item-data.js`): "must succeed at a DC 20 Will save or be pacified. A pacified creature takes a –2 penalty to attack rolls on any attacks that aren't nonlethal for 1 minute" (Pacifying). The save's failure is that penalty, and the nonlethal exception is a note.
- **More wordings read through `item-data.js`:**
  - Mirror Robe's "Creates a Diversion for you with a +9 Deception modifier": the robe's own check against the target's Perception DC.
  - An intelligent item's maneuver with no number (2026-10-26): Anima Robe (Heroic)'s "attempts to Feint a creature you're adjacent to" is rolled at its stat block's Deception +35 (`src/compiler/item-data.js`). Each action uses its own skill: Deception for Feint and Create a Diversion, Intimidation for Demoralize, Athletics for the maneuvers.
  - Aerekostes's "their wielder rolls twice and takes the better result" on its next attack: its user's roll twice.
  - Smoldering Armor's "The armor is sheathed in a Fire Shield": the spell's Effect on its wearer.
  - Deravon's Helm's "save to resist being compelled to drop Prone": the save or Prone, with its timing a note.
  - Anointed Waterskin's "granting you the effects of Bless": PF2e's Spell Effect.
  - Limestone Shield's "granting you standard cover from that effect": +2 circumstance to AC and Reflex saves against that effect, the moment's bonus.
  - Jug of Fond Remembrance's "Recall Knowledge … with a +2 circumstance bonus": PF2e's Recall Knowledge with that bonus, its degree and Stupefied the card's words.
  - Crown of the Companion's "as if you cast Share Life targeting the ally": the spell module's cast ("as if you cast" a cast verb now, not a ritual; a damage roll's formula in notes as its words).
  - Shadowpiercer's temporary Hit Points "equal to four times the effect's counteract rank" and Reaper's Grasp's "three times the level of the creature whose soul you chose": the item card's typed amount.
  - Hagbane Biscuit's bonus with three scopes: the saves against hags (PF2e's origin trait, hags now among the creature words); its Perception and Recall Knowledge parts by hand.
  - Heartening Missive (Bull, Rabbit, Turtle)'s "The recipient gains a +1 item bonus to Fortitude saves for the next 24 hours": that bonus on the recipient the presser targets.
- **Smoke sprayed into a square** (`covered-square.js` sprayedSmokePlan): Beekeeper's Smoker's adjacent square, Concealed inside. It's the ability module's placed zone, and its 1d4 rounds are the GM's.
- **A drifting cloud of sticky bubbles** (`covered-square.js` bubbleCloudPlan): Clinging Bubbles. Its cloud is placed for its minute, and a creature entering it gets the grade's circumstance penalty to its Speed. The penalty stays until the creature pops the bubbles, when the GM removes its Effect. The cloud's drift and its own AC and Hit Points are notes.
- **A snare that attacks** (`snares.js`): "a … wheel of spinning blades launches at it, making a Strike with an attack modifier of +35 and dealing 8d8 slashing damage" (Flying Blade Wheel Snare). The first creature into its square gets the ability module's card with the snare's +35 against its AC, and the damage on a hit (doubled on a critical hit). Its later Strikes are notes.
- **A counteract aimed at what isn't on a creature** (`counteract.js`, `scripts/counteract.js`): the triggering spell (Antimagic, Aeon Stone (Pale Lavender Ellipsoid), Countering Charms, Ring of Counterspells), a spell (Reflecting Shield, Devil's Trap), a magical fire (Frost Brand, Icicle), magical darkness (Brilliant, Primordial Flame, Daylight Vapor, Twilight Lanterns' darkness effect), the triggering illusion (Draconal Mask), an effect restoring the slain to life (Crimson Tome) or a magical effect or item (Cloth of Nullification). The counteract card goes straight to its one row, where the GM gives that DC and rank, and the item's own rank too when it's a stored spell's. The card rolls the item's counteract check, applies PF2e's counteract rule and reports the result. The GM ends what it counteracts. An activation can also have a counteract card beside its other reading, as consumables do: Golden Gloves' halo gives its enemies' save card, then a counteract card for one affliction its user suffers.
  - A flat check after its use (2026-10-27, counteract.burnout): Aeon Stone (Pale Lavender Ellipsoid) rolls a flat check with DC 5 after each activation, and failing it turns the stone dull gray for good. After the card applies, scripts/counteract.js rolls the flat check in the chat. On a failure it replaces the stone on its owner with PF2e's Aeon Stone (Consumed), the remaster's dull gray stone, keeping it worn and invested.
- **Ammunition made against one type of creature** (`bane.js`): Bane Ammunition's capsule "tailored to a particular type of creature". The ways card picks the type it was made for, and that way readies the bow's next Strike with that arrow. Its 2d6 persistent poison is PF2e's DamageDice with a target-trait predicate, so it applies only against a creature of that type. Warding Element Draught works the same way. The ways card picks the element its ingredient was distilled for, and its drinker gets the +1 item bonus to AC and saves against effects with that trait for 10 minutes.
- **A splash weapon that harms only some creatures** (`pf2e-rules.js` splashWeaponPlan, `scripts/bombs.js`): Holy Water's "can damage only creatures with the unholy trait". PF2e's Strike rolls its damage and splash. The splash card ticks only the creatures beside the target that have the trait, and says when the target hasn't it (its damage from PF2e's message is then the GM's to leave unapplied). Unholy Water works the same way with the holy trait.
- **A resistance by a recalled creature's trait** (`bane.js` victoryWaysPlan): Victory Plate's list of traits, each with its damage type. The ways card picks one: mental, holy for good, poison, any one energy type for a dragon or elemental, unholy for evil, void, or a higher precision resistance for an ooze. The item card then gives that resistance for 1 minute.
- **Effects its user picks one of, in a sentence** (`permanent.js` chosenEffectsPlan): Heartripper Blade's "Choose one of the following effects: restore 2d8 Hit Points; reduce the value of your Clumsy, Enfeebled, or Stupefied conditions by 1; or reduce the stage of one toxin or disease …". Each option is compiled alone as its own activation. The ways card offers the ones that play (the healing; the lowered condition, whose item card picks which one). The affliction's stage is a note for the GM.
- **Temporary Hit Points or healing from an amount its trigger gave** (`rows.js` levelTempOf, `from: "amount"`; the item card's Amount field): Bloodknuckles' "temporary Hit Points equal to the value of the triggering bleed damage, up to a maximum of 10", Reaper's Toll's and Vampiric Scythe's half the damage dealt, Blood-Drinker Blade's half the bleed, Aeon Stone (Azure Briolette)'s twice the spell's rank until its next turn, and Assassin's Skin's and Grisly Scythe's Hit Points regained. The presser types the amount, and the card applies its multiple (rounded down, capped) with the ability module's temporary Hit Points or healing.
- **Conditions that end on its user** (`reduce.js` END_OWN, the item card's second pass): Spleen Bloodstone of Arazni's "The Confused, Controlled, Grabbed, Immobilized, Paralyzed, Petrified, and Restrained conditions, … immediately end". Each one is removed. A condition another was overriding (Grabbed under Restrained) is removed once it shows, and one another gave (Immobilized from Restrained) is checked again at the end. Its Speed penalties and its level exception are a note.
- **A portion rubbed in** (`withItemData`): Tallowheart Mass's "If you rub a portion between your hands, … you gain the Quickened condition for 1 minute" is its user's Quickened for that minute. The extra action's limit is a note.
- **A short teleport for its user** (`teleport.js`, the teleport card, `scripts/teleport.js`): "You teleport to an unoccupied space within 200 feet that you can see" (Mad Mammoth's Juke), "within 10 feet of the target" (Golden Spur), "Teleport up to double your Speed" (Mercurial Mantle, Purloining Cloak), "a space adjacent to the target of your Strike" (Gloaming Shard), Master Magus Ring's "within a range equal to your Speed", and Dweomerveil, Shadow's Heart and Sky-Piercing Bow. Quartz-Coil Rail Transport's "transport yourself … to an unoccupied space up to 40 feet away" and Dimension Shot's "teleport to an unoccupied space adjacent to that creature" (the creature hit, targeted) read the same way. Pick has the presser click the space on their own canvas (right-click or Escape cancels). The active GM's client checks it's on the map, unoccupied and in range, measured from the user or from the creature targeted, then moves the token there at once (Foundry's teleport, no animation). Other sentences (a Strike before or after it, a longer range on a critical success) are notes. A teleport measured from something else ("within 500 feet of the army") isn't read.
  - Transposition Ammunition's "you fire transposition ammunition at any unoccupied square you can see within your weapon's first range increment … teleporting … into the square where you shot the ammunition" is teleport.weaponRange. Its range is the range increment of the ranged weapon the ammunition is loaded in (PF2e's selectedAmmoId), else of its user's held ranged weapon with the longest one, taken when the card is made. "If this would carry along any other creature … the activation fails" is teleport.alone. The greater one's two willing creatures along are notes.
  - Dweomerveil's "If you critically succeeded at the triggering save, you can instead teleport within 45 feet" is teleport.critical: a tick on the card, and the range checked is 45 feet when it's ticked.
  - "If this would bring another creature with you … the teleportation fails" (Quartz-Coil Rail Transport; Master Magus Ring's "the activation fails and is used") and Mad Mammoth's Juke's "You can't bring any other creature with you" are teleport.alone. The card moves only its user's token and says so in a line. Whether a creature rides along hidden in a container is the user's to say before Pick, as a requirement is.
  - Quartz-Coil Rail Transport's "You and all creatures in a line between your original location and your destination take 4 electricity damage" is teleport.line. Once the token has moved, the card deals that damage to its user and to each creature whose space the line between the centres of the two spaces crosses (PF2e's applyDamage), and names them.
- **Its user's own move** (`move.js`, the teleport card as a move, 2026-10-25): an activation whose Effect is a Stride, Step, Leap, Burrow, Fly, Swim or Climb for its user: Advancing's "Stride up to your Speed", Boots of Quick Marching's two Steps (10 feet), Staff of Sun Wukong's 50-foot Leap, Squire's Tabard's two Strides, Earthglide Cloak's Burrow, Chimera Flail's half-Speed Stride. Its range is its feet, a multiple of its user's land Speed (half rounds down to 5 feet), or its user's own Leap (10 feet at a Speed of 15, 15 at 30; Paws of the Grogrisant's doubled Leap twice that). A bonus "for this movement" adds to it (Cape of the Open Sky's +15 feet). Pick works as for a teleport, but the token moves on the map as a move does (animated, not Foundry's teleport), and the card says "Stride:", "Step:" and so on. A move in an activation's Trigger (Demon-Hunting Bands' "Trigger You Stride") is when it's used, not a move.
  - What the movement ignores, where it goes through and how it must end are the card's lines: "This movement doesn't trigger reactions", "Ignoring difficult terrain", "Through dirt and stone", "You can move only in a straight line", "Or Leap up to 20 feet horizontally …". Squire's Tabard's "your movement must end adjacent to an ally" is checked: the space must be within 5 feet of a creature PF2e counts as its ally. A move waits while the game is paused (Foundry 14's rule for a move; a teleport doesn't). The range's multiple of a Speed reads PF2e 8.6's land Speed (movement.speeds.land), which also fixes Master Magus Ring's and Mercurial Mantle's teleports.
  - Spleen Bloodstone's "For each additional action you spend, you can Step or Stride again with the same benefits" is move.again: the card stays open after a move, its button "Pick again (another action)", each measured from where the last ended.
  - Said other ways too: Umbral Armor's "allowing you to Stride twice your speed", Nightmare Cudgel's "As part of activating this item, you can Stride twice", Dancing Scarf (Greater)'s "You can also Stride up to half your Speed or Step". Umbral Armor's "causing you to become Concealed for 1 round afterward" is plan.userEffects: its user is Concealed as it's used.
  - A Strike along the way (Skyrider Sword's "making a melee Strike … during your movement", Lion Claw's "and then Strike", Chimera Flail's) is a note, so the activation stays partial: the card doesn't make it.
  - The move beside the rest: when the activation's plan reads everything but its move (Phase Spike's resistance 10 during its Stride; Invisible Chain Shirt's Concealed), the move is plan.item.move and its card is posted beside the item card. A sentence that says more than the move (Invisible Chain Shirt's "or until you use a hostile action") stays unread.
- **A check's success that pulls the creature toward its user** (`pull-toward.js`): Tentacle Cannon's Grasping Deep Shot, "On a success, the tentacle pulls the creature up to 10 feet directly towards you, until it's in a square adjacent to you". The success and the critical success get forcedMovement, a pull of up to 10 feet that stops beside its user; the ability module's card applies it with the core's forced movement. "A creature within 15 feet" is its range, not the Grapple's touch. "It can attempt to Grapple any creature, regardless of size" needs nothing, since the card never limits a Grapple by size.
- **A talisman of one dragon** (`bane.js` dragonWaysPlan; the item card's immunity, PF2e's Immunity rule): Dragonclaw Scutcheon's "immunity to all damage of that type until the end of your next turn", and Dragontooth Trophy's "the damage type of the affixed weapon changes to the type matching the dragon" on the triggering Strike (the readied Strike's DamageAlteration; its later Strikes until the end of the next turn are a note). The ways card picks the dragon's damage type. The three talismans' set bonus, a Dragon Breath, is a note. Talismans triggered by "You succeed at a Strike with the affixed weapon" now count as the triggering hit (`strikes.js` TALISMAN_HIT).
- **Effects picked from named paragraphs** (`index.js` chosenParagraphsPlan): an item that lists its choices as paragraphs, each with a bold name, after "Choose one of the following effects". One example is Alchemists+'s jar whose creature either confuses those who fail a Will save or blasts the creatures around it with sonic damage. Each paragraph is compiled alone as the item's use, so the ways card offers the save cards. A condition for "creatures that fail" a save is read as each creature's save or that condition. What comes before the choice (where the creature flies first) is a note on where to target.
- **A shot whose rider is the gun's modular configuration's** (`bane.js` modularWaysPlan): Hydrocannon's Tinker Shot, "A successful attack has an additional effect depending on which modular configuration the hydrocannon is currently in". The ways card picks the configuration: Bludgeoning's push (10 feet on a critical hit), Piercing's –10-foot Speed penalty for 1 round, or Slashing's 1d6 bleed (the Strike's extra persistent bleed). Each way is the readied Strike with the gun.
- **A Strike treated as a precious material** (`strike-damage.js` MATERIAL_STRIKE, `strike-card.js`): Quicksilver Cloak's "Make a ranged Strike against an enemy. The attack is treated as silver." The readied Strike's Effect carries PF2e's AdjustStrike materials (on ranged Strikes here), so its damage roll is silver, and the Effect waits for that damage roll.
- **Allies in an area put in a condition** (`rows.js` allyAreaRow): allies near its user who "are Quickened" (a minute, in a third-party scepter), read as for "gain". A third-party scepter writes its Frequency and Cost lines without an Effect heading, so the sentence is read from the allies on. Its limit on the extra action is a note.
- **A ray of stored energy at its user's spell attack** (`own-checks.js` SPELL_RAY): a third-party power ring's Release Power. It is a spell attack roll against AC for 5d4, doubled on a critical hit, and its type is the energy the ring stored. The exposure card offers its user's spell attack and picks the energy type (acid, cold, electricity, fire, force, sonic, vitality or void). The ring's own item bonus and the extra charges for more dice are notes.
- **Damage only the table knows the amount of** (`item-data.js` rewords, `scaled.js`, the save card's Amount field): Twisting Gale's sonic damage "equal to your normal Strike damage" in a cone behind the target, Juggernaut Plate's damage "equal to the total distance traveled", Kaldemash's Lament's barrage of Strike damage in a cone, Conflagration Club's "1d6 damage per charge stored" (its type, the charges' energy, picked there too), Rebound Fulu's damage back at the foe whose attack its shield blocked, Spiral Labyrinth's blocked damage (its Hardness 10 higher) at a creature its user picks, and Last Hope's 1d10 vitality per 10 Hit Points its user was healed, at the undead around it (the living creatures' healing is the GM's). Each is a basic save card whose damage the presser types as a number, or as dice for the club. The ability module's save card then gets that damage.
- **Rewords for cards that already exist** (`item-data.js`): Spring-Loaded Net Launcher gets the unattached Net's own rules at 60 feet, played as Halcyon Heart's thrown net (`thrown.js`). Azarim's Enfeebled 2 on whoever carries it becomes a DC 23 Fortitude save card at the creature targeted. A lawful creature, which the text gives no save, is the GM's to fail. Resolute Shield keeps off damage up to twice its Hardness: that's resistance (24, from the shield's own Hardness 12) to the triggering physical damage, read as Scapular of Shields' resistance. What the shield itself takes is the GM's. Rusting Carapace's rust is a basic DC 24 Fortitude save at the metal creature targeted, with 2d6 persistent damage on a failure; damage to an item is the GM's. Briar's save "to resist being" Stunned 1 (Stunned 3 on a critical failure) gets those rows. Key to the Stomach's "affected by a 5th-rank Cleanse Affliction spell to remove a poison" is the counteract card for one poison on its user (+15, rank 5). Gate Attenuator's "casts a 5th-rank spell" for the element it's attuned to puts its six listed spells on the cast card as picks, at its rank, attack and DC.
- **A breath that's its user's draconic benefactor's** (`permanent.js` benefactorBreathPlan): Pact of the Herald and Host's Breath of the Dragon is a cone or line of 1d6 damage per level, with a basic save against the higher of the user's class DC or spell DC. Its area, damage type and save are the benefactor's. The ways card picks the save (its three save links). Each way is that basic save in the 30-foot cone at the owner's better DC, with the dragon's damage type picked on the save card. The 60-foot line and the 1d4-round recharge are notes.
- **A bonus to its user's AC against an attack already rolled** (`rerolls.js` readArmorRaise, the reroll card's raise): Animal-Turning Fulu's "+2 item bonus to AC against the triggering Strike", triggered once the Strike hits. The card finds the latest attack roll at its user and works out its degree again against that AC + 2. The GM uses that result, and PF2e's message stays as it was. What it does on a miss (Concealed) is a note.
- **Rerolls and changes behind a coin flip** (`rerolls.js` COIN, SET_DIE; the reroll card flips 1d2 first): Lucky Copper, Silver and Gold reroll the triggering check (or save) on the smiling face. Fortune's Coin (12, or 14 for the Platinum) counts that number on the die instead of what was rolled, and works out the degree again. On the other side nothing happens. Robe of the Archmagi (Greater)'s "You or the ally automatically succeed at your save" turns the targeted creature's (else its user's) failed save into a success. The plain Robe of the Archmagi stays PF2e's AdjustDegreeOfSuccess, set before the roll.
- **Its user's save against the attack that hit** (`rerolls.js` readContest, the reroll card's "Roll the save"): The Demon's Lantern rolls its user's Reflex save with PF2e and compares it with the latest attack roll at its user. Higher, the attack misses: PF2e's message stays as it was and the GM undoes its damage. The attacker is then Dazzled until the end of its user's next turn. A tie isn't higher.
- **Rerolls said without "reroll"** (`rerolls.js` readOtherRoll, readConcealedAgainst): Keep Stone Amulet makes the caster roll the spell attack twice and take the worse result, so the card rerolls the latest spell attack at its user and keeps the lower one. PF2e marks a spell attack with "spell-attack-roll" among its domains. Scapular of Shields turns a critical hit at its user into a hit, which the GM applies. Extra Lung rerolls its user's latest save. Phantom Fang makes its user Concealed against the attack: if the attack hit, the card rolls a DC 5 flat check, and a failure means it misses. An attack that already missed needs no check. Eye of Fortune rolls its user's own flat check against the creature their latest Strike targeted twice and keeps the higher, since PF2e doesn't roll that check. The DC is 11 if the target is Hidden, else 5 (Concealed).
- **Degrees changed on another creature's roll** (`rerolls.js` readOtherRoll): Crimson Bluff's Swap Places makes the latest attack roll at its user one degree worse. Golden Greaves turns an enemy's failed Shove, Trip or Reposition at its user into a critical failure; the card finds the latest check at its user. Tome of Dripping Shadows turns the targeted creature's critical failure into a failure, and the Concealed and Hidden that follow are a note on the card. The Lost raises its user's own failed save by one degree. When there's nothing to change, the card says so. In every case the GM uses the result, and PF2e's message stays as it was.
- **An ally's condition taken onto its user** (`take-condition.js`, the take card, `scripts/take.js`): Bloodstained Waistcoat's Staunch Bleeding ends the targeted ally's persistent bleed damage and gives its user the same condition, with the same formula and DC.
- **Mythic Point costs** (`spend.js`, `scripts/spend.js`; the user's answer of 2026-10-19): a use whose effect is to "Spend a Mythic Point" (not a choice like "If you spend 1 Mythic Point when …", nor an intelligent item's "They spend"), and a cast of a spell with the mythic trait, carry `plan.spend`. On a ways plan the cost goes on each way; on a cast with choices, on the mythic ones. scripts/live.js checks it before a card applies and refuses without a point. Once the card's state has changed it spends the point, once per card whatever the steps, and posts a chat line. A cost on a route with no card (a zone, a conjured creature) is spent when the use is made. The world setting "Hero Points instead of Mythic Points" pays with Hero Points. Mythic Resilient and Mythic Armor Potency are two-button ways cards (a mythic source or any other) whose reroll cards change the degree. Mythic Striking rerolls PF2e's latest damage roll and posts the new roll if it's higher. Mythic Weapon Potency rerolls at mythic proficiency: the user's level + 10 in place of the roll's own proficiency modifier. Freedom's Flame's Holy Retribution is 15 spirit damage at the creature targeted, its holy weakness the GM's. PF2e only gives a character Mythic Points in a mythic campaign with a Calling. An NPC with the mythic trait has them.
- **A Mythic Point spent for more** (`mythic-upgrade.js`, the user's answer of 2026-10-19): an activation's "If you spend 1 Mythic Point …" upgrade becomes a ways card with two buttons, "As it is" and "Spending a Mythic Point". The second is the same plan upgraded where the text says how, and it costs the point. A cast's DC changes, its choices' own DCs too (Sorshen's Sinuous Guisarme: DC 50). The time a breath lasts changes ("you don't need to breathe for 7 days"): its rows' durations and its linked Effect's application.
