Otherwise the upgrade's words go on the card as a note (Vortex Pouch, Shield of Snakes, Sorshen's Scintillating Garment). A note-only plan, or one that already costs a point, is left as it is. The Cyrusian Wands' mythic proficiency is the spell module's.
- **Narrow bonuses on PF2e's roll options** (`item-data.js` rewords; the user's answer 14 of 2026-10-17: the whole roll, the narrower words a note):
  - Bonuses for named actions: Elixir of the Peaks' Athletics to Climb, High Jump or Long Jump (only while in mountainous terrain), Oozepick's Thievery to Pick a Lock or Disable a Device, Conspirator's Cookie's Diplomacy to Make an Impression or Request, Mender's Soup's Crafting to Repair, Aroma Concealer's Stealth to Hide or Sneak. Each becomes PF2e's action roll options.
  - Potion of Disguise's Deception DC, for 24 hours; the 2d12 hours the GM rolls ends it by hand.
  - Fungal Walk Musk's AC against fungi, by PF2e's origin trait.
  - Healing Vapor's bonus to saves against diseases and poisons, for the creatures targeted, as an area buff's.
  - Area buffs for the creatures targeted, as Knight Captain's Lance's: Inspiring Spotlight's saves and Charisma-based checks, Paradise Light's Will saves and Wisdom-based checks (its fast healing a note), and Undead Compendium's attack rolls and saves against undead. On an attack or its damage, the creature's trait is the target's (`target:trait`), not a save's `origin:trait`.
  - Rhyton of the Radiant Ifrit's Toast! is a bonus to Deception, Diplomacy and Intimidation, "against those creatures" a note. Mask of Shifting Monstrosity's Deception DC lasts for its 8 hours.
  - Hollow Robes' Empty Robes is its +2 item bonus to Intimidation for the minute. Its wearer isn't made Invisible: PF2e's condition would hide them, and the robes stay seen.
  - Third-party bonuses, read from their own packs: Steady Support's saves and Fortitude DC against forced movement (PF2e's `forced-movement` option on Shove and Reposition), Black Pearl of Mewru's saves against curses (its better degree on the triggering save a note), Draconic Steak's Intimidation and Honeyed Voice's Performance (who they work on, a note).
- **More Mythic Point and healing items:**
  - Whispering Veil's Spire Power gains a Mythic Point on the Focus Point card (`focus.js` resource "mythic"; a Hero Point under the world setting). It's lost at the end of the turn when unspent, as a Focus Point is. Fate Tempter's Ring gains one too. Preordained Spear keeps its doomed card, with the point a note.
  - Hollow Robes' Hollow Target ends all its user's persistent damage outright (`recovery.js` ends, no flat check) for a Mythic Point. The critical hit's damage reduced to 0 is the GM's.
  - Mask of Mercy rolls its user's latest healing roll again and keeps the higher.
  - Skeletal Claw gives the undead targeted 10 temporary Hit Points for 1 minute (`given-temp.js`: the core's automatic temp-hp row, on the ability module's card).
- **Bonuses against the creature marked** (`src/compiler/marked.js`): an item whose user picks one creature and gains a bonus against it. Spectacles of Inquiry and White Cleome's Eye give it to Sense Motive against that creature, and Oathbow adds 1d6 to the bow's Strikes against it for 7 days. Apply needs that one creature targeted. It gives the user an Effect with PF2e's TokenMark at that creature's token, and the bonus is predicated on `target:mark:<slug>`, so no other creature's rolls get it. Using the item again moves the mark. A roll with no creature in it keeps the whole roll: Oathbow's Survival to Track, with its creature a note. (Before this, the spectacles' bonus went to the creature targeted.)
- **One-off readings:**
  - Worldscale Shield's Absorb Blow is the readied-bonus card's immunity to physical damage for the triggering attack (PF2e's Immunity rule, spent by the damage it then takes, or at the end of the turn). It costs a Mythic Point. The shield then breaks: its Hit Points drop to its Broken Threshold, or to 0 if it was already broken. PF2e's data gives this shield no Hit Points, so with none the card says to break it by hand.
  - Avalanche Boots' Shove pushed farther is a ways card at the creature targeted: after a success, 5 feet more; after a critical success, 10 feet more; or 10 feet more and Prone (`shove-farther.js`). Each is the core's automatic row with a push, played on the ability module's card. A push alone now counts as a targeted row (`route.js`).
  - Spirit Bulb's "to have it cast" is the cast card's choice of two at the bulb's rank: Plant Form on its user, or Summon Plant or Fungus.
  - Emetic Paste gives its bonus to Fortitude saves for the hour to the creature targeted (its user, or a sickened creature in reach). That the bonus is for saves to reduce Sickened, and the save it makes at once, are a note.
  - Sky Hammer's Comet Fall reads as a cast of a 6th-rank arcane Fireball at DC 45. The spell module reads it too, with its centre and its user's immunity, so with the spell module active the activation is the spell module's.
- **Next-Strike riders:**
  - Hyldarf's Fang's Linnorm's Fang coats the hand cannon itself with its venom for the round (`hit-delivery.js`), so its next hit posts the DC 34 save card. The venom's block names no save; it reads as Fortitude, PF2e's save for injury poisons.
  - Sawtooth Reaper's Bloodmist Whirl readies its next Strike with the target Off-Guard to it. That's a new Strike extra (`strike-damage.js` offGuard), PF2e's EphemeralEffect of the condition on the attack. The fog's concealment and Ensnaring Disarm's free Strike are the Strike card's notes.
  - Cover a Strike ignores (`ignoresCover` in `strike-damage.js`, 2026-10-26): Golden-Cased Bullet's "ignore the bonus to AC granted to a target in standard or lesser cover" (each grade its levels) and Arcing Hand Crossbow's Curve Bolt, "This attack ignores the target's cover" (every level). The readied Strike's Effect has PF2e's EphemeralEffect of Effect: Alchemist Goggles (its one rule suppresses the target's cover bonus to AC), for a target with that `target:cover-level`.
  - A save's conditions longer after a critical hit (`criticalTime`, 2026-10-26): Rattling Bolt's "Deafened and Stupefied 1 for 1 round (or 1 minute on a critical hit)". After a critical hit, the save card's conditions last the minute (`scripts/strikes.js`). Its "An undead affected by a rattling bolt further loses any lifesense ability" is a note Effect on the failing rows for an undead (`plan.ifTarget`, `src/live/if-target.js`), lasting as long as the Deafened.
  - Scope of Limning's Outline readies the next Strike, and its hit leaves the glow as a note Effect on the creature for a round (`hit-notes.js`).
  - Starshot Arrow's Strike carries PF2e's `ignore-range-penalty:6`.
- **Coronet of Stars' reassurance** rerolls the targeted ally's latest failed check, keeping the higher (`rerolls.js` who "target"). The reroll card's applied line names the creature whose roll it was.
- **Saves for the allies of the creature targeted** (`hit-around.js` aroundAllies, plan.around): a third-party blade's judgment after a critical hit deals its own damage and Slowed to the target, then a basic Reflex save to that creature's allies within 20 feet, Slowed 1 on a critical failure. Apply posts the targeted card and that save card at the creatures of the target's side within the distance (PF2e's isAllyOf), never the user's own allies. The targeted card says it before Apply.
- **Megaphone Mutagen's benefit** is its bonus to skill checks of auditory actions, except Deception and Diplomacy. That's PF2e's `item:trait:auditory` option, the one its own Deafened rule uses, with the two skills left out by `check:statistic` (a new scope in `rows.js`). The longer range of auditory actions is a note.
- **What each melee attack roll sets off while an item lasts** (`attack-riders.js`, plan.item.attackRiders; Martial Mayhem Mutagen, four grades): Apply gives its user a watch Effect for the mutagen's time. Each melee attack roll by that creature then applies the rows for the degree PF2e rolled (`scripts/attack-riders.js`), through the ability module's applyChange. A hit makes the target off-guard to the user's next attack before their next turn (its scoped off-guard, as Feint's, spent by that attack). A miss makes the user Clumsy 1 until the end of their next turn. The Major's critical hit makes the target Clumsy 1. Each is said to the GM in the chat.
- **A condition on an own attack's critical hit** (`own-checks.js` criticalRider): a released creature's attack at its own modifier, as a third-party jar's pteranodon's, adds a condition said in its own sentence to its critical success row only (Dazzled for a round there). No PF2e item's plan changes.
- **A bonus for the creatures in its light** (`light-aura.js`, plan.item.lightAura; Sprite Apple): the light stays the item card's own Effect. Apply also starts the ability module's zone on the eater's token, as wide as the bright light and lasting as long, whose modifiers each creature inside gets: +1 Performance (Pink), +1 to saves against fear (Teal), +1 Nature (Chartreuse). Since 2026-10-26 the narrower halves are modifiers with PF2e's predicates: Chartreuse's +1 Diplomacy at a creature with the animal or plant trait, and Golden's −1 to the Will DC against a Diplomacy check (`will-dc` with `check:statistic:diplomacy`, as PF2e's Stone Face does for Intimidation). Its "can't be Concealed if you're visible …" is in the light Effect's words: PF2e has no rule for it.
- **Vine Arrow's hit rider** sits in the paragraph after its hit, so a reword joins them. The −10-foot penalty to Speeds is then the hit's (2d4 rounds), the Escape DC 19 a note, and Immobilized stays on the critical hit.
- **Only against what triggered it** (`readied-bonus.js` bonus.against, `againstRolled` in `scripts/fortune.js`). This covers a reaction's bonus, resistance or immunity against the triggering attack, check, effect or damage: Sparkwarden, Ferrofluid Urchin, Shacklebreaker, Deflecting Branch and about thirty others. The Effect lasts until that trigger resolves, with the end of the turn as the latest. What spends it:
  - an attack roll or a check at its creature (an attack that hits leaves one with resistance or immunity in place until the damage);
  - its creature's own save, when the bonus is to saves and no damage is still to come;
  - the next damage its creature takes, for one with resistance or immunity.

  The card says "against what triggered it". The note is gone, unless its sentence says more than the bonus, such as Broken Tusk Pendant's "or a +2 item bonus if …".
- **What a critical success at the readied roll gives** (`readied-bonus.js` bonus.onCritical): Champion's Laurels' "If you critically succeed at the check, you gain 2 temporary Hit Points that last for 1 minute". When that roll spends the Effect with a critical success, its user gets the ability module's temporary Hit Points for that time (`fortuneRolled`).
- **The next save against one trait's effects, whenever it comes** (`readied-bonus.js` bonus.lasting; Psychic Warding Bracelet): the readied-bonus card replaces the monster builtin "save-bonus", and its Effect has no time limit. A bonus for one roll with a predicate is now spent only by a roll whose options match it (`scripts/fortune.js`), so a Fortitude save doesn't use up the bracelet's bonus against mental effects.
- **A consumable's opening "This …" sentence is flavor** (`flavorSentence`): with no Effect heading, the first sentence after its Activate line is flavor when it starts "This" or "These" and has no link, number or rule word. Blood Sight Elixir's "This thick, blood-red elixir sharpens your senses …" is one.
- **A mutagen's opening paragraph is flavor** (`flavorSentence` in `src/compiler/flavor.js`). When a mutagen has a Benefit heading and no Effect heading, the paragraph after its Activate line is flavor if it has no heading, link, number or rule word. A partial plan drops it from its notes, so Martial Mayhem Mutagen's four grades now resolve. Dashes compare the same whether the notes write them as " - " or the text as "—".
- **A critical hit's harder save** (`CRITICAL_DC` in `src/compiler/strikes.js`, strike.criticalDC): Blister Ammunition's "On a critical hit, increase the Fortitude DC by 2 (DC 21), and the target is Dazzled for 1 round". After a critical hit, the Strike's save card uses that DC (`scripts/strikes.js`). The Dazzled is already the critical row's.
  - Rhino Shot's "If the hit with the rhino shot was a critical success, the target is knocked prone unless it critically succeeds at its save" is strike.criticalWorse. After a critical hit, the save card's success row gets the failure's Prone too, so only a critical success escapes it. After an ordinary hit the save is as written.
- **A Strike's save only for the creatures its damage harms** (strike.saveOnly; Gravebane Ammunition). "If the target takes vitality damage, it must succeed at …" means a creature with void healing, which vitality damage harms. The readied Strike's save card goes only to such a creature hit (`scripts/strikes.js`). The ammunition's extra vitality damage is PF2e's own rule on its Strike, so the card names it (strike.ownRules) without a note, and it isn't rolled twice.
- **A placed burst of light whose creatures get a bonus inside** (`spotlightPlan` in `src/compiler/covered-square.js`; Inspiring Spotlight, both kinds). PF2e's text, "Creatures within the burst gain a +2 item bonus to saving throws and Charisma-based skill checks", becomes the ability module's zone: placed within 240 feet (120 for the portable one), with its modifiers for each creature inside, for its minute. This replaces the earlier reading, which gave the bonus to the creatures the presser targeted. The darkness counteract, turning it off early and moving the burst are notes. Paradise Light's Sanctum works the same way (`emanationBonusPlan`): its 30-foot emanation on its user gives +1 item to Will saves and Wisdom-based checks to each creature inside. Its fast healing 2 inside and the hostile-action save are notes.
- **A critical failure's persistent damage of the type picked** (type-choice.js criticalPersistent, `withDamageChoice` in beads.js): Explosive Missive's "A creature who rolls a critical failure also takes 1d4 persistent damage of the same type". Each damage choice carries its own 1d4 persistent component of that type, on the critical failure row only, with the core's componentMultipliers keeping it undoubled. It was a by-hand note before.
- **A Strike's splash** (strike.splash; Jistkan War Crossbow, the Drake Rifles' Drake Shot): after the readied Strike's attack roll, the bomb card's splash list follows (`postStrikeSplash` in `scripts/strikes.js`). On a hit it lists the creatures within 5 feet of the target, since PF2e's damage roll already gives the target its own. On a failure it lists the target too, as the splash trait says. Each entry is ticked, and Apply deals the splash. Drake Rifle's sentence repeating the failure's splash isn't a note any more.
- **A vision its user ends** (`sensesOf` in `src/compiler/rows.js`, sense.toggle and sense.invested): Obsidian Goggles' darkvision lasts "until you deactivate it as an Interact action or the item is no longer invested by you, whichever comes first". The item's next activation deactivates it, removing its Effect and adding none, as a light with no time is put out. When the goggles stop being invested, an `updateItem` hook in `scripts/live.js` removes the Effect.
- **A Strike only against a kind of creature** (`AGAINST_KIND` in `src/compiler/strikes.js`, strike.against): Carver-cutter's Chop Down, "Make a Strike with the axe against a plant creature or a creature made primarily of wood". Its extra dice are predicated on `target:trait:plant` or `target:trait:wood`. A Strike at any other creature spends it with no riders (`riderFor`), and the card says so.
- **Counteracting a triggering spell or affliction in more words** (`FREE`, `CLASS_DC_MODIFIER`, `HALF_LEVEL_RANK` in `src/compiler/counteract.js`). Three wordings now reach the counteract card's single row for what triggered it: Bloodstone Doll's "Attempt a counteract check against the triggering spell" (+9, rank 2), The Locksmith's "the triggering affliction" and The Theater's "attempts to counter the misfortune effect". The doll's control of the summoned creature is the GM's note. The Theater and The Locksmith use their user's numbers (counteract.fromUser): half the user's level, rounded up, as the rank, and their best class DC − 10 as the modifier. The card fills these in from its user, or leaves a note if the user has no class DC.
- **A poison its miss gives** (`withItemData` and `withHitDelivery`, AFTER_MISS): Viper Rapier's "The target is affected as if successfully poisoned with cave worm venom (DC 43)". The text becomes that venom's own stages, as PF2e's Cave Worm Venom gives them, at DC 43. The trigger "You fail, but don't critically fail, an attack" makes the creature missed the target of the save card. The affliction takes the venom's name and its injury and poison traits, not the weapon's.
- **A shield that guards its user by itself** (`src/compiler/floating.js`; Floating Shield, two grades). Its own AC bonus from PF2e's data becomes a circumstance bonus to its user's AC for its minute, as if raised. That it can't Shield Block, and that grasping it ends the float early, are a note Effect for that minute (2026-10-26), its user's to keep.
- **A use after the first: a candy's or chew's "Secondary Effect"** (`src/compiler/secondary.js`). The block is compiled as an activation, with its "Secondary Effect" heading as the Activate line. The item becomes a ways card with two buttons: its use, compiled from its own words up to the secondary effect, and the secondary effect's own card, which can come whenever, since the card stays open. This covers Ginger Chew's sickened lowered by 1, Crackling Bubble Gum's pop, Silver Crescent's ray, Poison Fizz's spray, Galvanic Chew's stun and Cinnamon Seers' reroll. Before this, a linked-Effect plan dropped the secondary effect without a word, and those items counted as resolved. A secondary effect with nothing a card can play (Energizing Pill's, Scholar's Drop's) stays a note on the use, so the item counts as partial.
- **A condition its user lowers beside a card at another creature** (Grim Ring; reduce.js TRIGGERING names the condition: "Reduce the value of the drained condition you gain by 1, and the undead that caused the condition takes 2d6 vitality damage"). The expose card says it, and its Apply lowers the user's own condition as it sends the damage to the creature targeted (`scripts/poisons.js`). A sentence whose two clauses are each already done, a condition the plan lowers and damage it deals, isn't a note either.
- **One persistent damage type ended outright** (`readRecovery` in `src/compiler/recovery.js`): Assassin's Skin's "The persistent bleed damage ends." is recovery { types: [bleed], ends }, applied beside the healing typed on the card (the bleed damage). The recovery's own sentence is no longer left unread.
- **Damage said again isn't a note** (`withoutDamageRestated` in `src/compiler/direct-damage.js`). Some partial plans keep, as a note or a by-hand line, a sentence that only says the damage they already deal, its dice and type the plan's own: Clear Cutter's Axe's "deals an additional 1d10 persistent fire damage to the plant". That sentence goes, and the plan stops being partial when nothing else is left.
- **A potion the item holds** (`src/compiler/patch.js`, plan.item.patch; Potion Patch). PF2e doesn't track what fills it, so the item card picks one of its user's potions, up to the patch's level (10th for the lesser, 15th for the moderate, any for the greater). Apply uses that potion with PF2e's own use, and the potion's own card follows.
- **An alchemical item installed in the item** (`src/compiler/installed.js`, plan.item.installed). Its numbers are the activation's: Living Leaf Weave's elixir of life, Dread Helm's ampoule, Bone Dreadnought Plate's lodestone bomb, Ooze Skin's acid flask, Troll Hide's two elixirs, Powered Full Plate's bottled lightning. PF2e doesn't track what's installed, so the item card picks one of its user's own items of that kind. Apply gives an Effect with that item's numbers: item bonuses (a bomb's own bonus, or an elixir's "+N item bonus"), Resistance equal to its splash damage, FastHealing equal to its level, for the time the text gives (Ooze Skin's is the flask's level in rounds). Dread Helm's mental splash damage goes to each other creature within 5 feet. The installed item is then used up, both of them for Troll Hide. Powered Full Plate's lower Strength requirement is PF2e's ItemAlteration on the armor: 1 lower, or 2 with a +3 bottled lightning. PF2e works out the armor's penalties from that. Anything else the text says stays a note. Magnetic Shield isn't covered, because its metal weapons can't be told apart.
- **A whetstone's weapon riders** (`attack-riders.js`, rider.weapon). The item card picks one of its user's weapons, and the watch Effect lasts the whetstone's time, or 1 minute when the text gives none (the time PF2e's own whetstone Effects use, such as Mighty Counterweight's). Only that weapon's rolls count, by its `item:id`. Frenzied Quintessence Quickens its user for 1 round after each hit. The extra action's limit stays a note, as Quickened Standard's does, so the item counts as partial. Analysis Eye works on the first hit at each creature: the GM gets the creature's weaknesses and resistances in a whisper to choose from. With only one, the user gets it too. Leeching Fangs reads PF2e's damage-taken message, whose origin is the weapon. Hit Point damage that weapon deals to a living creature heals its user half as much, rounded down.
  - Its user's own action with that weapon (2026-10-27): Windstep Sheath's Step as part of Quick Draw is the watch Effect's words, with no rule (rider.rules empty).
  - Its own rules on that weapon (2026-10-27, rider.rules): Pinpoint Arrowhead's "you treat any cover the target has as one degree less". The watch Effect carries PF2e FlatModifiers on the picked weapon's ranged Strikes, by its id: +1 against a target in lesser or standard cover and +2 in greater cover. That is what the target's AC would be with one degree less cover. It lasts a whetstone's minute.
- **Silkspinner's Shield** links two PF2e Effects. Its user's own is the Climb one, picked by the item's slug (`OWN_LINKS` in `consumables.js`). The other, Animated Strike, is the struck creature's, so it isn't the GM's note either. For the minute, each shield bash Strike with that shield (`item:id`, the attack riders' own) gives the creature struck −10 feet to its Speeds for a round on a hit, or Immobilized for a round on a critical hit. A successful Escape against DC 30 ends either (the ability module's escape flag on the penalty's and the condition's Effect, 2026-10-25).
- **Bangles of Crowns' reaction** marks the creature its trigger names (the frightened enemy, the presser's target). Its +1 circumstance to checks for the round counts only against that creature, and it gives 15 temporary Hit Points for a minute. Checks count as rolls at a creature for the mark, while a save against that creature has it as its origin, so a save doesn't get the bonus.
- **Temporary Hit Points "that last until the start of your next turn"** (Immortal Bastion's 100) keep that time. Dropping to 1 Hit Point instead is the GM's.
- **Fulu Compendium's Paralyzing Page** applies a Spirit-Sealing Fulu at DC 25, reworded as that fulu's own save: Will or Paralyzed for a round, for the creatures in its 30-foot emanation. The card's note says only undead save. The same note comes from any area save "for all undead/living/… creatures in" an area (`area-trait.js`), such as Ghost Ampoule and the Guises of the Smirking Devil.
- **Savior Spike**: its trigger's Grab an Edge is the roll ("the check"), any check with PF2e's `action:grab-an-edge` (a Reflex save or Acrobatics, `TRIGGER_ROLLS` in `readied-bonus.js`). It readies +1 item and the degree changes, spent by that roll alone. A readied bonus's predicate is tested with PF2e's own Predicate, its `or` terms too. Degree sentences the card reads aren't notes as well (Matchmaker Fulu, Sanitizing Pin).
- **Item DCs from their user** (world setting `itemDCsFromUser`, the user's request of 2026-10-19; `raiseItemDCs` in `item-data.js`, `scripts/item-dcs.js`): as each card is posted, every save check against a fixed DC in its plan (its own, a Strike rider's, a zone's, a poison's, the creatures around it) rises to the user's higher of class DC and spell DC when that's above it, and so does a cast's DC. A note says so. Flat checks and the user's own checks against a DC aren't raised, nor the user's own save (`isOwnSave`, a self target). Bombs (`scripts/bombs.js`) and etched runes' critical-hit riders (`scripts/rune-strikes.js`) do the same on their own paths.
  - A poison (`isPoison`: PF2e's poison category, or the poison trait with contact, ingested, inhaled or injury) rises too. For a user with Potent Poisoner it gets +4 on top, past the feat's own cap of their class DC (the user's answer of 2026-10-19, since poisons are weak). That covers its exposure card and an injury poison coated on a weapon.
  - Setting or not, PF2e's Powerful Alchemy applies: an infused alchemical item (the infused trait) used by an actor with Powerful Alchemy saves at their alchemist class DC (`actor.classDCs.alchemist`), as PF2e's own rule does for inline checks. Bombs included.
  - One note says what raised the DC: "Its DC is 31, its user's spell DC 27 + 4 (Potent Poisoner), over the item's own 18 (the world setting).".
- **The item card's applied lines:** a bonus the ability module applies without a line of its own is said on the item card ("+2 item to performance for 1 hour"), so the card no longer reads "Nothing to apply." after giving one.
- **A roll on its own table** (`random-table.js`, the table card, `scripts/table.js`): Madcap Top's d20 table and Pistol of Wonder's d% table, plus Lucky Draw Bandolier's d6 card draw. Each row is compiled alone as the item's use. A spell it names ("Slow affects the target", "A 3rd-rank Illusory Disguise makes you look like the target", "creates a Toxic Cloud centered on the target") is the spell module's cast, at the table's own DC and spell attack ("Any spell DC required is DC 27"); other rows get any card the readers find. Roll rolls the die and posts that row's card, or its words for the GM. The top reads 11 of its 20 rows and the pistol 19 of its 32. A table whose die heads its first body row (a Magic+ robe's d20), or one of rows alone with its die said before it (Jabali's Dice's "Roll 2d6 to determine the effect"), reads the same way. A Foundry RollTable the text links (Rod of Wonder's "Roll on the Rod of Wonder table", Deck of Illusions' card "chosen randomly" from its deck) is drawn from live. The row drawn is compiled then (a row that links a spell is its cast at the item's own DC and attack), and its card follows.
- **Resistance to all damage but some** (`rows.js` allButResistance, the item card's Resistance with exceptions and doubleVs): Silhouette Cloak's resistance 20 to all damage except force and ghost touch, doubled against nonmagical damage. Its reaction gives "the benefits of the first activation" until the end of the turn, which `withItemData` fills in with that activation's resistance; its other benefits are by hand. The light trait's exception has no PF2e word, so it's a note. Phase Spike's resistance during its Stride, with its exceptions in parentheses, reads the same way.
- **Fruit it grows that heal when eaten** (`permanent.js` fruitPlan, plan.item.creates, `card.js` createdItemSource): Verdant Branch's "1d4 ripe and flavorful fruits" that each restore 2d6+5 Hit Points when eaten with an Interact action, and Satchel of Numberless Seeds' tree of 5 fruits. The item card puts that many consumables in its user's inventory, each eaten as the module's own heal. When they wither is a note.
- **Any skill, picked on the card** (`skill-pick.js`): Stone of Unrivaled Skill's "+3 status bonus to skill checks using the skill you chose for your contract" for 10 minutes.
- An artifact's **Destruction** paragraph isn't part of the activation before it (`permanent.js` splitActivations). Orb of Dragonkind's explosion "within 90 feet" isn't its breath's range, and the Bloodstones of Arazni's explosions aren't their activations' saves.
- **A skill action whose success does more** (`item-actions.js` successGains, `scripts/foundry.js` applyRiders): "You Administer First Aid to stabilize an adjacent ally with the dying condition. On a success, they gain 6d6+20 Hit Points, can immediately Stand as a free action, and become Quickened for 1 round." (Medic's Shield). PF2e's own First Aid is rolled, and on a success or better the action's row adds the healing (now rolled, not 0) and Quickened. Standing and the extra action's limit stay on the card.
- **A skill its user picked when the item was made** (`src/compiler/skill-pick.js`, the item card's picker): Words of Wisdom's "When the tattoo is applied, choose whether the phrase is of threatening words (Intimidation), persuasive words (Deception), or dramatic words (Performance)". The activation's "+1 item bonus to Diplomacy checks and checks of the skill associated with your phrase" and "When you roll a critical failure on a Diplomacy check or the associated skill during this time, you get a failure instead" become the Diplomacy bonus (the core's row) and an Effect with the picked skill's bonus and PF2e's AdjustDegreeOfSuccess on both, for the minute.
- **A penalty given to the triggering creature** (`withItemData`, `readOutrightConditions`): "You point the night verse at the triggering creature. A gust … freezes its bones, imparting a –5-foot status penalty to its Speeds for 1 round" (North Wind's Night Verse) is "The target takes a –5-foot …", at the creature targeted. A plan of target modifiers alone goes to the targets, not its user (`route.js` targetedRow). A penalty only from later hits (Silkspinner's Shield) isn't applied outright.
- **Ammunition whose damage takes the place of its weapon's** (`src/compiler/strikes.js` strike.instead, `scripts/strikes.js`): "The target takes 4d6 bludgeoning damage on a successful hit. Even on a failed attack roll (but not a critical failure), the target takes 2d6 bludgeoning damage" (Big Rock Bullet, all three grades), read as in place of the sling's damage. The readied Strike's attack roll posts that damage at the target on the ability module's card: the hit's, doubled on a critical hit, or the failure's. The sling's own damage isn't rolled. Catch Rock is the target's own reaction, so the card states it in a line rather than leaving a note.
  - **What its target must be, and what its target may do** (strike.requires, the strike card's "Its target:" lines): Starshot Arrow's "The target must be one you can personally detect", "There must be a line of effect between you and the target" and "The target must be within the maximum range of your weapon" are the user's to choose by, as a requirement is. Big Rock Bullet's Catch Rock is its target's reaction. An introduction to the range rule that follows it, Sniper's Bead's "its magic greatly lessens the effect of distance on your triggering attack", is said by that rule.
- **A snare whose damage type its crafter chose** (`snareChoices` in `src/compiler/snares.js`, the ways card): "If you choose stones, the snare deals 9d8 bludgeoning damage; if you choose spikes, it deals 9d8 piercing damage" (Striking Snare). There's one way for each, each the snare's zone with a basic Reflex save against its own damage. The ways card closes once one is placed.
- **Damage and a save with no degrees given** (`withItemData`): "it takes 2d6 cold damage and must attempt a DC 22 Reflex saving throw", where PF2e's own text ends (Ice Slick Snare). It's read as a basic save, and "It leaves a slippery patch of ice on the ground (the GM's)" is the zone's note.
- **A bonus PF2e can't narrow, on the whole roll** (`withItemData`): Vaccine's "+2 item bonus on all saving throws against other strains of the same disease" is +2 to saves against diseases, its one strain's immunity a note; Phantom Roll's "+1 item bonus to Stealth checks you attempt during the Avoid Notice exploration activity" is +1 to Stealth; Infiltrator's Elixir's "+4 status bonus to your Deception DC to avoid others seeing through your disguise" is +4 to the Deception DC (PF2e's "deception-dc"; adding the level when untrained is a note); Armor Polishing Kit's "+1 circumstance bonus on Diplomacy checks to Make an Impression on soldiers, guards, …" is +1 to Make an Impression. Each lasts its item's time and keeps the narrower words as a note.
- **Times in weeks and years, and "expires after"** (`itemDuration`): a stated "Duration 1 week" or "1 year" is read in days (7, 365), and "These effects expire 24 hours after you eat the roll" or "This bonus expires after 24 hours" is the item's time. A "for 1 week" in a sentence isn't the item's time: Cayden's Tankard's "that item can't produce the effect for 1 week" is another item's.
- **A DC's bonus** (`selectorsOf`): "your Deception DC", "Fortitude DC" are PF2e's "{statistic}-dc" selectors.
- **An item's own toggle switched on until its user moves** (`src/compiler/option-on.js`, `scripts/toggle.js`): Sure-Step Crampons' unnamed activation switches on PF2e's own "sure-step-crampons" toggle, whose rule gives the +2 circumstance bonus to Fortitude and Reflex against Shove, Trip, forced movement and being knocked prone. "until the next time you move" switches it off when its user's token moves.
- **Restored to its maximum Hit Points, its afflictions removed** (`src/compiler/restore.js`, plan.item.restore): "you're restored to your maximum Hit Points, and all afflictions of 20th level or lower affecting you are removed" (Elixir of Rejuvenation). The item card heals what's missing through PF2e (ending Dying through the core) and removes afflictions of level 20 or lower. Bringing back a creature dead for a week is a note.
- **Afflictions as Pseudo Afflictions tracks them** (`afflictionsOf`, `removeAfflictions` in `scripts/foundry.js`): PF2e's production builds have no affliction items, so a creature's afflictions are also Pseudo Afflictions' Effects ("Affliction Effect: Viper Venom", its flag holding the save and stages). The counteract card lists them as afflictions, its rank half the Effect's level and its DC the affliction's save, not as plain Effects (Contagion Metabolizer, Panacea). Removing one also removes the conditions it gave.
- **A drake rifle's Drake Shot** (`withItemData`, the Strike reader): "Make a ranged Strike with the drake rifle. As long as the Strike isn't a critical failure, the drake rifle deals 1 splash damage per weapon damage die to the target and creatures within 5 feet …" and "On a hit, the drake rifle provides one of the following additional effects based on its damage type" (Acid, Cold, Electricity, Fire, Poison). The readied Strike is the rifle's own. It carries its splash of its own type on the damage roll (a failure's splash is a note) and its type's rider on a hit: Sickened 1; a –10-foot status penalty to Speeds for 1 minute; Dazzled and Off-Guard for 1d4 rounds; pushed 10 feet and Prone; Enfeebled 1 and Clumsy 1 for 1 minute.
- **A weapon's own Strike, riders alone** (`src/compiler/strikes.js`): a weapon's activation Strike is that weapon's even with no extra dice (Gloaming Arc, Growth Gun, Tiger's Claw, Trollhound Pick), so only a Strike with it carries the riders.
- **A square it covers for a while, with a save on entering** (`src/compiler/covered-square.js`): Spider Gun's Web Shot (all three grades). The square is placed within 30 feet for 1 minute, and a creature that enters it attempts the Reflex save (Immobilized for 1 round on a critical failure), as the ability module's zone. The Athletics check in its place, a failure's difficult terrain and the webbing's statistics are notes.
- **A weapon's own critical-hit sentences** (`src/compiler/rune-strikes.js`, `scripts/rune-strikes.js`): "When you critically succeed at an attack roll with a mindlance, the target becomes Frightened 2 unless it succeeds at a DC 24 Will save" (Mindlance, Serpent Dagger), Thundercrasher's "a Fortitude save against your class DC" (its wielder's class DC, filled when it's posted; without one, the GM's), Stargazer's "Dazzling the struck creature for 1 round", Fearcutter's "a creature with the unholy trait" (only against it), Deflecting Branch's "you knock the target Prone", Spider Gun's persistent poison of 1d4 plus its damage dice. Damage that the weapon's own PF2e rules roll on a critical hit (Golden Blade of Mzali, Chain of Command, Bloodletting Kukri) isn't posted again.
- **An ointment for several creatures** (`othersTargeting`): Energy Ointment's "can be applied to up to 5 willing creatures" goes to each creature targeted, its picked type's resistance on each.
- **Clouds and gusts as zones** (`withItemData`, the ability compiler's zone reader): Smogger's smog "centered on you, which moves with you", Concealed inside "for up to 1 minute"; Smoke Fan's "The smoke fills a 5-foot radius … lasts for 1 minute" (a burst where it's used, Concealed inside); Wind Ocarina's gusts, an emanation of difficult terrain around its user for 1 minute (what they do to ranged attacks and gases are notes).
- **Allies who Stand at once** (`withItemData`, `readReductions`): Core Bugle's Reveille, "You and all allies within a 30-foot emanation can immediately Stand as a free action", ends Prone on its user and on the allies targeted.
- **Conditions on an adjacent creature, or one picked from a list, counteracted** (`readCounteract`): Violet Ray's "You apply the violet ray to an adjacent creature and attempt to counteract the Blinded, Clumsy, … condition with a counteract rank of 6 and a counteract modifier of +22" (the counteract card offers the creatures beside its user; one condition, its source's DC and rank), The Teamster's "Select one of the following conditions currently affecting you: …" (+31, its rank by its level; an automatic counter against a much lower source a note). "A single condition" picks one.
- **Snares that leave an area where they were** (`snareThen` in `src/compiler/snares.js`, the ability module's zone.then, ability d6e48fb): Smoke Screen Snare's concealing smoke for 1 minute (Lesser: its square and the adjacent ones; Greater: a 20-foot burst), Hampering Snare's 10-foot square of difficult terrain for 1d4 rounds, Shadow Cloak Snare's darkness in its square for 1 minute (a note: lighting isn't played). The first creature to enter sets it off; the area appears at the snare's square.
- **Squares that only make a noise** (2026-10-27, `alarmZone` in `src/compiler/snares.js`): Alarm Snare, Flare Snare (its flare seen 2 miles off by day, 5 on a clear night) and Popdust are a placed square, gone once set off. Setting it off sends the GM a note of the noise (the ability module's note save), and who hears it is the GM's to tell. Alarm Snare's note has its 100-to-500-foot range. A Tiny creature doesn't set Alarm Snare or Flare Snare off: their saves carry `only: { minSize: "sm" }`, the ability module's size filter for zone entries (ability 411cb9f, asked of the auto-combat session 2026-10-27). A smaller creature gets nothing there and the snare stays set. Hoof Stakes Snare's save has `minSize: "lg"` the same way (`sizeGate` reads "When a Small or larger creature enters the square" and "triggers only when a Large or larger creature enters its square"). "A Medium or smaller creature that enters" (Trip Snare, Torrent Snare, Pit Illusion Snare) is `only: { maxSize: "med" }` the same way (`sizeCap`, ability 4461e8f).
- **A snare's own area and trait** (2026-10-27, `snareZone`): Spirit Trap's "You set this snare up in a 10-foot-by-10-foot area" makes its zone a 10-foot square, and "The first creature with the spirit trait that steps into the area" makes its save `only: { traits: ["spirit"] }`. Any other creature leaves it set.
- **A critical failure's "but" told after it** (2026-10-27, guards.js asFailureBut): Tar Rocket Snare's "As failure, but the creature takes a direct hit. It takes 2d6 bludgeoning damage and is pushed 10 feet in the direction the rocket faces". The damage and push are read, so no GM line. The rocket's direction is a line of the card, as Expulsion Snare's square is.
- **A save for everyone around its square** (2026-10-27, `snareZone` burstOf, the ability module's zone.burst, ability 5e46e0e): Shrapnel Snare's "Everyone in a 10-foot radius of the snare's square", Puff Dragon's 10-foot emanation, and Bomb Snare's "The target and all creatures in adjacent squares" (an emanation of 5 from the creature). Set off, each posts one card of its save for the creature that set it off and every other creature the area reaches, its damage rolled once. The zone has no save of its own. Shrapnel Snare's critical success "The creature in unaffected," is read as meant (item-data.js). Bomb Snare stays partial: its damage comes from the bombs it's made with. Flame Drake Snare's 15-foot cone and Frost Worm Snare's 30-foot line "in the direction from which the creature entered" are aimed bursts (area type cone or line, ability baa2411). The ability module aims them back the way the creature that set the snare off came.
- **A creature's Perception check against the item's DC** (`src/compiler/perception-check.js`, the core's save family with Perception, core 60e5ff9): Mercurial Mantle's Cunning Dodge, the triggering enemy's DC 38 Perception check, Off-Guard for the turn on a failure and until the end of its next turn on a critical failure. Only its user's attacks counting, and the Step and Strike, are notes.
- **A mist given its radius later** (`withItemData`): Metalmist Sphere's "creates an opaque mist in a burst centered on one corner of your space … The radius of the burst is 5 feet" (10, 15 for the higher grades): a concealing burst for 1 minute, as a zone. Its damage to creatures with a weakness to the metal is a note.
- **An area of stalagmites it raises** (`raisedAreaPlan` in `src/compiler/covered-square.js`): Stoneraiser Javelin's 5-foot burst within 30 feet for 1 minute, difficult terrain, 4d4 piercing on each move into it, and the DC 28 Reflex save (Prone on a critical failure) for the creatures there as it appears, as the ability module's zone. Being pushed out, the cover and the stalagmites' statistics are notes.
- **A condition's penalty ignored for a while** (`suppressOf` in `src/compiler/rows.js`, plan.item.suppress): Chilled Fire Noodles' "you temporarily ignore the -1 status penalty to AC and saving throws caused by the Fatigued condition for 1 hour", Ring of the Weary Traveler's "For 10 minutes, you can ignore the penalties from the fatigued condition". An Effect with PF2e's AdjustModifier suppresses the fatigued modifier, and the condition stays.
- **A maneuver after the Strike's hit** (`followOf` in `src/compiler/strikes.js`, `scripts/strikes.js`): Iron Cube's "If it hits and deals damage, you can attempt an Athletics check to Trip the creature you hit. If this knocks the target Prone, the target takes 2d6 bludgeoning damage", Serpent's Lash's "you also Reposition the creature". The readied Strike's hit posts the maneuver's card (PF2e's own action, its success's damage after it) at the creature still targeted. The free hand, the multiple attack penalty and the distance are notes.
- **A flat check for the creature touched** (`withItemData`, `readRecovery` who "targets", `scripts/live.js`): a third-party balm used during Assisted Recovery, which names its damage types a sentence before the flat check it gives the creature touched. The check goes to the creatures targeted, even when the item's own Effect is its user's.
- **A creature's maneuver at its own modifier down a line** (`readOwnCheck`, `ownCheckPlan`): a third-party alchemical creature that Shoves each creature in its path along its line at a +N given as an inline roll. It targets each creature in the line, in order, and adds its critical success's own damage. Stopping at its first failure is a note.
- **A candy's bonus while chewed, its second use a note** (`ownRow`, `modifiersOf`): a candy chewed for up to 10 minutes that gives an item bonus to melee weapons' damage. It's read as the Strike damage of melee weapons for that time. A "Secondary Effect" is now a note instead of making the whole item a note.
- **A weapon's own activation shedding a trait and adding damage for a while** (`withItemData`, `weaponAdjustOf`, `strikeDamageOf`, `src/live/card.js`): a third-party whip's activation, for 1 round losing the nonlethal trait and adding bleed damage. Each is an Effect on that weapon alone, by its id: PF2e's AdjustStrike removing the trait and DamageDice of persistent bleed on its damage.
- **A module's own Effect its text doesn't bear out** (`src/compiler/unverified-links.js`; live play loads other modules' Effects' rules when the world is ready). Live play already checks such an Effect against the text before applying it and makes a note when they differ. The item is now compiled without that Effect, and what its text reads to is used instead when it's more than a note: an Effect a grade off, a status bonus where the text says item, other numbers. Six Alchemists+ items and one Tian Xia+ item read this way (Concealing Puffballs, the Greater Ravenous Flytrap, Mandrake Gum, the Major Aromatic Lavender, the Greater Moss Cover). The Effect stays for a text that is only a note, and for an item made in one of several kinds, whose text lists them side by side.
- **A resistance type picked when made** (`checkRule` in `src/compiler/verify-effect.js`): a type the Effect's own ChoiceSet picks counts as stated when each choice is stated (Croque Matron's own Effect now applies). An Effect whose resistance is limited to the damage's source by the rule's own predicate (`origin:trait:X`) doesn't count: PF2e tests that predicate on the creature itself, so the resistance never applies (Wrought Sirloin's), and the text is read instead.
- **An action's bonus said after it** (`readItemAction`): the item's activation is the action, and a later sentence gives the bonus to its checks (the Concealing Puffballs' item bonus to Sneak).
- **Temporary Hit Points an item grants you** (`tempHitPoints` in `src/compiler/rows.js`): "grants you N temporary Hit Points", for the stated time (the Greater Moss Cover's oil).
- **A released creature's Strikes along its Strides** (`readOwnCheck` in `src/compiler/own-checks.js`): a creature that Strides some times and Strikes once on each Stride, each time at a different creature (Compressed Gogiteth). The ability module's attack card targets up to that many enemies, at the item's modifier and damage. The Strides and the end on a critical failure are notes.
- **A spell replicated in words** (`readCastWords`): "replicating the effects of" an Nth-rank spell, with the DC its inline check gives (Encapsulated Titanosaur's Earthquake). The spell module casts it.
- **Ammunition that releases something around its target** (`withItemData`, `ammoStrike` in `src/compiler/strikes.js`): its persistent damage to the creature there becomes the Strike's extra persistent damage, and a condition while that damage lasts becomes a hit rider. Ending the condition with the damage is the GM's (Fume Ammunition). Ammunition whose text already says "On a hit" is read too.
- **Standing up after the action it's used in** (`readReductions`): "you can …, ending the Prone condition" ends its user's Prone (Surfing Grass). The extra Crawl distance is a note.
- **A third-party module's markup** (`withItemData`): a bold heading with its space inside the tag (`<strong>Activate </strong>`) and a bold action glyph are read as PF2e's own.
- **Resistance to damage some creatures deal** (`sourcedResistances` in `src/compiler/rows.js`, `resistanceEffectSource` in `src/live/card.js`): "resistance N to X and Y damage dealt by <trait> creatures", or "to <tradition> spells and to damage dealt by <creatures>". It becomes PF2e's custom resistance, its definition the damage types and `origin:trait:X` of the source, as PF2e's own Effects do it (Purifying Pie, Wrought Sirloin).
- **A light its user sheds** (`lightOf` in `src/compiler/rows.js`, plan.item.light): "you shed bright light in a N-foot radius (and dim light for the next M feet)", dim light alone, or "light like a torch" (20 feet bright, 20 more dim). It becomes an Effect with PF2e's TokenLight rule for the item's time (the Sprite Apples, Biolumncescent Risotto).
  - A radius its user picks (2026-10-27): Twilight Lantern's "reduce or increase its radius", from 5 feet up to double, triple or quadruple its standard radius. Its moonlight is always dim, so the standard is a hooded lantern's 60 feet of light, all dim. The card has a radius field (the standard at first); Apply refuses a radius outside the bounds, and the light it sheds is replaced by one of the radius typed (not put out). It lasts until its user deletes the Effect.
  - The item its user holds or wears can shed it too: "The candlecap sheds dim light", a weapon that "glows as bright as a torch" (Vine of Roses), "causing it to shed light as a torch" (Eclipse), "glows even brighter, shedding bright light" (Shining Hackle), and a light that works "as normal for a hooded lantern" (Oathlamp of Accord: 30 feet bright, 30 more dim). Candle's "A lit candle sheds dim light … for 8 hours" works the same way. It isn't read for a weapon a gem is applied to (Limning Gem) or for a planted toadstool's own glow.
  - Its time can come a sentence later: One-Hour Flower's "At the end of an hour" gives 1 hour, and "The radius of this light shrinks …" is a note on the light.
  - A light with no time lasts until it's put out: the Effect has no end, and the item's next activation deletes it instead of lighting it again ("Activating the candlecap again douses the light").
- **Once within a time, a bonus to one roll its user picks** (`pickedRoll` in `src/compiler/readied-bonus.js`): the readied-bonus card. Its Apply, pressed just before the roll picked, gives the bonus to the next roll of those kinds that turn (the Halfling Sandwiches). The time and the immunity after it are notes.
- **A fulu affixed to a creature** (`withItemData`, `affixedTargeting` in `src/compiler/primed.js`): the Thousand-Pains Fulus' "A creature to which the fulu is affixed must attempt a basic Fortitude save" and "The fulu deals" its damage. That creature, touched, attempts the basic save against the damage.
- **What a failed save primes for later** (`withPrimedNotes` in `src/compiler/primed.js`): "On a failure or critical failure, if the target … it takes …" becomes a note Effect on the target from those rows, for the item's time (the Thousand-Pains Fulus' persistent damage when their condition is met).
- **What the damage dropping the creature does** (`withZeroHPNote`): "If the target is reduced to 0 HP by this effect, …" is a note on the save's card (Meloth's Lifedrinker).
- **The activation itself one of several actions** (`readItemAction`): "When you Activate the blast boots, you can High Jump or Long Jump" offers PF2e's High Jump and Long Jump on the card (the Blast Boots). The extra distance is a note.
- **Temporary Hit Points from another creature's level** (`levelTempOf` in `src/compiler/rows.js`, plan.item.levelTemp): "You gain temporary Hit Points equal to twice the creature's level". The card takes the creature its presser targets (the one the trigger names) and gives that many through the ability module, for the stated time (Bloodthirsty, Blood-Drinker). "You regain HP equal to double the unholy creature's level" heals that much the same way (Holy's Holy Healing).
- **A save at its wielder's better DC** (`withItemData`): "with a Will save; the DC is equal to the wielder's class or spell DC, whichever is higher" becomes a basic save against the owner's class-or-spell DC (Meloth's Lifedrinker's Vision of Meloth).
- **A swarm sent at the triggering enemy** (`withItemData`): "flies to the triggering enemy's space …, dealing" its damage. The enemy the presser targets takes it (Wasp Guard).
- **Not read:** a character weapon's own longer reach (Soulpiercer's "reach of 15 feet"). PF2e's reach-15 is an NPC attack's trait, which AdjustStrike won't add to a character's weapon.
- **Healing beside a linked Effect** (`withStatedHealing` in `src/compiler/consumables.js`): when the text gives its user "You regain …" or "you recover …" healing outright and PF2e's data has none, the core rolls that healing as the Effect is applied (Kindler Cameo, Wine of the Blood, Golden Breath Fulu, Chalice of Justice, Emerald Fulcrum Lens). An item that gives its bearer void healing still heals them. Healing under a condition, recurring, or for others isn't read this way.
- **A Perception check with its link's other parameters** (`src/compiler/perception-check.js`): Purloining Cloak's "@Check[perception|dc:38|name:…|showDC:all]" before your Strike, as Mercurial Mantle's.
- **Other senses, with their acuity and range** (`sensesOf` in `src/compiler/rows.js`): bloodsense ("blood sight"), tremorsense, scent, lifesense, echolocation, wavesense, thoughtsense and motion sense. Its acuity is read when the text calls it precise or imprecise (imprecise otherwise), its range in feet in the same sentence or one after ("Blood sight has a range of 60 feet"), and its time, also when given "with a duration of" one. PF2e's Sense rule with them, on the item card's Effect (the Blood Sight Elixirs).
  - A range sentence of its own that says only the range and what the sense detects is read with it. Blood Sight Elixir's "Blood sight has a range of 60 feet and allows you to detect living creatures who are taking persistent bleed damage or who have the dying or wounded conditions" is the sense's meaning, which its Sense rule carries to the GM's table as any sense does. The major elixir's approximations of a creature's condition stay a note.
- **What a hit does that isn't a condition** (`src/compiler/hit-notes.js`, `compileStrike`): with a save on the hit, the save's own card at the creature hit, its failure and critical failure a note Effect on it for the time the text gives (Blister Ammunition's itch, Escalation Ammunition, Juxtaposition Ammunition). A critical hit's condition is that row's (Blister Ammunition's Dazzled), and a critical hit's higher DC is a note. With no save, the hit's own lasting effect is a note Effect on the creature hit, when the text gives its time (Magekiller Bullet's curse for a week, Kortos Diamond, Beacon Shot, Conduit Shot, Wormhole Shot). It is read only from the hit on. It is not a save before the attack (Reactive Flash), not what its user gains (Reaper's Toll), not a list of options (Hydrocannon), not a Strike its trigger names, and never in place of another reading.
- **A spell's effects in words, its DC first or after** (`readCastWords`, `withItemData`): "This has the effect of a DC 37 vision of death spell" (Dread Blindfold), "This has the effects of a Scrying spell (DC 40)" (Planar Ribbon). The spell module casts it at that DC.
- **An ally in the banner's aura stands** (`readReductions`): "An ally within the banner's aura can Stand as a free action" ends Prone on the ally targeted (Standard of the Sure-Footed).
- **A condition for as long as a persistent damage lasts, any row** (`withWhilePersistent` in `src/compiler/primed.js`, for consumables, activations, snares' zones, Strike saves and bombs): a condition with no time of its own, which a sentence ties to the persistent damage ("for as long as it takes this persistent damage", "while the persistent damage lasts", "until the persistent damage ends"). Its type comes from the row's own persistent damage, else, for a bomb, the one its text names (Freezing Embrace Snare's clumsy, the Spider Satchels' Fascinated — "fascinates the target" read as the condition). It is not used when the condition's floor is what lasts (Tallow Bomb), nor when the row's persistent types are several (Bloodburn Censer's fire or poison).
- **A condition for as long as a persistent damage lasts** (`degreeRow` in `src/compiler/spellguns.js`): "The target is dazzled while the persistent damage lasts" is the condition with `duration: { mode: "while", persistent: <its persistent damage's type> }`. The ability module (17daa23, after 0.8.1) gives it with that damage and takes it away when the damage ends (the Sparking Spellguns). The persistent damage must land in the same row. Fume Ammunition's comes with the later damage roll, so its Dazzled still has no end and a note.
- **Extra Strike damage of dice set by the armor's save bonus** (`withItemData`): a Battlezoo hide whose extra electricity is a d6 for each point of its save bonus against electricity gets 1 + its resilient rune (at most 4) dice of that damage on its melee Strikes for the stated time (Runebranded Hide).
- **Damage of its user's choice of two types, each degree giving both** (`withRowTypeChoice` in `src/compiler/type-choice.js`, `withDamageChoice` in `src/compiler/beads.js`): "your choice of fire or poison damage", with each row's "@Damage[…fire] or @Damage[…poison]", becomes a picker of those types. Each type has its own rows, and a condition "until the persistent damage ends" lasts with that type (Bloodburn Censer). Before, a row dealt both. Bloodburn Censer's aura activation has no item card yet, so this reaches the simulator only.
- **Live routes for plans read but not played** (`src/live/route.js`, `src/live/poison-card.js`): an affixed fulu is used, not set off by its primed damage's condition (the Void and Burl Thousand-Pains Fulus). An "aura" counts as an aura activation only as a trait ("(aura, manipulate)"), not as flavor ("it has a foreboding aura"). That gives a save card to the Banners of Creeping Death's Void's Embrace, Moritype, Morning Glow's Reveal Demons, Robe of Beneficence's Compelled Nonviolence and Daybreak Ammunition.

- **A square its user coats** (`coatedSquarePlan` in `src/compiler/covered-square.js`): Colorful Coating's 5-foot square within reach for 1 minute, as the ability module's zone, with what each colour says there. Violet gets difficult terrain, and its Reflex save on entering (a –15-foot penalty to Speeds, Immobilized on a critical failure) through the ability compiler's reading. Green gets difficult terrain, with its uneven ground's Balance DC as a note. Orange gets hazardous terrain's 1d4 acid on entering. Escaping, climbing and "once per round" are notes. A colour with none of these (Blue, Red, Indigo, Yellow) stays the GM's.
- **A snare by its trait** (`isSnare` in `src/compiler/snares.js`): a consumable with the snare trait is a snare, whatever its category (Grasping Tree's is "potion").
- **An AC bonus against the triggering attack, as a reaction** (`readiedBonusOver` in `src/compiler/readied-bonus.js`): the ability compiler's "ac-reaction" is a monster's own reaction, which its live cards offer only for NPCs. An item's is the readied-bonus card instead: its user's Effect for that turn, as Sparkwarden's (Deflecting Branch, Bismuth Armor, Cane of the Maelstrom, and Dragon Turtle Plate's "to gain a +1 …"). A miss does what the text says: Bismuth Armor's caster is Dazzled for 1 round (bonus.onAttack.miss, the ability module's timed condition).
- **A poison or curse a weapon's hit gives** (`src/compiler/hit-delivery.js`, `postPoisonCard`, `poisonStrike`): when the trigger is the hit itself ("You successfully Strike a creature with the lionfish spear", "You damage a creature with the serpent dagger"), the save card goes on use to the creature hit (Lionfish Spear, both grades; Serpent Dagger). When it's the next hit ("The next creature you successfully Strike with this weapon is exposed to …", Alghollthu Lash; Protector's Final Gift; Viper's Fang's "If you hit with this attack"), it's the injury poison's coat card, with three differences. It coats only that weapon (a talisman's coats any of its user's weapons). A critical miss doesn't waste it. And it lasts the time the text gives ("within the next minute").
- **An emanation around its user, damage on entering or a turn's start there** (`readSelfAura` in `src/compiler/aura-zones.js`): "engulfs you" or "orbit you" in an emanation template for a time, and "Creatures (and objects) that enter this aura or start their turn within it take … damage (… save)". It's the ability module's zone on its user's token, the basic save at those times (Incendiary Plate, whose area is difficult terrain for others; Horn of Rust). Its user's own bonus, when PF2e has an Effect named after the item, is that Effect on its user as the zone starts (Horn of Rust's +1 item bonus to AC, `scripts/zones.js`).
- **A save for its drinker's attacker, whatever its sentence** (`isLaterSave`): one any creature that hits its drinker sets off (Frogskin Tincture's toxin for a biting attacker, `withHitDelivery`) is the later card for the item's time, even when its save sentence names another case ("If you are Swallowed Whole …"). So is a fulu's save set off by its creature's own later action (Stumbling Fulu's "When the creature to which the fulu is affixed completes a Stride action").
- **Fewer false "later" and "aura" readings** (`LATER`, `saveSentence` in `src/live/poison-card.js`): "aura" counts as an activation trait only in its trait list ("(aura, concentrate)"), not in "a stench aura, such as …" (Noxious Jerkin). "You can use the higher of your class DC or spell DC" isn't an action for later. A save given only by its link counts as the save's sentence (Linnorm's Sankeit's cone).
- **A save only for a drinker of some kind** (`KIND_GATED` in `src/compiler/index.js`): "If you're unholy and drink this potion, it fails to work, and you must succeed at a … save" is a note beside the item's own linked Effect (Elysian Dew's Aura: Elysian Dew).
- **A spell cast on a Strike's hit, in other words** (`castOnHit`, `strikeOver`): "On a hit, the trident casts 3rd-rank Paralyze on the target (DC 25)". It wins over a save the ability compiler read beside it (a disease another activation gives). "If the Strike was a critical hit, the target is Stunned 1 even if it critically succeeds at the save" is the critical hit's rider (Devil's Trident).
- **An activation's save whatever its trigger reads** (`pressedEvent` in `src/live/poison-card.js`): a permanent item's activation is pressed by hand when its trigger comes, so its save card goes to the creatures its presser targets, whatever event the trigger reads as (Reactive Mail's spike at a creature using a manipulate action; Exploding Shield's cone as it's destroyed). A Strike's and a zone's keep their own cards. A consumable named "snare" is a snare, but a permanent item isn't (Snare of Speed is a drum).
- **Its user's own skill check against a creature's DC** (`targetedCheck` in `src/live/route.js`): the ability module's card at the creatures its presser targets, its user's skill rolled through the core's check family. Its degrees' text is the GM's when it isn't read (The Crows' Thievery against the Reflex DC; Whip-Tongue Sling's Athletics against the Fortitude DC; Spider Lily Tattoo's Deception against the triggering undead's Perception DC; Self-Emptying Pocket; Hodag Leather's toss after its Strike).
- **An aura's save as it's made and on entering, its damage type picked** (`choiceAuraWays` in `src/compiler/aura-zones.js`): an aura activation whose rows give "your choice of fire or poison damage" (`withRowTypeChoice`), and "Each living creature that's in the area or enters it attempts" the save. It's the ways card, a way for each type, each the zone on its user's token with that type's rows. Its condition lasts while that persistent damage does (Bloodburn Censer). "Living" and the hour's immunity are notes. As with any emanation, its maker gets a card as it's made, and the GM can skip it (the maker chooses whether it's affected).
- **Walls it raises** (`raisedWallsPlan` in `src/compiler/covered-square.js`): "walls … in two 60-foot lines going in opposite directions", greater difficult terrain, "Whenever a creature enters or starts their turn in the wall's space, they take … damage (… save)". It's one 120-foot line placed through its user's space, with the basic save on entering and at a turn's start (Alkenstar Phalanx). The walls' height and cover are notes.
- **A save with a failure's damage and a critical failure's exposure** (`withItemData`): "If the target fails a … save, it takes … damage. If the target critically fails the save, it's also exposed to …" becomes its degree rows. The exposure is the critical failure's note, with the poison's own save (Tarantula Ampoule).
- **A talisman's hit, with no Effect heading** (`TALISMAN_HIT` in `src/compiler/strikes.js`): "Trigger Your Strike with the affixed weapon hits a target Requirements …", what follows read as for a trigger's hit. "The weapon's physical damage for the Strike becomes spirit damage" is that damage roll's type (Blessed Ampoule, PF2e's DamageAlteration on the readied Strike, spent by the roll).
- **A snare's outright damage, no save** (`damageSnare` in `src/compiler/snares.js`): "The target and all creatures in adjacent squares take @Damage[…] damage" is the zone's damage on the creature that enters, once. The adjacent creatures' damage is a note (Boom Snare).
- **A save for whoever the affixed weapon hits** (`withItemData`): "A target hit by a ranged Strike from the affixed weapon must succeed at a … save or be silenced" becomes the next ranged Strike's save on a hit. Its failure's silence is a note Effect for the time the text gives (Golden Silencer, both grades).
- **A cloud breathed into an adjacent square** (`belchedCloudPlan` in `src/compiler/covered-square.js`): "a cloud … that fills a single square adjacent to you. Creatures within the cloud are Concealed … remains for 1 minute … deals … damage to creatures that enter the cloud on their turn, as well as to creatures that start their turn in the cloud". It's the zone in that square, its damage on entering and at a turn's start. Once a round, the wind and the tonic's later uses are notes (Capsaicin Tonic).
- **A cloud's time and cover in other words** (`withItemData`): the Spun Clouds' "The cloud dissipates after 1 minute" is the zone's time. Black's "A dark storm cloud conceals creatures within it" is Concealed inside, and Red's "Any creature in the cloud, or that later enters the cloud, must succeed at …" is its save as it's made and on entering.
- **One check against each enemy's DC** (`withItemData`): "attempting a single @Check[intimidation] check compared to the Will DCs of all enemies within 30 feet" becomes the check against each targeted enemy's Will DC, on the ability module's card (Gorget of the Primal Roar). The card rolls for each target, not once for all.
- **A wound that makes the creature hit give off an aura** (`src/compiler/hit-auras.js`, strike.zone, `scripts/strikes.js`): "afflicts the next living target struck by the dagger … emits a stench aura in a 10-foot emanation for 1 minute … must succeed at a … save or become Sickened 1". The dagger's next Strike posts, on a hit, the creature's save card. The ability module's zone is attached to that creature's token, with the same save for the others in it (Ghoul Stiletto's at a turn's start or end; Ghast Stiletto's for those there as it starts and those entering). The critical failure's Slowed lasts while the creature is sickened, and the immunity is held by `immunity.js`. Since 2026-10-26, "the next living target struck" is checked as it hits: a creature PF2e calls undead or a construct (its mode of being) doesn't take it, and neither does a miss, so nothing is spent. Ghast Stiletto's "–2 circumstance penalty to saves against disease and to recover from the sickened condition" is the zone's modifier for each creature inside, as PF2e's own Effect: Stench has it (`item:trait:disease` or `action:retch`).
- **Saves for the creatures around the one a Strike hits** (`src/compiler/hit-around.js`, strike.around, `scripts/strikes.js` with `creaturesNear`): "All creatures in a 10-foot emanation around the target of the attack (not including the target) must succeed at a … save or be pushed 10 feet from the target" (Dispersing Bullet), and a pull toward the creature hit followed by "explodes, dealing … damage to the original target and all creatures in a 5-foot emanation (… basic save)" (Singularity Ammunition). On the ammunition's hit, each save card is posted at the creatures within that distance of the creature hit, and at that creature too when the text includes it. A push or pull from or toward that creature is its rows' forcedMovement, and the card is posted with the ability module's moveFrom (ability 0.9.2), so the push or pull is measured from the creature hit and a pull stops beside it. With an older ability module it's the card's note, moved by hand.
- **One of its user's items moved or changed, or itself** (`objects.js`, the objects card, `scripts/objects.js`, 2026-10-25): what it does is picked on the card from its user's own items, listed when it's posted, and applied on the active GM's client as PF2e keeps items.
  - Store: Retrieval Belt's "One item you're holding with a Bulk of 1 or less vanishes into the belt's extradimensional space". The item goes into the belt (a PF2e container: its containerId, stowed). Retrieve: "The item stored in the belt appears in your hand", out of it and held. "Neither Store Item nor Retrieve Item can be activated again for 1 minute" stays a note.
  - To hand: itself (Lady's Knife's, Blackaxe's), or one picked (Retrieval Prism's attuned item of 1 Bulk or less, Called's item). It's held, in two hands when its usage says so.
  - Don: Crushing Coils' "The armor wraps around you, allowing you to don it": itself worn.
  - Repair: The Forge's "restore the object to its full Hit Point total, removing the broken condition", for one of its user's items of up to 4 Bulk with Hit Points below their maximum. An object that isn't its user's is the GM's. By an amount: Rust Scrub's "restore 2d4 Hit Points to an item that has been damaged by rust" is rolled on Apply and added up to the item's maximum. "The GM determines what portion of damage to an item was caused by rust" is a card line, your ruling for numbers left to the GM (the text's number applied, the GM may change it).
  - Reload (2026-10-26): Autoload Leathers' "You reload a weapon with reload 1". The picker lists its user's weapons with that reload, room for a round and ammunition for them (PF2e's `isAmmoFor`). Apply loads one round with PF2e's own `attach`, as the weapon's reload button does.
  - A weapon made in the hand: Boots of the Secret Blade's dagger ("draw a +1 striking dagger from it, and make a ranged or melee Strike with it") is PF2e's own Dagger with the text's runes, held, flagged as the boots'. The next activation's dagger replaces it, as the text says. Necklace of Knives' dagger lasts while it's held: once it's dropped, stowed or worn, it's gone (an updateItem hook on the active GM's client). The Strike is its user's own, with the new weapon.
- **Your rulings for what has no game numbers** (`decided.js`, 2026-10-25, docs/needs.md "Your decisions"): an entry whose leftover words show only messages and speech, minds, or information (src/compiler/needs.js, the needs index's own rules) and no game numbers at all is done as you decided. Game numbers are dice, DCs, distances, bonuses, conditions, damage, Strikes or teleports; those stay work to build.
  - Speech and minds: a note Effect of its words for its time. A note-only plan keeps its note row and is no longer note-only. A plan with nothing read gets a note row. A partial plan's GM lines become note Effects on the row they're for (Spider Lily Tattoo's undead that believes you're undead, Sinuous Recorder's attitude).
  - Information: plan.whisper, posted as a card whispered to the GM and the user's owners, the GM saying what it tells (Navigator's Star, Ring of Sigils, Linguist's Dictionary, Signaling Snare).
  - Never the spell module's items (its catalysts and scrolls): only items this module handles.
  - Out of combat and objects (2026-10-27, your answer): when what's left is only these, or these beside speech, minds or information, the card shows it as its own lines and the entry counts as done (decided "card-line"). A partial plan keeps its notes, and its unread lines and item-card GM lines join them, because the item card shows neither. A plan with nothing read gets its words as the card's line (Everyneed Pack, Timeless Salts, Tentacle Potion's limb). It never applies to a unique item or an artifact (`isSpecialItem`): their rules get built. Nor does it apply to words that act on a creature in a fight (a condition, a Strike, a save, the next elixir: `COMBAT`), which stay work to build.
  - A unique item's or an artifact's lore (`src/compiler/lore-lines.js`, 2026-10-27): their effects are all built, never card lines in their place (your ruling). A sentence with no rule in it (its history, its look, how it's destroyed) is listed by the plan's source name. Once every sentence its card leaves over is listed, those become its card's lines and it counts as done (decided "lore"). A rule beside them keeps it partial.
  - One-off rules (`src/compiler/one-offs.js`, 2026-10-27, your answer): a rule no other item would use becomes its card's lines and counts as done (decided "one-off"). It's listed with what building it would take in `docs/build-later.md`, which `tools/needs.mjs` writes (Baleblood Draft's next elixir 50% longer). Never a unique item or an artifact.
- **What the ability compiler leaves as notes, read into a row** (`row-readings.js`, 2026-10-25):
  - A shrink for a time: Reducer Round's "The target shrinks to Tiny size for 1d4 rounds. Its reach becomes 0 feet, and it's Enfeebled 1 for this duration" is that row's note Effect with PF2e's own rules, as PF2e's Shrink Effect has them: CreatureSize tiny, reach 0, equipment resized. The conditions "for this duration" are granted by that Effect (GrantItem), so they end with it instead of lasting forever. A rolled time is its average on the Effect.
  - Persistent damage a failed save adds: Splintering Spear's Shatter, "A creature that fails also takes the same amount of persistent bleed damage as the spear deals on a hit". The failure and critical failure deal the basic save's damage and the spear's own 1d6 bleed (from the item's text) as persistent damage.
  - Healing by its user's condition value (2026-10-26, `src/live/scaled.js`): Oracular Crown's "The amount of healing increases to 5d8 if you're cursebound 2, 7d8 if you're cursebound 3, or 9d8 if you're cursebound 4". The heal carries `byCondition`, and the card picks the formula from its user's cursebound value as it applies.
  - A save bonus only against persistent damage (2026-10-26): Antivenom Potion's "for 1 minute after drinking the potion, you gain a +1 bonus to Fortitude saving throws to avoid taking persistent poison damage". It's a note Effect for the minute in place of the whole text's reminder, as PF2e has no option for a save against persistent damage. Its flat check is the card's own.
  - What a critical failure's Prone makes it do (2026-10-26): Deravon's Helm's "on a critical failure, the target must spend all three actions on its next turn remaining prone". It's a note Effect on that row to the end of its next turn, a behaviour rule beside the Prone the rows give.
  - A save bonus only to reduce the sickened condition (2026-10-26): Emetic Paste's "+2 item bonus to that save and to all saving throws to reduce the sickened condition for 1 hour". It's no longer a plain bonus to every Fortitude save: the creature's Effect has PF2e's FlatModifier for a Retch (`action:retch`, as PF2e's Stench uses it) and a roll note on its Fortitude saves while sickened. Its save at once is the creature's own roll, a line of the card.
  - An ignition its hit needs (2026-10-26): Oil's "You must succeed at a DC 10 flat check for the oil to ignite successfully when it hits" is `ownCheck.ignite`. The card rolls that flat check as it's thrown, since it doesn't depend on the attack roll. On a failure, the attack card goes out with no damage on a hit. Where it lands is a line of the card.
  - An Effect each hit shortens (`shortensOnHit` in `installed.js`, `scripts/shorten-on-hit.js`, 2026-10-26): Bone Dreadnought Plate's "each time you're hit by an attack that deals damage of one of these types, decrease the remaining duration by 1 minute". The core's damage listener sees an attack's damage (a weapon's or an NPC's attack) of one of those types on its wearer. Once it's applied, the installed Effect is a minute shorter, whatever its resistance left of the damage, and the owners and GM are told. "Once activated, the armor can't be deactivated" needs nothing.
  - An illusion's disbelief undone (2026-10-26): Storyteller's Opus's Hoodwink, "If you succeed, the creature doesn't disbelieve the illusion". Both successes give the creature a note Effect saying so, in place of the GM's line, as your ruling has illusions under the GM's control.
  - A weapon's own glow (2026-10-26): Boughshatter's Absorb Corruption, "While under this effect, the morningstar glows with dim green light in a 20-foot radius", is the item card's light Effect for its minute (`lightOf` in `rows.js`).
  - A healing its item stops for a while (`toggle-off.js`, `scripts/toggle-off.js`, 2026-10-26): Splithead Bow's Two-Headed Arrow, "after using this ability, you do not regain Hit Points from wielding the bow on your next turn". The bow's own PF2e toggle that gates its fast healing (`splithead-bow-healing`) is turned off as the activation is used, with an Effect on its user to the end of its next turn. When that Effect is gone, the toggle is back on.
  - A class's way to use it (2026-10-26): Spectacles of Inquiry's and White Cleome's Eye's "If you're an investigator, you can use this activation as part of the action you take to Devise a Stratagem or Pursue a Lead …" is its user's to take, a line of the card (`isCardLine` in `row-readings.js`, which the mark's reading honours too).
- **Temporary Hit Points equal to the damage it deals** (`equalTempHP` in `guards.js`, 2026-10-25): Life's Last Breath's "or take 4d6 void damage; you gain an equal amount of temporary Hit Points that last 1 minute". Each row that deals damage also gives its user temporary Hit Points of the damage that row rolled, for the minute (the core's temp-hp `fromDamage`, recipient self). Any other amount stays a note for its user.
- **Falls eased, through PF2e Automatic Environment Resolution** (`falls.js`, `scripts/falls.js`, 2026-10-26, its session's contract: core a05e18c, environment c434cc6). The environment module rolls falling damage on its disaster cards (fissures, a tornado's pick-up). Its rule: any noDamage means none, else the largest `shorter` comes off, and a creature lands Prone only if the fall hurt it (and has no landsOnFeet).
  - An activation or a consumable: the item card's Effect, flagged `flags["pf2e-automatic-environment-resolution"].fall = { shorter?, noDamage?, landsOnFeet?, source }`, for the item's time. Tumbler's Belt's Land With Grace is 120 feet shorter and on its feet, for the reaction's round. Aerial Cloak's Fall Gently is 30 feet. Jade Cat is 20 feet for a minute. Dragonaut's Wingsuit's Descend is no damage for a minute.
