Since 2026-10-26 the rest of each sentence is in the Effect's words: the cloak's landing in a space of its choice within 20 feet (`glide`) and the Wingsuit's 60 feet a round (`rate`). Jade Cat's "not Off-Guard when you Balance, and narrow surfaces and uneven ground are not difficult terrain" is also a roll note on its Balance (`balance`), as PF2e's own Steady Balance has it.
  - Worn with it always on: `api.fallEases(actor)`, which the environment module asks. Gliding Membranes (25 and 50 feet), Angelic Opera Cloak (no damage), Soft-Landing (10), Daredevil Boots (10, not when its user has Cat Fall), Shawl of Seasons on Fall (10).
  - Falling damage taken off by an amount (2026-10-26, environment f06ece4 and core 0e35ee9): a worn Impact Foam Chassis is its own plan (`builtin: "fall-foam"`, `readFoam`), not a card. `api.fallEases` gives `{ reduceBy, minFeet: 10, itemUuid }` while it's worn: 20, 50, 100 or 200 damage off by grade. After the fall, the environment module's hook `pf2e-automatic-environment-resolution.fell` runs on the GM's client that applied it. The chassis that activated is used up (`foamUsed`): the one whose reduction it was, or, for a fall it took nothing off, the one easing most. Its foam keeps the Hit Points it has left (20, 40, 100 or 200 less what it took) on an Effect around the creature for 1 minute (`foamEffectSource`). That Effect grants PF2e's Immobilized. It gives standard cover against other creatures as PF2e's Effect: Cover has it (roll options, +2 to AC, to Reflex against areas, and to Stealth to Hide or Sneak), and −2 to the creature's own attacks for their cover. It has an Escape DC 15, which the ability module's escape flag ends. Three Interact actions, or damaging the foam (AC 12) to 0, are the GM's: they delete it.
  - As a fall is coming: the environment module's hook `pf2e-automatic-environment-resolution.falling` fires before the GM applies its card. A line whispered to the GMs and the creature's owners names its fall reactions, to use from the sheet's Activate first, and what its worn items already ease.
- **Weather an item ignores, through PF2e Automatic Environment Resolution** (`weather.js`, `scripts/weather.js`, 2026-10-26, its session's contract: core 3315048, environment 823a3be). The keys are the environment module's: sight, fog-mist, precipitation, concealment, wind-ranged (up to `windUpTo`), wind-movement; with `weapon`, only that weapon's Strikes.
  - Timed: Ixamè's Eye's "see clearly through fog and mist for 1 minute" is the item card's Effect, flagged `flags["pf2e-automatic-environment-resolution"].weatherIgnores = { ignores: ["fog-mist"], source }`. The key isn't `weather`, which marks the environment module's own Effects. Whether a creature that hid by the fog alone is still hidden stays the GM's note.
  - Worn or wielded: `api.weatherIgnores(actor)`, which the environment module asks. Cape of the Open Sky worn ignores sight, concealment and wind on its movement; its Gentle Landing as it falls is no falling damage (`api.fallEases`). Wintershot held ignores precipitation, fog and mist, and wind penalties up to −2 for its own Strikes (Heroic: any wind penalty).
- **An activation's own wait before it's used again** (`cooldown.js`, 2026-10-26): "can't be activated again for 1 minute", "You can't Activate the item again for 10 minutes", "can't be activated again for 2d6 hours", "you can't activate it again for 7 days", and the ability compiler's "You can't use this activation again for 1d4 rounds" are the plan's trigger.recharge. Using one keeps on the item when it's usable again, rolled when it's dice, and tells its user. Using it before then warns the GM, as a frequency does; the Activate button's tooltip shows what's left. Retrieval Belt's "Neither Store Item nor Retrieve Item …" is one wait for both. A wait that starts when the effect ends ("Once deactivated") or waits for something other than time stays a note.
- **One willing ally its user chooses** (`chosen-ally.js`, `src/live/hp-floor.js`, `scripts/hp-floor.js`, 2026-10-26, `test/chosen-ally.test.js`): the Bewitching Bloom tattoos' "Choose a willing ally you can see within 30 feet". The item card goes to the ally its presser targets, and each bloom's benefit is an Effect of PF2e's own rules on that ally:
  - Cherry Blossom: the recovery check DC lowered by the dying value for 1 minute, as PF2e's Nine Lives Catfolk does it (DC 10 whatever the dying value).
  - Amaranth: the "hp floor" Effect until the start of its user's next turn. The core's damage listener sees each damage before PF2e applies it. When the damage comes from a hazard or an enemy (PF2e's `origin:enemy`, or no ally's item; not `origin:ally`, an ally's item or its own), PF2e's own Hit Point update can't go below 1 (`preUpdateActor`). Once that damage lowered its Hit Points or temporary Hit Points, the Effect is used up, and the GM and its owners are told whether it held.
  - Bellflower: +2 status bonus to Escape while grabbed or restrained (as PF2e's Escape Fulu), and a roll note on its saves while confused, frightened or paralyzed, until the end of the next turn. The new save or reaction Escape it allows now is a card line.
  - Lilac: +2 status bonus to Recall Knowledge (`action:recall-knowledge`), the Effect gone after that roll; the attempt is a card line.
  - Red Rose: a PF2e toggle, "Beloved within 10 feet", on the Effect for 1 minute. While it's on, the +1 status bonus to saving throws applies and PF2e's TempHP gives 5 at the start of its turn; leftover temporary Hit Points go with the Effect.
  - Magnolia: its PF2e Effect (+1 Diplomacy) on the ally, then the ally's Will save card for later (`plan.again`, the ally its user). Its Fascinated lasts until the end of the ally's next turn. Its "temporarily immune to being fascinated by any magnolia bewitching bloom" is the bloom's own immunity at every result (`immunity.js`): being fascinated is all its save does, so it isn't only one part of it.
- **A talisman used with a free action or 1 action** (`action-ways.js`, 2026-10-26): Golden Liberator's Feather on the ways card. The free action's wings give its user a fly Speed of 50 to the end of its turn (PF2e's BaseSpeed on the item card's Effect), with its +4 AC against reactions to its movement a note Effect; its Fly is its own move. The 1 action's way is those wings, then the buffet's save card (basic Fortitude DC 41, 8d8 spirit) at the adjacent enemies its presser targets.
- **A grade of ammunition loaded** (`gradeWaysPlan` in `permanent.js`, 2026-10-26): Alchemist's Flamethrower's damage "based on the weakest alchemist's fire loaded", its list of four grades. The ways card picks the grade. Each way is that grade's basic Reflex save in its line (30 to 120 feet, DC 15 to 37). A critical failure also takes its persistent fire (1 to 4).
- **Its user's shield raised** (`raise-shield.js`, 2026-10-26): Shield of Snakes' Snap Up, "The shield of snakes moves into position, allowing you to Raise the Shield", is its own card. Its Apply raises its user's held shield with PF2e's own Raise a Shield. Shield of Endurance's Temporary Endurance raises it too (2026-10-26). Its shield's 10 temporary Hit Points for a minute are a note Effect on its user, as PF2e gives a shield none; damage to the shield comes off them by hand.
- **An illusion a creature can see through** (`disbelief.js`, the disbelief card, `scripts/disbelief.js`, 2026-10-25): your ruling for illusions, the spells' rule. Possum's Play's "Any creature that touches the illusion or uses the Seek action to examine you can attempt to disbelieve the illusion. The DC to disbelieve the illusion is 18" is plan.disbelief. Once the item card applies, a card whispered to the GMs follows for the illusion's time (Possum's Play's minute). Its Disbelieve button rolls each targeted creature's Perception (PF2e's own roll) against the item's DC. One that succeeds sees through it: the card names it, and an Effect on it says so for the rest of that time. The card also lists who tried and is still fooled, and what ends the illusion ("Any action requiring you to move or make noise"). Possum's Play's "the triggering attack gains the nonlethal trait" is a note Effect on its user for the round (at 0 Hit Points it's unconscious, not dying, as PF2e's rule for nonlethal damage says).
  - Read sentence by sentence (`illusions.js`, 2026-10-27): an item's illusion whose every sentence is one of the reading's own. Deck of Mischief's Card Trick has the GM's disbelief card at DC 20 for a creature that Seeks or touches the card. It has no time, so the card stays until the GM is done with it, and what is left in the deck and the swap are the card's lines. Camp Shroud's five grades have the card for a creature outside the mist that Seeks or Searches, at DC 18 to 35, for 12 hours, ended when the fire is put out. The mist's size and what it hides are the map's. Veiled Figurehead's Veil! has the ship's new look on its user for 6 hours (a Dismiss removes it) and the card at DC 25 for a creature that boards the ship or Seeks.
  - A DC that is its user's, and a second check (2026-10-27): Wooden Nickel's Perception is "against your Deception DC" (`disbelief.against`). The card reads its user's Deception DC when it's posted. Its Crafting check that "will also reveal the item's true nature" is the card's second check (`disbelief.also`, with `through`: a success sees through it too; a fitting Lore is the GM's to roll by hand). Misdirecting Haversack's Society DC 20 to Decipher Writing is a second check that reads the real text without seeing through the disguise. The GM picks the check on the card before rolling. Wooden Nickel's "Prerequisites You are a master in Deception" is now read as a requirement (`requirement.js`): the GM is whispered when its user lacks it.
  - Disguises that aren't the item's illusion (2026-10-27): "disguise" in these items' words leaves nothing to disbelieve. Potion of Disguise keeps its +4 to Deception DC; its reminder becomes the disguise's words (the bonus only against seeing through it, its level added even if untrained, each grade's size). Both last the 24 hours that the GM's secret 2d12 can reach, for the GM to end sooner. Emergency Disguise's −2 circumstance penalty to Impersonate with it is a modifier with PF2e's predicates (`action:impersonate`, not `feat:quick-disguise`) for 24 hours, beside its words. Tentacle Potion (Lesser, Moderate) is the ways card, used once. Its limb is words on the drinker for its time. A fortified tail is the second way, as the next better potion: the moderate's for the lesser, and for the moderate the greater's, with its tentacle Strike. Glasses of Sociability's name and Undead Detection Dye's colors are whispered, the dye's DC 20 secret check the GM's. Trustworthy Round's called target is the card's lines. Herd Mask's swap is a one-off. Clockwork Disguise, Hand-Hewed Face and Foe-Sensing Rod are checked as having nothing to play.
  - Seeing through illusions, and illusions seen for what they are (2026-10-27): Clarity Goggles' secret counteract is the GM's roll, as Scope of Truth's is. Its words go on its user for the 10 minutes, with its Perception as the modifier and half the goggles' level as the rank (the PFS note). Arclord Eye's magic sense is PF2e's `magicsense` Sense rule (imprecise, 30 feet, 10 minutes); what it learns of each effect is the GM's, in words beside it. Dust of Appearance coats each creature its presser targets in the burst for a minute, in words, since PF2e has no rule that keeps a creature visible. Sun Sight's counteract at what its rays hit is a one-off beside its cast. Talespinner's Lyre's memory plays on its user while Sustained, up to a minute. Secret-Keeper's Mask's stolen-item copy, Pocket Stage's and Pocket Gala's structures, and Illusory Backdrop's scene are the card's lines, the map's and the GM's. The backdrop also gets the GM's disbelief card at its level's DC (27): the item gives none, and the card says the GM may change it. Illusory Program is checked as flavor.
  - Disguise spells played as their Effects (2026-10-27): Tales in Timber's Statue Disguise puts Illusory Disguise's rules (PF2e's Spell Effect: +4 status and its level as proficiency to Impersonate) on one note Effect for the hour. Oaken Resilience's resistance and weakness at the armor's rank (2, 4, 6) go on a second, which its user can Dismiss after fire damage. Speak with Plants and the standing-still +2 are in its words. Veiled Figurehead (Greater) puts the same Illusory Disguise rules on everyone aboard that its presser targets, in place of a cast (the spell module casts it on its caster alone), for the ship's 24 hours. The GM's disbelief card (DC 33) covers the whole illusion. Dreamer's Butterfly's +1 status to Identify Magic for a round goes on each onlooker targeted, its user too; the dream-or-sleep limit is in its words. Aeon Stone (Western Star)'s and Masquerade Scarf (Greater)'s Illusory Disguise casts keep their cast, and what the disguise also hides or what ends it becomes the cast card's lines.

It's PF2e's EphemeralEffect of Off-Guard on its user's attacks and their damage, not against a concealed or hidden creature. Whom its user can see is the table's.
- **Difficult terrain ignored** (`ruleEffectsOf` in `rows.js`, 2026-10-25): "you ignore … difficult terrain" (Winterstep Elixir's from ice and snow, Underbrush Cloak's from plants and fungi, Frostwalker Pattern's difficult and greater from ice and snow until the end of the next turn) is PF2e's own rule on the item card's Effect for its time. It's an ActiveEffectLike `add` to `system.movement.terrain.difficult.ignored`, and `.greater.ignored` when named, with `{ environment: "all", feature }`: PF2e's features ice and snow, "undergrowth" for plants and fungi, "all" when none is named. PF2e's own movement cost honors the "all" entry and reduces greater difficult terrain to difficult. The named features are for the environment module's regions (asked of its session).
  - Levitating over it (2026-10-26): Three Bird Pinion's "levitate 1 foot off the ground for 1 minute … you ignore most difficult, greater difficult, and hazardous terrain on the ground". PF2e's rule covers both kinds of difficult terrain, any feature, for the minute. The hazardous terrain and what "most" leaves out are in the Effect's words.
- **Its user's own moves with a Speed for them** (`move-actions.js`, 2026-10-26): Dragonaut's Wingsuit's Drake's Glide, "You take 2 move actions, each of which can be a Fly or Stride. The moves are its user's own on the map, a line of the card.
- **Heat and cold** (`temperature.js`, `scripts/temperature.js`, 2026-10-25, the user's: items that interact with temperature work through PF2e Automatic Environment Resolution). The text's protection becomes the environment module's shape, agreed with its session: `counts`, from the temperature a creature feels (the core's TEMPERATURES keys) to the one it counts as, and `fatigueHours`.
  - "Protected from" or "immune to" levels of a side: each counts as mild heat or cold, or as normal when mild is among them (Environmental Endurance's own reading). Protection from extreme cold covers severe too. Steps down are read as written: Warming Parka's negated severe cold and extreme as severe, Rime Jar's "treat extreme cold as severe cold", Nightbreeze Machine's, Heavenly Rolling Flames' "one degree lower", Volcanic Vigor (Greater)'s incredible heat as severe. Navaratna of the Solar Ruby and The Desert ignore it all.

Elixir of the Peaks' "while in mountainous terrain" is a line on the card. Slithermaw's Bane's "heat or cold (your choice)" is a ways card, and its other sentences stay notes.
  - A worn, held or invested item (Shade Hat, Sihedron Ring, Everburning Coal held, Warming Parka, Traveler's Cloak's 2 hours, which don't apply at extreme or incredible temperatures): `api.temperatureProtections(actor)`, synchronous, which the environment module calls. It also reads Shawl of Seasons' season picked on its sheet, and the aura of an ally's Banner of the Restful within its radius.
  - The environment module (0.1.2) and the core (0.7.5) take the mildest of gear, Endurance and each protection; protections don't chain, and the largest fatigue bonus counts. Without them, the Effect is only a note on the sheet.
  - Not read: Glowing Lantern Fruit's planted fruit (an aura around a square), auras of shelter (Palanquin of Night, Restful Tent), and items that change a region's temperature (Aroden's Hearthstone, Primordial Flame).
- **A retaliation beside an activation's own result** (`retaliationBeside` in `direct-damage.js`, plan.alsoRetaliation): Thunder Mail's Call Down Lightning deals its 6d6 electricity around its user, "Additionally, for the next minute, whenever a creature touches you or hits you with a melee unarmed attack or a non-reach melee weapon attack, it takes 1d8 electricity damage". The retaliation is read as Numerian Steel Breastplate's is (a non-reach melee weapon's hit comes from an adjacent creature). Its later card is posted beside the activation's, kept open for the minute.
- **A counteract said again**: Panacea Fruit's "The fruit has a counteract rank of 9 and a 1d20+30 modifier for the rolls against each condition" only repeats its counteract card's rank and modifier, so it isn't left over (`restatesCounteract`, used by the honesty pass too).
- **A creature moved to a space its user picks** (`src/compiler/placed.js`, `scripts/placed-moves.js`, 2026-10-25): Roc-Shaft Arrow's "save or be moved to a space you choose within 10 feet (20 feet on a critical failure)", Whip-Tongue Sling's Tongue Twister ("On a success, you can move the creature up to 15 feet into any space within 20 feet of you"). The ability module's card's plan carries placed: the degrees that move the creature and how far. Once that card is applied (the ability module's "applied" hook, ability 0.9.2), the teleport card is posted for each creature whose degree has a row. The creature is the one moved and its user the one who presses Pick: the space must be within that distance of where the creature stood, and within nearSource feet of its user (Whip-Tongue Sling's 20). The token moves at once, flagged as the core's forced movement. Roc-Shaft's "If this would move the target into a hazardous space, this effect gains the incapacitation trait" is a line on that card. Juxtaposition Ammunition's failed save teleports the creature instead ("a safe, unoccupied square of your choice within 60 feet of its original position and within the weapon's first range increment"). Its nearSource is the range increment of the weapon that fired it, filled in on the hit, and its card says "Teleport". Its "On a critical failure, the target is also Sickened 1" is the critical failure's row. "It doesn't deal the Strike's normal damage" is strike.noDamage, a line on the hit's message telling its user not to roll the damage. With an older ability module, the plan is as read before (`withoutPlacedMoves`): a note and, for Roc-Shaft, the core's straight push.
- **Extra Strike damage told as something the weapon fires** (`withItemData`): Blade of Fallen Stars' "On your next ranged Strike, the weapon fires … In addition to the normal Strike damage, the projectile also deals 6d8 cold damage" is the next ranged Strike's extra damage, with its Faerie Fire a note. Solar Shellflower's construct, "For the next 1 minute, whenever you Strike an enemy … dealing 3d4 persistent fire … or twice that on a critical hit", is its Strikes' extra damage for that minute (PF2e doubles extra dice on a critical hit).
- **Healing gated on what the item holds** (`withItemData`): Essence Forge's "If the essence value in the forge is … at least 50 gp, the forge heals you for …" heals its user, with the gate a note.
- **An activation ends at a section heading** (`splitActivations`): text after an `<h2>` (Essence Forge's crafting table, Devil's Trident's disease) isn't the last activation's. A Strike that casts a spell on its hit wins over a plain cast of that spell (`strikeOver`).
- **Notes without the Activate line** (`ownRow`, `compileEach`): a sentence that begins with the activation's own line ("Activate—Absorb Essence 1 (…) Requirements … Effect …", or just its name and traits) loses that line. The note then starts at what the activation does (16 activations' notes, such as Darkvision Scope, Chromatic Robe and Essence Forge).
- **Notes without the item's crafting or Destruction line** (`src/compiler/notes.js` cleanNotes, used for consumables, activations and Strike notes): "Craft Requirements …" is cut from a note, and an artifact's "Destruction …" note is dropped (33 plans).
- **A square that harms only some creatures** (`exhaledSquarePlan` in `src/compiler/covered-square.js`, the ability module's `only: { traits }`, ability b4ad3ce): "fill that square with soothing vapors that harm any fiend, fey, ghost, spirit, or undead creature that ends their turn in that square for the next minute, causing them to take … damage (… basic save)". The zone's save at a turn's end applies only to a creature with one of those traits. "An incorporeal creature that takes this damage must also attempt" a second save, for an incorporeal one only (Sanctified Beans). Not done: Anathema Fulu, whose area is wherever its four pieces are placed.
- **A chime placed within reach that troubles only some creatures** (`placedChimePlan` in `src/compiler/covered-square.js`): "place it on the ground in a square within your reach … an area in a 10-foot emanation. Creatures with the fey, spirit, or undead traits must attempt a … save when they enter the affected area and at the beginning of every turn". The zone is placed within reach for its 10 minutes (the emanation as a burst the GM places). Its save on entering and at a turn's start applies only to creatures with those traits, and a failure's difficult terrain is a note Effect until that creature's next turn (Aetheric Irritant, all four grades). Before, the ability compiler's reading put it on its user, with difficult terrain for everyone and no save.
- **An aura that harms some creatures and heals the rest** (`readHealingAura` in `src/compiler/aura-zones.js`): "Living creatures that start their turn in the area regain 1d8 Hit Points, and any fiend or undead creature that starts its turn in the area takes … damage (… save)", in an emanation around its user, Sustained up to 1 minute. It's the zone on its user's token for that minute. The damage, or its basic save, comes at a turn's start only for creatures with those traits (`zone.damage[i].only` / `zone.saves[i].only`). The healing of the living is a note (Soulcutter's Soothe Souls, both grades).
- **A save whose failure is the GM's to carry out** (`failureNotesPlan` in `src/compiler/save-or.js`, after the other save readers): "The triggering creature must attempt a … save. On a failure, …" or "If that creature fails a … save, …", where the failure is nothing a row holds. The save is rolled at the creature the presser targets, and the failure's and critical failure's words go on the card for the GM (Cauthooj Bagpipes' Throw Song redirecting the Strike, Planar Ribbon's swap of places). A sentence about the activation's own trait isn't one of them.
- **Determination's benefit** (`readCounteract` in `src/compiler/counteract.js`, the counteract card): Flame Navette's "gain the benefit of the fighter's Determination class feat, with a counteract rank of 8 and a counteract modifier of +22". The card is its user's alone. It lists their conditions, any of which ends outright with no roll and no numbers, and their spell or magical Effects (a spell's, or one with the magical trait or a tradition's), each counteracted at +22, rank 8. With the feat, its own modifier is a note.
- **A counteract's degrees** (`readCounteract`, the triggering effect): Herald's Ring's "attempting to counteract the effect. The ring has a counteract modifier of +23" is reworded so the modifier is in the counteract's sentence (`withItemData`). Its Critical Success … Critical Failure lines (how much sonic damage is reflected) become notes for the GM beside the result the card reports.
- **A feat's Strike in the item's words** (`withItemData`, `src/compiler/strike-damage.js`, `src/live/strike-card.js`, `scripts/strikes.js`):
  - Iron Cudgel's "you use Brutal Finish, as the fighter feat" becomes a melee Strike with one more weapon damage die (two at 18th level, by its owner's level), as PF2e's own feat rule has it: a DamageDice with no die size, which PF2e gives the weapon's. Only weapon dice may go without a size.
  - "On a failure, you deal damage equal to one weapon damage die" is strike.failDice. A failed attack spends the readied Strike and posts that many of the attacking weapon's dice, of its damage type, at the creature missed (the ability module's card, as Big Rock Bullet's failure).
  - Energizing Lattice's half on a failure ("the target still takes half the force damage" when the Strike fails without a critical failure) is strike.failHalf: the extra dice of that type. A failed attack spends the readied Strike and posts those dice at half (multiplier 0.5) on the ability module's card at the creature missed. Its sentence about the glow ending is the requirement's, which is the user's to meet.
  - The turn ending and the feat's own extra die are notes.
- **Warning Shot** (`withItemData`, the item-action card): Admonishing Band's shot "with the effects of Warning Shot" is PF2e's Demoralize. Its range (the firearm's maximum), the missing language penalty and the feat's immunity rule are notes.
- **A bonus handed to the triggering ally** (`ownRow`, `src/compiler/consumables.js` OTHERS_GRANT): Admirer's Bouquet's "they gain a +1 item bonus to saves against emotion and mental effects as long as they continue to carry" becomes the row for the creature the presser targets (the triggering ally), with no end. Its end when the bouquet is put down, and the new save it allows, are notes.
- **An activation that takes minutes isn't the item's standing bonus** (`compileActivations` in `src/compiler/permanent.js`): an activation that takes 10 minutes or more reaches the ability compiler as a passive. It used to read the item's own PF2e modifiers (which PF2e applies itself) as the activation's save bonus. When that happens, the activation is read again without the item's modifiers, its toggles kept. Crown of the Kobold King's second activation now casts Nightmare, and Serithtial's casts a 9th-rank Heal or Ring of Truth (DC 45).
- **One of the ways of a toggle named for the activation** (`choiceOptionPlan` in `src/compiler/option-on.js`, the toggle card): Shawl of Seasons' Change of Seasons and its RollOption "shawl-of-seasons:change-of-seasons", one way per season. The toggle card switches PF2e's toggle to the season picked, so PF2e gives Spring's +5-foot Speed. The item's other toggle that waits on this one (its current season, +1 to Fortitude) is a note for the sheet. It replaces any row read from the activation's words, since the toggle is PF2e's own implementation.
- **Fast healing for the rage** (`readUntilRage` in `src/compiler/rage-time.js`, plan.item.untilRage.fastHealing): an item whose activations need its user to be able to Rage, and whose activation gives "fast healing equal to half your level" with no end of its own (a Barbarians+ crown). It joins the until-the-rage-ends Effect as PF2e's FastHealing rule, at half the user's level when Apply is pressed (at least 1), and only while raging.
- **A roll rolled twice, named in a clause before it** (`readRollTwice` in `src/compiler/fortune.js`): "when you make an attack roll against …, you can roll twice and take the better result" readies the user's next attack roll (or the save, skill check or Perception check it names) with PF2e's RollTwice, as the other fortune items do (a Barbarians+ crown's once during the rage). Its other sentences are notes.
- **A bonus's trait limit on the card** (`modifierText` in `src/live/card.js`): a modifier predicated on item:trait:… (alone or in an "or") reads "against emotion or mental effects" (Admirer's Bouquet).
- **No opening flavor left for the GM** (`withoutFlavorNotes` in `src/compiler/flavor.js`, on activations and consumables): a partial plan whose notes, unread sentences or row words include its Effect's first sentence, when that sentence is flavor (no link, no number, no rule word, as `withoutFlavor` decides), loses it. The plan is no longer partial when that was all it left (Tentacle Cannon's Unrelenting Grasp, Bloodgorger Scythe's Sapling Slash, a third-party blade's critical-hit reaction). A plan that isn't partial (the GM's card, a builtin) keeps its words whole, since its first line may be all it says.
- **A spell slot an activation costs** (`src/compiler/slot-cost.js`, plan.spend.spellSlot; `scripts/spend.js` slotOf, checkSpend, paySpend): a cost line asking for a spell slot of some lowest rank (Magic+ scepters).
  - When the effect is the same whatever the slot, it's spellSlot { min }. Apply spends the lowest slot its user has left at that rank or above: a prepared entry's first prepared spell not yet expended, or one of a spontaneous (or flexible) entry's slots. With none left, the card refuses and applies nothing. The card says what it costs, and the chat line names the slot and entry spent.
  - When the numbers are the slot's rank (healing dice for each rank, an aura or effect lasting a round for each rank, a counteract at the slot's rank), it's the ways card. There's one way per rank from the lowest to 10th, each compiled in that rank's words and each spending a slot of exactly that rank. A rank's way may be a timed reminder only (Rotting Aura's rounds, Height of Your Power's).
  - Healing for every creature in a cone (or a burst, emanation or line) heals each creature the presser targets in that area (`textHealing`, OTHERS_GRANT): Purifying Wave's Nd8, its counteract a note.
  - A statblock whose effect has no Effect heading gets one in its rank's words, so the Frequency line doesn't swallow the effect's first sentence.
- **Header lines that end at their paragraph** (`ownRow` in `src/compiler/rows.js`): a Frequency, Trigger, Requirements or Cost line with no closing period ("once per day;") gets one where its paragraph ends. A block with no Effect heading (a Magic+ statblock) no longer loses its effect's first sentence along with that line.
- **A cloud a bomb leaves around its target** (`src/compiler/bomb-cloud.js`, plan.item.cloud; the bomb card): Silver Orb's filings that "linger airborne in a 5-foot burst around the target" for 1 minute. The cloud is the ability module's zone, a burst set down at the target's square (it doesn't follow the target). Those inside are Concealed, and each creature ending its turn in it attempts the basic save against its damage, whose "silver source" is the damage's material. It forms on any degree but a critical miss: a hit's damage card lists it, and a plain miss (PF2e rolls no damage for a bomb without splash) gets the bomb card from the attack roll (`cloudOnMiss`). Apply sets the zone down. Its breathing-only limit and what the filings do to the creature hit are notes.
- **A weapon its user treats as their best** (`src/compiler/proficiency-loan.js`, plan.item.proficiency): Blade Phantom's Guide's "For 1 minute, you treat your proficiency with that weapon as equal to your highest weapon proficiency", for a weapon no higher than its level. The item card picks one of its user's weapons (only those up to that level), and Apply gives an Effect with PF2e's MartialProficiency for that weapon (by its id) at the user's highest weapon proficiency rank, for its time.
- **An activation's retaliation** (`retaliationPlan` in `src/compiler/direct-damage.js`, now also for activations): "For 1 minute, adjacent creatures that hit you with a melee Strike, as well as creatures that touch you or hit you with an unarmed attack, take 1d10 electricity damage per hit" (Numerian Steel Breastplate). The card is kept open for its time, as a retaliation potion's: each time, target the creature and press. Instinct Crown (Dragon)'s lasts "until the end of your rage" (a minute at most, its end a note), and its type, its dragon's breath, is the card's picker.
- **A shield bash's save on a hit, and "As failure, and …" rows** (`STRIKE` in `src/compiler/strikes.js`, `withItemData`, `asRowCopies` in `src/compiler/guards.js`, `bombSave`):
  - Jawbreaker Shield's reaction "make a shield bash Strike" is a readied Strike. Its "If the shield bash hits, … must attempt a DC 31 Fortitude saving throw" is the hit's save card, with its 1d6 bleed on a failure.
  - A bomb's or a Strike's save now accepts rows that only deal damage.
  - A row's "As failure, and …" (or "but") is read after the row's own first sentence too. It copies the failure's damage when the row names none, so Jawbreaker's critical failure bleeds as its failure does.
  - The row copier also runs on bomb and Strike saves, so Trollhound Pick's "As failure, but … Enfeebled 2" keeps the failure's minute. The other guards don't run there: they would give Durian Bomb's Sickened the Dazzled's round.
- **A rune's bonus as its weapon's potency** (`withWeaponPotency` in `src/compiler/runes.js`): Cunning's Recall Knowledge "gaining an item bonus … equal to the weapon's item bonus to attack rolls from its potency rune", compiled for the weapon it's etched onto, is PF2e's Recall Knowledge with that +N item bonus (the item-action card). The critical hit's +2 circumstance bonus is a note.
- **A no-damage attack whose degrees are PF2e's own Effects on the target** (`src/compiler/effect-strike.js`): Doom Switch's Bragging Rights, "treated as a simple melee weapon for the purpose of proficiency. This attack deals no damage". The card picks its user's simple melee attack (Strength and simple proficiency; a creature without proficiencies uses its best melee Strike). The ability module's card rolls it against AC and puts PF2e's linked "(Success or Better)" or "(Failure or Worse)" Effect on the target, whose degree choice the ability module presets. The temporary Hit Points when the target drops are notes.
- **The roll its user then makes with PF2e** (plan.then, the card's "Then:" line): Gallows Tooth's "make a melee Strike against an adjacent creature. That creature is Off-Guard against the Strike and until the end of your turn" puts the Off-Guard on the creature targeted, and the Strike is its user's own. Mesmerizing Opal's "attempt a Deception check to Feint", with the degree it improves readied for that Feint, works the same way. The card says what to roll; neither sentence is a note.
- **A miss that gives the activation's use back** (strike.missRefund, `scripts/strikes.js` refundUse): Hydra Head Club's "If your Strike misses, the activation does not count against the hydra head club's activation frequency". When the readied Strike misses, one use comes off the item's record for that activation (`scripts/activations.js`), so its next use isn't over the frequency. A hit keeps it used.
- **A sentence that says more after the amount it heals is kept** (`amountTempOf` in `rows.js`): Grisly Scythe's "You regain a number of hit points equal to half the void damage inflicted, and the creature struck becomes Sickened 1 from the agony" stays a note, so its Sickened isn't lost.
- **A higher resistance against some sources, for the readied bonus** (bonus.resistanceAlso): Warding Tattoo (Fiend)'s and (Trail)'s "Your resistance to damage from fiends and magical attacks by fiends becomes resistance 5" (hazardous terrain and environmental hazards for the Trail tattoo). The readied Effect gets a second Resistance rule beside its resistance 2, predicated as PF2e's own rules for the tattoo are: origin:trait:fiend, or origin:trait:environmental or terrain.
- **Energy Ointment's lower flat check DC** (`chosenResistance` in `rows.js`, the resistance Effect): "… grants resistance 10 to the attuned energy type and reduces the DC for the flat check to end persistent damage of the attuned energy type by 3". The type picked on the card gets the resistance, and the same Effect carries PF2e's ItemAlteration on persistent-damage conditions of that type (pd-recovery-dc, subtract 3), as PF2e's own Horned Lion Amulet Effect does. "can be applied to up to 5 willing creatures" is its targeting (each creature targeted), not a note.
- **Twisting Twine's toss** (`readOwnCheck` in `own-checks.js`): "You toss the ball of twine into a square within 20 feet" is the maneuver's range (20 feet, at the creature in that square). "At the end of your turn, the twine winds itself back into a ball and returns to your hand" is the item finding its way back, with no rule in it.
- **More reach for the Strike** (strike.reach, the strike card's line): the Extending rune's "You Strike with the weapon, and you have reach 60 feet for the Strike" (120 feet for the greater rune) and Tasset of Flexibility's "Make a Strike with a melee weapon, increasing your reach by 5 feet for that Strike". The readied Strike carries the reach as its line. The Strike is PF2e's own at a creature that far, and PF2e doesn't measure melee reach. Tasset's "with a melee weapon" makes it a melee Strike.
- **Another of its activations recharged** (item.recharges, `rechargeOf` in `permanent.js`): Smoldering Armor's "The fire shield activation recharges" names its other activation ("You gain the effects of Fire Shield"). Apply takes that activation's uses off the item's record, so it can be used again today.
- **A penalty its nonlethal attacks don't take** (`withNonlethalExempt` in `permanent.js`): Pacifying's "A pacified creature takes a –2 penalty to attack rolls on any attacks that aren't nonlethal for 1 minute". The failure's –2 to attack rolls is predicated on the attack not being nonlethal (PF2e's item:trait:nonlethal), so the creature's nonlethal attacks don't take it.
- **PF2e's own rules on its user, with no reading of their own** (item.ruleEffects, `ruleEffectsOf` in `rows.js`, the item card's Effect): Mirrorblade's "For the next 10 minutes, you aren't Off-Guard to melee attacks from creatures flanking you" is PF2e's flanking.offGuardable set to false, as PF2e's own Deny Advantage sets it, on an Effect for the 10 minutes. Sturdy Neck Stock's "For 1 minute, you aren't off-guard to Hidden, undetected, or flanking creatures, or creatures using surprise attack of your level or lower" is Deny Advantage's own wording, so it gets PF2e's rule for it: offGuardable set to its user's level. Copper Penny's "you Stand and can't be knocked prone again on the current turn" is PF2e's Immunity to prone on its user to the end of the turn, beside the Stand (the prone it ends).
- **Only after its user's action succeeds** (plan.gate, `withActionGate` in `permanent.js`, `scripts/poisons.js`): Glamorous Buckler's "On a successful Feint, the target is Dazzled for 1 round", its trigger its user's Feint. Apply looks at its user's latest roll of that action in the recent chat. A failure is refused with a word, and a success puts the Dazzled on the creature targeted.
- **A fortune with a time of its own** (rollTwice.duration): Aerekostes's next attack rolled twice "before the end of your next turn". The RollTwice Effect lasts 1 round, to the end of the next turn, instead of the minute a fortune otherwise waits for its roll.
- **A miss's own words, no rule**: Deflecting Branch's "If the attack misses, you have knocked it away" names the state its other activation's trigger refers to. It isn't a note.
- **A higher bonus against one kind of attacker, its user's pick** (`readiedBonusPlan`): Broken Tusk Pendant's "You gain a +1 item bonus to AC against the triggering attack, or a +2 item bonus if the animal is the specific kind depicted by the pendant". The ways card has two buttons, +1 and +2 for the pendant's animal. Each is the readied bonus's card against that attack. Its user knows which animal the pendant depicts.
- **A higher bonus at a kind of creature** (`readiedBonusPlan`): Conrasu Coin (Arbiter)'s "+2 circumstance bonus on the Diplomacy check, which increases to a +3 circumstance bonus to Make an Impression on a creature with the aeon trait". A second modifier of the same type is predicated on the target's trait (target:trait:aeon), and PF2e keeps the higher circumstance bonus.
- **PF2e's own rule said again**: Sniper's Bead (Major)'s "As normal, you still can't hit a target more than six range increments away" isn't a note.
- **A thrown weapon coming back**: Dragonbone Arrowhead's "…, and when you make a thrown Strike with it, it flies back to your hand after the Strike completes" is the weapon's way home after the thrown 20 feet trait it gains. The weapon stays in its user's inventory, so there's no rule to apply and it isn't a note.
- **A critical hit's penalty to the save** (strike.criticalSaveBonus): Blindpepper Bolt's "If the Strike was a critical success, the target takes a -2 circumstance penalty to its Fortitude save". After a critical hit, the save card carries the core's own save bonus (check.bonus, –2 circumstance). After a hit the save is as written.
- **What a save near something burning leaves unread** (`withBurningLeft` in `index.js`): Devil's Breath Incense was counted resolved as a single save at the drinker. Its burning for 10 minutes, the creatures who come within 10 feet later, the hour of immunity after the save and its other use (an adjacent creature inhaling it to carry off a curse) are now notes, and the plan is partial. Temporary immunities in general aren't tracked yet (question 18 in the decisions file).
- **A temporary immunity after a use** (`src/compiler/immunity.js`, `scripts/immunity.js`; the user's answer to question 18, 2026-10-20). Examples: Serene Smelling Salts' "temporarily immune to any serene smelling salts for 1 hour", Aetheric Irritant's "A creature that critically succeeds at the save is immune to all aetheric irritants for 24 hours", Fortune's Coin's "Either way, you're temporarily immune to fortune's coin for 1 hour". About 90 PF2e activations say something like this.
  - Its reading has a key, a time and the results that give it. The key is the item's name without its grade (every grade shares one; a kind such as Spun Cloud (Red) stays its own), the base name for "all" or "any" of them, or an ability it names ("immune to Guarded Fortune"). The time is "for 24 hours", "for the next 24 hours", or "until … daily preparations" (a day). The results come from a degree heading, "critically succeeds", "succeeds" or "regardless"; otherwise every result gives it.
  - Saves and zones: every save plan in the item's plan gets the ability module's own `trigger.immunity` (with the key as its slug). The ability module's save cards give the creature its "Immune:" Effect on Apply, by degree. Its postCard leaves out a creature already immune, so zones (Aetheric Irritant, Spun Cloud, Burglar's Rosebud), Strike saves and the expose card's saves hold to it too (ability 7fc93f5).
  - The item module's own cards (the item card, a cast, a fortune, a reroll, a ways card, a counteract, an item action): plan.item.immunity, only when every result gives it. Before Apply, a creature it would go to that's still immune is refused, or left out when others are targeted. After Apply, each creature it went to gets the same "Immune:" Effect, built with the ability module's immunityEffect and flagged under its id, so both modules see one convention. An expired Effect doesn't count.
  - The same immunity said again in another degree's paragraph joins the first (Pheromone Flare: its critical success, and its failure's "After this, it becomes temporarily immune for 24 hours" with the critical failure that's "As failure"). An immunity to another creature's attempts to end it isn't one to the item (Mindrender Baton): the ability compiler's own reading of it is taken off, and the sentence is a note.
  - An immunity to one part of it (2026-10-27, the ability module's `trigger.partImmunity`, 8ee1e7e): Poison Fizz's "temporarily immune to being blinded by poison fizz for 1 hour" and Durian Bomb's "immune to the sickened condition caused by other durian bombs for 1 minute" go on each save that gives that condition. The condition, its time, the item's key, "all" (from any of them) and the degrees the sentence names (Poison Fizz: a critical failure) are kept. On Apply, a creature already immune gets its row without that condition (its damage and the rest as rolled). One the card gives the condition gets an "Immune: Blinded (…)" Effect for the time. Bombs' saves get it too (`bombs.js` bombSave).
  - Not held to, so a note and the item partial: one with no clock ("once its effects end", "until it wakes up", "1d4 days", "until the next lunar new year"), and one for creatures in an area when the card has no save (Nightmare's Lament's smoke). Not an immunity to the item at all, and left alone: a benefit ("you become immune to fear effects"), the weather, and PF2e's own Demoralize or Battle Medicine immunities.
- **Several Strikes in one activation** (`src/compiler/volley.js`, 2026-10-20). Examples: Liar's Gun's "Make up to four Strikes", Caress of the Great Serpent's eight Strikes with a −2 penalty and 1d6 piercing to its user for each one made, Splithead Bow's two arrows at separate targets, Spike Launcher's "Make a Strike … against each creature in the area", Shield of the Unified Legion's shield bash "against each enemy within the area". The Strike card readies that many Strikes, `strike.volley`, with the text's own sentences as its lines: at whom, how far, the area, the penalty, the user's damage, and that the multiple attack penalty rises only after the last.
  - Against each creature in an area, Apply needs the creatures targeted and readies one Strike at each of them; with none targeted, it's refused.
  - On each attack roll: a Strike at a creature already struck (when each must be a different one), or at a creature not targeted for an area's volley, isn't one of them and spends nothing. Each one made deals the user its damage (Caress).
  - The results in the hit's words for the bombs' reader: Spike Launcher's −5-foot Speed (Immobilized on a critical hit), until someone takes the spike out; the shield's force damage, its push 10 feet, and Prone on a critical hit.
  - PF2e's multiple attack penalty is the user's to pick on PF2e's own buttons, so "the penalty doesn't increase until after" is a line, not a note.
  - Notes, the plan partial: Coldstar Pistols' one attack roll for both Strikes, the Splithead Bow's healing next turn, Minotaur Chair's bonuses against a prone foe. Not read: one roll against several creatures' defenses (Hippopotamus Klar, the Five-Headed Arrow).
- **What a Strike's text says of the multiple attack penalty** (`strike.map`, src/compiler/strike-damage.js, 2026-10-20): Piston Gauntlets' "This counts as two attacks when calculating your multiple attack penalty", Crimson Fulcrum Lens' "doesn't count toward your multiple attack penalty", Iron Cube's "the penalty doesn't increase until after you've made both of them". PF2e's penalty is the user's to pick on its own buttons, so each is the card's line ("double", "none", "held"), not a note. Also the lens's "(or 2d8 precision damage if you make a jaws Strike)": that die on a jaws Strike (PF2e's `item:slug:jaws`), the 1d8 on another.
- **Something burning that creatures near it save against** (`burningZone`, src/compiler/index.js, 2026-10-20): Devil's Breath Incense's 10 minutes of burning, its Fortitude save "after which they're temporarily immune to the incense for 1 hour". It's the ability module's zone, a 10-foot area the GM places next to its user, its save at a creature in it as it's lit, coming in or starting a turn there. The immunity (above) keeps that to one save each. Its other use, inhaling the stick, is a note. And a lit candle's zone with no time of its own takes its burning time (Thawing Candle's "Once lit, the candle burns for 10 minutes": before, its zone never ended).
- **A lit candle's or incense's area while it burns** (`src/compiler/lit-area.js`, 2026-10-20; ability c4b48ed). Candle of Inflamed Passions' −1 status to saving throws against emotion effects for creatures in the area, Candle of Truth's −4 status to Lie and its Will save "when first entering … (including you)", Incense of Distilled Death's fast healing 4 for undead in the area. It's the ability module's zone, around its user for a candle (carried, as Thawing Candle's) or placed beside it for incense, for as long as it burns.
  - Its penalties carry PF2e's own predicates (`zone.modifiers[i].predicate`: "item:trait:emotion", "action:lie").
  - Its rules for the creatures with a trait go in `zone.inside`.
  - Its save is its maker's too (`zone.saves[i].own`).
  - What a failed Candle of Truth save does (no deliberate lies while within 10 feet) is the GM's line, and that it's only on first entering is a note.
- **A rider only for a creature a material gets past** (`plan.ifTarget`, src/compiler/materials.js `withMaterialRider`, src/live/if-target.js; 2026-10-20). Moonlit Spellgun's critical success: "If it has a weakness to silver or a resistance that can be bypassed by silver, it's also Enfeebled 1 for 1 minute". The attack card posts the ability module's card with that rider to the creatures it fits (a weakness to the material, or a resistance with it among its exceptions), and the plain plan to the rest.
- **A second dose in a day** (`plan.dose`, src/compiler/consumables.js, scripts/live.js; 2026-10-20): Iron Wine's "Drinking more than one cup of iron wine in a single day gives you weakness 5 to fire until your next daily preparations", and Rainbow Vinegar's (every grade one key). The item card counts each dose on its drinker (`flags.pf2e-automatic-item-resolution.doses`), and on one within the last day of world time it also gives PF2e's own Weakness Effect, whose own time runs to the next daily preparations.
- **A Bane rune of one type** (src/compiler/rune-loans.js, src/live/card.js `runeEffectSource`; 2026-10-20). Bane Oil gives "the weapon the benefit of one type of Bane rune for 1 minute". The rune loan's picker offers the rune's 13 types (aberration … ooze, or fungus and plant). PF2e's own Bane rune has no rules, so its Effect is PF2e's DamageDice: 1d6 of the weapon's own damage type on that weapon's damage, against a creature with the type's traits. A new coat replaces the weapon's last.
- **Retaliation while a resistance lasts, for creatures of one trait** (`plan.item.retaliate`, src/compiler/direct-damage.js `withRetaliateAfter`; 2026-10-20, a third-party salve's damage at an ooze that hits its wearer with an unarmed Strike). After the item card's Apply, a "later" attack card is posted for the creature it gave the resistance to and stays open for that time. Its hint says whom it's for (`retaliation.who`), and its Apply refuses a creature without the trait (`retaliation.only`).
- **Resolved only when the card does all of it** (`src/compiler/honest.js`, 2026-10-20, from an audit of the resolved plans). A result row whose GM line the GM must still carry out ("the rest by hand", "The target can't use reactions until the end of your turn", Gearbinder Oil's slowed lowered) makes the plan partial. These don't:
  - a line that does nothing ("The creature is unaffected", "takes half damage and no other effects");
  - a card line (Hauling's direction);
  - a behaviour rule beside something the row really applies (the user's rule for spells, 2026-09-26);
  - a note Effect that carries what can't be automated (the user's note-Effect rule).

  - A behaviour rule or the GM's call with its own time, and no numbers or conditions, becomes a note Effect on the creature for that time instead of a GM line (Floppy Rag Doll's "can't use reactions until the end your turn"). Its time is the line's own, else the item's.

  - A rule sentence the plan has no trace of (`withUntracedRules`) is a note, and the plan partial. That's one with dice, a DC, a distance, a condition as a rule ("is also knocked Prone"), persistent damage taken or dealt, or a resistance or weakness, none of which the plan carries. Stampede Medallion's Prone on a critical failure beside its basic save was dropped like that before. A plan that gives PF2e's own Effect is left out, since its rules are that Effect's.

  - A condition for a time ("is paralyzed for 4 rounds") is traced only by that condition for that time in the plan, and a new save at its turns only by a save the plan plays at those turns. A typed bonus or penalty ("a +1 status bonus") is traced only by a modifier of that value and type, and a Speed of a kind ("a fly Speed of 50 feet") only by that Speed. A sentence with any of these untraced is a note, whatever else of it the plan has. That made 15 plans partial, Golden Liberator's Feather's fly Speed and the save penalties for one kind of creature among them. A drug's stages are left out (Pseudo Afflictions plays them from the item's text), and so is a wayfinder's resonant power.

  The whole text's reminder, added beside a row that reads only part of the text, is dropped once later readings leave nothing unread. Before this, those plans counted resolved: the censuses went from 768 to 749 resolved consumables and from 826 to 779 resolved permanent activations.
- **An activation's damage by its weapon's own striking rune** (`src/compiler/rune-steps.js`, 2026-10-20). Examples: third-party Dragonbreath weapons, whose damage rises to 6d6 with a greater striking rune and to 8d6 with a major one; Guiding Star's 4, then 6 and 8 piercing damage by rune. The item's runes are known as its plan is compiled (`system.runes.striking`), so the damage is that rune's step and the sentence is read. A step's sentence isn't a dropped rule for `withUntracedRules`, since the item's rune decides it (Kinetic Club's "If the club has a greater striking rune, increase the additional damage to 2d6" is the Strike reader's).
- **A new save at the creature's turns** (`src/compiler/save-ends.js`, 2026-10-20; played by the ability module's `duration.saveEnds` and `note.saveEnds`, ability 1610bff). Each time the creature's turn ends, the active GM gets a save card for it, and its degree acts by the item's words:
  - It ends the condition on a success. Tusk and Fang Chain's critical failure confusion; Golden Silencer (Greater)'s silenced note Effect; Jawbreaker Shield's broken jaw, a note Effect for its minute.
  - It takes a round off on a success and ends it on a critical success. Spirit-Sealing Fulu (Greater)'s paralysis, now 4 rounds on a critical failure as its text says.
  - It ends on a success, at a DC of its own whatever the item's check: Bewildering Spellgun's success, "confused for 1 minute but can attempt a … save at the end of each of its turns".
  - It steps the condition up or down. Gearbinder Oil: its rows' steps on slowed, at most Slowed 3, for up to 6 rounds, a critical success ending it; first a GM yes or no on whether the creature took an action with the attack, manipulate or move trait that round. Dreaming Round: slowed up by 1 on a failure and down by 1 on a success, its fatigued gone with it at 0; its sleep at 0 actions stays the GM's.
- **A condition for its next action only** (`src/compiler/guards.js`, 2026-10-20). Tusk and Fang Chain's success, "Confused on its next action", is a note Effect until the end of the creature's next turn (PF2e can't count one action), not a confusion with no end. Its critical failure's "doesn't receive a flat check to end its confusion if it takes damage" is a note Effect for that confusion's minute.
- **A burst its user sets off once each round while the item's Effect lasts** (`src/compiler/round-burst.js`, 2026-10-20). Phoenix Flask used to roll its feathers' save once, when drunk, and gave no Fly Speed. Now the card gives the drinker PF2e's Effect: Phoenix Flask, and posts the feathers' basic Reflex save card for later (`plan.again`). That card stays open for the minute: each round, after the drinker's first Fly, target the creatures within 10 feet and press it.
- **Its user's own Effect, then the save card it had** (`src/compiler/effect-then-card.js`, 2026-10-20). Some consumables give their user a linked PF2e Effect and also hold a save for others. The ability compiler read only the save, so the Effect was never given. Now the item card gives the Effect first and then posts the same save card as before:
  - kept for later: the breath potions' exhale (Blight, Frost and Storm Breath, Cayden's Brew, Rovagug's Mud), used again for the hour when the text allows a reuse;
  - set off by an attacker: Vermin Repellent Agent's arthropods, for its 4 hours; Draft of Stellar Radiance's "Any creature that targets you", for its minute, with its 24-hour immunity;
  - its drinker's own save: Matsuki's Medicinal Wine's, beside its bonus against diseases and poisons.

  - an activation whose words give its user the Effect: Final Scalecloak's Embody the Storm, then its attackers' card for the minute (its "If a foe attempts to Strike you" read as an attacker); Kraken's Guard's grapple bonus, then its cone's card; Morning Glow's light, then the demons' save.

  Blight Breath's resistance lasts "for as long as you hold your breath", so the exhale's card ends its Effect. These stay as they were:
  - a spell the item grants as another activation (Roaring Potion's Bullhorn);
  - an Effect that is a result's or another creature's (Crackling Bubble Gum's failure, Sinuous Recorder's Speed penalty, Bewitching Bloom's ally);
  - a save at a creature's turn, which is a zone's (Arctic Worm Chitin Shield, Mamlambo Scale).
- **Rows read from plain words** (`src/compiler/guards.js`, 2026-10-20). A snare's save rows now go through the same guards as an item's.
  - A row "As success, but the penalty is -20 feet" is the success's Speed penalty at that value (Hobbling Snare's critical failure).
  - A row's damage in plain words is that row's damage. Fang Snare's "takes 1 piercing damage and 2d8 poison damage"; Thunder Snare's "As success, and the target also takes 1 sonic damage", which also gets the success's Deafened.
  - "Until the end of its next turn" in an item's row counts on the creature's own turns, not its user's (Thunder, Trip and Rending Snare, Heart of Sarnax, Wintershot).
  - A time in a sentence goes to the conditions named before it, or to all of them when it opens the sentence. Stunned with a value is never timed: Stunning Snare's "Off-Guard for 1 round and Stunned 1", Pyronite, Titan's Grasp. Liar's Bangle's "For the next minute, it is Stupefied 1" keeps its minute.
  - An item that comes back by itself after a while is a card line, not GM work: Eternal Eruption's "reforms itself in a container on your person".
- **Its allies' bonus, then its enemies' save** (`src/compiler/permanent.js`, 2026-10-20). Horn of the Archon's Archon's Note gives "Allies in the area" (its save's 60-foot emanation) a +1 status bonus to attack rolls and saving throws for 1 round. The card applies that to the allies targeted, then posts the enemies' Fortitude save card (`plan.again`, now played after a save card too).
- **A save harder for some creatures, or not for them** (`src/compiler/kind-saves.js`, `src/live/if-target.js`, 2026-10-20). The save card goes out once for each group of targets. A group whose trait or sense the text names gets its penalty on the save (`plan.ifTarget [{ traits | senses, bonus }]`): Lamentation of the Faithless's holy creatures at –2 status, Anglerfish Lantern's aquatic and water creatures at –2 circumstance, Deravon's Helm's humans at –1 status, Daybreak Ammunition's creatures with darkvision at –2 circumstance. A kind it says is immune gets no card, only a line on the item's card (Lamentation's unholy creatures), and so do undead and constructs for a save only "a living creature" attempts (Slumber Arrow, Ghost Ampoule, Moritype). Slumber Arrow's failure is now its –5-foot Speed penalty, and its critical failure adds Slowed 1; it used to slow on a failure.
- **A Speed penalty for whoever fails its save** (`src/compiler/guards.js` failsAlso, 2026-10-20). Crackling Bubble Gum's pop, "A creature that fails its save is also bound with sticky gum, taking a -10-foot item penalty to its Speed for 1 minute", goes on its failing rows. Its early end, 3 Interact actions to remove the gum, is said on the Effect for the GM (the ability's `modifier.until`).
- **Soothing Powder** (`src/compiler/recovery.js`, 2026-10-20). Its list of powder types and its own-type flat check are the card's picker and roll, not GM work. The Greater's "persists for 1 minute, lowering the DCs of flat checks against that type of persistent damage" is a note Effect on the creature for that minute.
- **Its holder's resistance, a weakness for everyone around it** (`src/compiler/weakened-around.js`, 2026-10-20; ability f25a67d's `zone.inside[i].who`). Maelstromic Destabilizer is a 10-foot zone around its holder for its minute. The holder gets the resistance to spirit damage, and every other creature inside gets the weakness, each as an Effect while it's inside. A creature immune to spirit damage gains nothing from the weakness, as PF2e plays it.
- **What a Quickened's extra action is only for** (`src/compiler/honest.js` withActionLimit, 2026-10-20). Zealous Banner's "can use the additional action only to Strike", Energizing Treat's, Quickened Standard's and Snare of Speed's limits are now a note Effect beside the Quickened, for its time, instead of a note left to the GM. That's the user's rule for a behaviour beside a real result, and the note-Effect rule. A Quickened the card doesn't give, Boots of the Blight's on a save it doesn't play, stays a note.
- **Craft Requirements after result rows** (`src/compiler/item-data.js`, 2026-10-20). A Craft Requirements paragraph right after an item's degree rows is taken out before reading, since the ability compiler read it into the last row. Whirlwind Vial's critical failure is now its 20-foot push and Prone with nothing by hand, and the leftover lines of Bomb Snare, Mamlambo Scale and Necrobinding Serum are shorter. What follows the paragraph is kept (Vaccine's Duration).
- **A proficiency or class its Requirements need** (`src/compiler/requirement.js`, 2026-10-20). Its class too: Accompaniment Cloak's "You are a bard", Hexing Jar's witch, 39 items; the class alone, what more it says the GM's, and a character with no class not judged. An item whose Requirements name a rank in a skill, a save or Perception (a talisman's "You're an expert in Stealth", Lion Badge's "expert in Will saves") is checked against the character who uses it. Below that rank, the GM gets a whispered warning, as for an activation used past its frequency, since the item has been used already. An NPC isn't judged (PF2e keeps no ranks for it), and neither is "an expert with the affixed weapon" (PF2e doesn't keep what a talisman is affixed to).
- **An activation only after a hit with the weapon** (`src/compiler/strike-gate.js`, 2026-10-20). Its Requirements are "your last action was a successful Strike with it": Alghollthu Whip (a Grapple or Trip too), Apotheosis Knife, Bow of Sun Slaying, Obsidian Edge (a ranged Strike), Catoblepas Maul, Bloodgorger Scythe, Burr Shield and Vampire-Fang Morningstar. Rending Gauntlets need two unarmed hits on one creature in the round.
  - Before its card is posted, live play looks at its user's latest rolls in the chat. If they don't qualify, the activation is refused, with no card and no use counted.
  - Its card then goes to the creature struck when its presser targets none.
  - What else a requirement says stays the GM's: Bloodgorger's target taking no bleed, Vampire-Fang's sunlight. One with another way in (Scizore of the Crab's "or you have a creature Grabbed") isn't refused.
- **Read for docs/needs.md's reader needs** (2026-10-25, `test/reader-needs.test.js`):
  - A condition only for creatures of a trait (`traitGated` in `src/compiler/guards.js`): Lacquered Waist Drum's ghosts. It's now `plan.ifTarget` at its degrees, so the save card for a ghost has it and the one for another undead doesn't. Before, it was a note Effect to apply by hand.
  - A save harder for creatures named by their trait as a word (`src/compiler/kind-saves.js`): Mythotoxin's "Mythic creatures take a -2 circumstance penalty to saves against mythotoxin".
  - More damage on a failure for a kind (`failDamage`, 2026-10-26): Locust Leather's "Swarms that fail this save also take 1d6 persistent poison damage". A swarm's failure rows deal the basic save's damage and that.
  - A sense its user keeps by doing something, beside its own time: Darkvision Scope's "as long as you continue to look through the scope" is on the Effect's words, not a note.
  - A sense while its user Sustains, up to a time (2026-10-27): Dragon's Eye Charm's Darkvision through the charm, sustained up to 10 minutes, lasts 10 minutes. The Sustain is on the Effect's words ("at most while you Sustain the Activation (end it sooner if you stop)").
  - "On this check, you roll twice": the check the sentence before attempts (`src/compiler/fortune.js`). Brooch of Inspiration's "Recall Knowledge using Lore" is PF2e's `lore-skill-check`; the Major's "with any skill" is `skill-check`.
  - Breathing underwater, which no PF2e rule plays: "You can breathe underwater for 8 hours" is in words on its user's Effect, for its time (Submersible Helmet, Sailor's Collar).
  - A DC to move it that no PF2e rule sets (2026-10-27): the Immovable rune's "you can be moved only if a creature succeeds at a DC 40 Athletics check to Force Open your armor", and Immovable Potion's "the DC to move you from your place, including knocking you Prone, is 40" is words on its Effect, for the GM to use against Shoves, Trips and forced movement.
  - Sure footing (2026-10-27): Sure-Step Potion's "you can Step into difficult terrain, and you aren't Off-Guard on uneven ground" is the same kind of rule, words on its Effect for the hour. And Conrasu Coin (Bythos)'s longer Stride with the Nimble Roll feat after a missed attack is a line of its readied card (bonus.limits), its user's own move.
  - A longer range for auditory actions (2026-10-27): Megaphone Mutagen's "The range of your actions and spells with the auditory trait increases by 10 feet" (15, 20, 40) is words on its Effect, as no PF2e rule plays it.
  - A sentence that says only what the bonus's time already means: Affliction Suppressant's daily saves, which its 10 minutes never reach.
  - A Quickened's extra action for the actions it names: Propulsive Boots' "You can use the extra action to Stride, Climb, or Swim" is a note Effect beside the Quickened.
  - An effect a successful Escape ends (`src/compiler/escape-ends.js`): the ability module's own `escape { dc }` on a hit's riders, since its 0.9.0 ends them on that Escape. Vine Arrow's Speed penalty and its critical hit's Immobilized (DC 19), and all a Spring-Loaded Net Launcher's net gives (DC 16). Black Tendril Shot's "effects last until the target Escapes" was already on its save rows from the ability compiler, so the sentence is no longer a note.
  - A grab with endings of its own (2026-10-26): Ouroboros Flail's Chained Grasp, "Grabbed by the flail until the end of your next turn unless you move, your target Escapes, or you attack with the flail". Its Grabbed also ends on an Escape at its link's DC (`[[/act escape dc=31]]`). The other two endings are words on the grab, a note Effect on the rows that grab.
  - A DC and damage by the weapon's potency rune (`src/compiler/rune-steps.js`): Screaming Pinion's "If the screaming pinion has a +3 potency rune, the DC increases to 35 …, and the damage increases to 10d10 sonic", by the weapon's own rune at the card. What the rune replaced is kept on the plan (`runeStep.replaced`), so the honesty pass doesn't count the base sentence as a rule left out.
  - An ally area's size on a lowered condition (`src/compiler/reduce.js`): Fearless Sash's "You and each ally in a 5-foot emanation reduce your Frightened values by 1" carries `radius: 5`, for the card's words. That also means the honesty pass finds its "5-foot" in the plan.
  - A resistance to splash or area damage in the honesty pass (`src/compiler/honest.js`): "resistance 3 to area damage" is held by PF2e's names, `area-damage` and `splash-damage`, as the reader keeps them, so it isn't counted a rule left out (two of an installed module's alchemical foods).
- **Live-play pieces for docs/needs.md** (2026-10-25, `test/live-pieces.test.js`, live cases `regainMythic`, `alliesBelt`, `rageCrown`, `flareLight`):
  - A Mythic Point given back (`readRegain` in `src/compiler/reduce.js`, `payRegain` in `scripts/spend.js`): Preordained Spear's "and you regain 1 Mythic Point" is `plan.item.regain`. Once its card applies, the point is added up to its user's maximum (a Hero Point under the world setting, as its costs are paid), with a line in the chat.
  - The allies' own Effect beside its user's (`ownPlan` in `src/compiler/consumables.js`, `plan.allyLinks`): Belt of the Five Kings' "(Allies)" for "allies within 20 feet of you". Once its card applies, each ally within that many feet of its user's token gets it (not its user, nor a creature of another side).
  - A retaliation until its user's rage ends (`src/compiler/direct-damage.js`, `retaliation.untilRage`): Instinct Crown (Dragon)'s card stays open while its user rages and closes when the Rage Effect is deleted; a use after that is refused.
  - A light placed in a square (`src/compiler/placed-light.js`, `scripts/placed-light.js`): Flare Beacon's "casting bright light in a 20-foot radius and dim light in the next 20 feet for 1 minute" is a Foundry light at its user's square, made as it's used. The active GM's client removes it once the world's time passes its end (Foundry's updateWorldTime, which a combat's rounds advance). Its user moves it when they put it farther away.
  - Another result for some creatures (`src/compiler/kind-saves.js`, `src/live/if-target.js`): `plan.ifTarget` entries with `heal` (healing instead of the save: Spirit Fan's living target, "the energy restores 3d8+8 Hit Points", healed by the save card itself in scripts/poisons.js, for a creature PF2e says is living), with `formula` (another damage: Lance of Sun's Radiance's dragons at 6d12 spirit), or a penalty for a trait's creatures named as a word (Golden Gloves' "Unholy creatures take a –2 item penalty to their roll").
  - A condition its user takes beside the save it gives others (`src/compiler/user-conditions.js`, `plan.userEffects`): Pharasmin Visor's Dazzled until the start of its user's next turn, given through the ability module's applyChange as it's used.
  - Its user's temporary Hit Points and a DC by its user's conditions (2026-10-26): Death Tusk Helm's "You then gain 5 temporary Hit Points that last 1 minute" is given to its user as it's used (the ability module's tempHp). Its "Increase this DC by the sum of your Doomed and Wounded values (maximum DC 20)" is `plan.dcBy`: the save card's DC is raised by its user's values as it's posted (`src/live/scaled.js`).
  - One creature's damage and condition, then a save for the others near it (`oneThenAroundPlan` in `src/compiler/hit-around.js`): Staff of Sun Wukong's Meteor Slam. The card at the creature targeted, its 10d10 bludgeoning and Enfeebled 1 for a day automatic, and `plan.around`'s basic Reflex card at the creatures within 10 feet of it of any side, but not its wielder (`notUser`).
  - A second Effect only for persistent damage (`ownPlan` in `src/compiler/consumables.js`): Horned Lion Amulet is a ways card, used once. "Fire damage" gives its Effect; "Persistent fire damage" gives both Effects and the DC 10 flat check (`item.recovery`). An Effect a card gave that lowers a type's flat-check DC with no time of its own goes once that persistent damage is over (scripts/live.js).
  - Temporary Hit Points from a share of the damage the target then takes (`src/compiler/drain.js`, `src/live/drain.js`, `scripts/drain.js`, 2026-10-26): Blood-Drinker Blade (Greater)'s Exsanguinate. Its 6d6 bleed is persistent, as PF2e has all bleed. It is the card's damage at the creature targeted. `plan.drain` (`{ fraction: 0.5, type: "bleed", duration: 1 minute }`) replaces the typed amount. Once the card is applied (the ability module's `applied` hook), the creature gets a mark for the minute. The first bleed damage it then takes is seen through the core's applyDamage listener: a tick of the persistent bleed, not a bleed that is only set. Its user then gets half of the Hit Points and temporary Hit Points the creature lost, after resistances, rounded down. They come as PF2e's TempHP on an Effect that ends when the minute from the activation does. The mark is used up, and the GM and the user's owners are told. Damage that takes nothing off (immunity, resistance) leaves the mark for the next.
  - Named options to choose from (`src/compiler/named-options.js`, 2026-10-26): an Effect's "choose one of the following effects" is a ways card, one button for each option. Each option is read as the activation would be with that option for its Effect: the words before the choice, then the option's own, with its degree rows and its traits ("Crush Heart (death)"). Before, one option was read and the others dropped, though the plan counted whole. That was Tactician's Helm, which played Move It!'s Effect for every use. An option that is only its user's own moves and basic actions is a line of the card: Charge!'s "Stride twice", Protect!'s Stride and Raise your Shield, Re-Arm!'s Interacts.
  - A condition that follows once another ends (`src/compiler/after-ends.js`, `scripts/after-ends.js`, 2026-10-26): Heart of Sarnax's Crush Heart gives Slowed 2 for 1 minute once its critical failure's Stunned ends. Its Bestow Vim gives Quickened for 1 minute, what the extra action is for as a note Effect beside it, then Fatigued for 1 hour. `plan.afterEnds` holds `[{ degree, when, then }]`. Once the ability module's card is applied, each creature of that degree that has the condition is marked. When PF2e deletes the condition and the creature has none of it left, the active GM's client gives it the next condition, granted by an Effect for its time.
  - A death at 0 Hit Points from its damage (`withSlain`): "A creature reduced to 0 HP by this damage is slain" is the core's `postDamageTerminal` `{ kind: "death", threshold: 0 }`, which the ability module plays. Before, the sentence was dropped unread.
  - A temporary immunity from when the effect ends (2026-10-26, `after`). Starsong Nectar's 24 hours "once its effects end": the item card flags its PF2e Effect (`immunityAfter`), and once that Effect is gone the creature gets the "Immune:" Effect (`setUpImmunityAfter`, scripts/immunity.js). The card still refuses a creature already immune. Captivating Bauble's "When the effect ends": its save's `trigger.immunity` has the degree `"after-end"`, which no result has, so the ability module's card only leaves out the immune. The failures' `afterEnds` give the immunity once Fascinated ends.
  - Destroyed on a failed save, and its user's backlash (`withAfterApplied`, 2026-10-26): Celestial Peachwood Sword's Violent Exorcism. Its failures are the core's terminal death. `plan.backlash` (`{ degrees, perLevel: "1d6", type: "void" }`) is rolled on its user once the card is applied, by each destroyed creature's level (scripts/after-ends.js).
  - Its user healed by the damage the card dealt: Horns of Naraga's "If you are an undead, you regain Hit Points equal to the void damage the creature took". `plan.drain` is `{ fraction: 1, heal: true, immediate: true, onlyIf: "undead" }`. The ability module applies the card's damage with the card's item. The item module's damage listener sees that damage, and the user, if undead, regains the Hit Points the creature lost, up to its maximum (scripts/drain.js).
  - An activation ends before an affliction's stat block it doesn't name (`splitActivations`, 2026-10-26). Sweet Hag's Eye's Sickeningly Sweet, for the eye eaten, followed its Meltaway Mint and was read as the mint's save. Now the mint is its own Reflex DC 18, with 1d4 persistent fire on a failure.
  - Concealed to the end of its user's next turn, ended sooner by a hostile action (`CONCEALED_UNTIL` in row-readings): Invisible Chain Shirt's Obscured Approach. The hostile-action end is a note Effect beside the condition, and the Stride is a line of the card.
  - Fruit that withers (`creates.lasts`, `scripts/expiring.js`, 2026-10-26): Verdant Branch's fruits are flagged with the world time they wither at, and the active GM's client removes them once the world's time passes it.
  - An affliction a degree exposes the creature to, with its stat block in the item's text (`withExposure` in row-readings, 2026-10-26): Tarantula Ampoule's critical failure. The row's `followUps` (the core's) hold the venom's own save, handing on its stages as the ability compiler reads a stat block (`readAffliction`). The ability module posts that next card.
  - A burst for a round, lit (`src/compiler/spark-column.js`): Spark Wafer is the ability module's zone, placed within 20 feet for 1 round. Its basic Reflex save falls on a creature starting its turn there. Before, the save was rolled at once at the creatures targeted. `zone.light` puts a Foundry light at the zone's centre for that round, removed with the zone (scripts/placed-light.js `lightZone`).
  - Plans counted whole with a rule left in their notes (an audit of 2026-10-26 found 18, most of them card lines). The rest:
    - Elysian Dew's unholy drinker gets no aura, and its Fortitude DC 30 save card (Drained 2) instead (`plan.item.unlessKind`, `src/compiler/drinker-kind.js`).
    - Flame Layer Balm marks its wearer for the hour; the first fire damage it takes gives it PF2e's Spell Effect: Fiery Body for a minute (`plan.item.armed`, `src/compiler/armed.js`, `scripts/armed.js`). Before, Fiery Body came at once.
    - Golden Blade of Mzali's Elemental Form is fire at rank 7, and turns PF2e's own "used Elemental Form" toggle on (`plan.toggleOn`). Turning it back off at daily preparations is the user's, so the plan stays partial.
    - Amulet of the Hellcat's Invisible is on an Effect its user's next Strike ends (`endsOnStrike`, `scripts/ends-on-strike.js`).
    - Robe of Stone's Elemental Form is earth (`application.choice`, the Effect's ChoiceSet preset). Its 30-foot tremorsense and burrowing through stone are an Effect for the form's minute (`src/compiler/form-extras.js`).
  - A swim Speed's own limits in its words (`speed.note`): Buoyant Buckle's swimming only along the surface, ended once its pouches take damage (AC 10, Hardness 0). What hits the pouches is the table's to say.
  - After a save card (`withSaveAfterApplied`, scripts/after-ends.js, 2026-10-26), Cauthooj Bagpipes' Disorienting Fugue:
    - a critical success's immunity lasts an hour: `plan.immunityLonger`, the ability module's "Immune:" Effect lengthened once the card is applied;
    - a critical failure's creature attacks itself: `plan.selfAttack`, its own first melee Strike's damage rolled with PF2e and applied to it (a confused creature attacking itself hits). Before, that sentence was dropped unread.
  - A spell an item holds for later (`src/compiler/stored-spell.js`, the objects card's `cast-stored`, `ready-stored` and `expend-stored`, 2026-10-26). The spell is the item's flag (`storedSpell`: `{ source, rank, name }`).
    - Furnace of Endings, by grade: its card with nothing stored picks one of its user's scrolls that fits (rank, fire or void trait, three actions at most) and burns it.
