With a spell stored, it casts it through the spell module's `castFromItem` at the creatures targeted, at its user's best spell DC (as a scroll's), and empties.
    - Spell Bastion: storing picks one of its user's spells that fits (3rd rank or lower, 2 actions or fewer, able to target another creature); spending its casting is the user's own, a line of the card. Its readying is an Effect to the start of the user's next turn. Its unleashing casts at the attacker targeted, and its last activation lets the spell go.
    - Ring of Counterspells' harmless expending empties it.
    - Not Spell Reservoir: the spell module keeps and casts the reservoir's spell itself.
    - Archaic Wayfinder holds one cantrip (not one cast as a free action or a reaction), at its caster's cantrip rank, cast from it once.
  - A resistance traded for another (`withTradedResistance`, 2026-10-26): Chromatic Robe's Align Energy costs its Mythic Point. It gives resistance 15 (20 for the Greater) to the type picked. The Effect's words say the other three types lose theirs and that it lasts until the next daily preparations, and a new use replaces the last Effect (`resist.note`, `resist.replaces`). Its cost's sentence no longer leaves the plan partial (`withSpend` reads unread lines too).
  - A flat check to Cast a Spell by degree (`withCastFlatCheck`, `castFlatCheckSource`, 2026-10-26). Blessed Lantern Flail's Purge Unholy Magic: DC 5 until its user's next turn, DC 5 for a minute, DC 11 for a minute. `plan.castFlatCheck` is held on the save its Strike's hit posts (`bombSave` keeps it; the dropped-row guard no longer takes those rows for empty). Once the card is applied, each creature of those degrees gets an Effect carrying the spell module's own runtime flag, `castFlatCheck { dc }`. The spell module's casting rules roll that check as the creature casts, and a failure loses the spell.
  - A fortune for any roll at legendary proficiency (2026-10-26): Perfected Robes' "If your next action is to attempt a d20 roll with which you have legendary proficiency" is the roll-twice card with selector "legendary". At Apply, its user's legendary statistics are read off its sheet: skills, saves and Perception at rank 4, Strikes at legendary proficiency (PF2e's `item:proficiency:rank:4`) and spell attacks. That gives one RollTwice rule each, and the first roll among them spends the Effect. With none, the card refuses.
  - A worse condition the card can't see the cause of (`src/compiler/instead-way.js`, 2026-10-26): Imposing Shield's Frightened 3 "if the Shield Block reduced the incoming damage to 0" is a second button beside Frightened 2, as the user's 2026-10-11 ruling has choices.
  - Its user blinded for good as it's thrown: Skull Bomb's thrower is Blinded with no end (`plan.userEffects`, `src/compiler/user-conditions.js`); a consumable's plan now reads its user's conditions too.
  - Prone only on the ground: Drake Rifle (Fire)'s Drake Shot leaves its rows' Prone off for a target whose token is above the ground (`strike.groundedProne`, `withoutAloftProne`).
  - A Speed while the item is on (`src/compiler/on-off.js`): Steamflight Pack's fly Speed of 20 feet, for its 20 minutes of water. Either activation turns it on; the item's next activation turns it off (`speeds[i].toggle`, as a sense's toggle).
  - Behaviour rows as note Effects (2026-10-26, the user's 2026-10-25 rulings on speech and minds): a row that only says how the creature acts is a note Effect on it for the row's time. Robe of Beneficence's critical failure ("As failure, and …") applies the row's own Stunned 2 and notes the rest; Successor Doll's line with no end of its own lasts until the GM removes it; Graverose Garland's flight to a grave and Stage Fright Missive's Sickened 1 when it speaks are labelled note Effects on their rows (`withBehaviourRows`, `src/compiler/row-readings.js`). Shining Shield's "As failure, but the duration is 1 minute" lasts a minute, not its reminder's round.
  - Healing and the same amount as damage (2026-10-26): Swarmeater's Clasp's "You recover 3d10+8 Hit Points and deal the same amount of bludgeoning damage to the swarm" is a damage card at the swarm within its user's reach. Its user regains the roll itself, whatever the swarm resisted (`plan.drain.fromRoll`, scripts/drain.js). A user with void healing regains none, since the recovery is vitality healing.
  - A shield's own temporary Hit Points (`src/compiler/shield-temp.js`, scripts/shield-temp.js, 2026-10-26): Ablative Shield Plating's card puts an Effect on its user for the shield it plates (the one held, else its only one), with a counter of the plating's temporary Hit Points for its time. The Hit Points that shield then loses, by PF2e's Shield Block or by hand, come off the counter first; the Effect goes once it's used up. A shield that's already plated is refused.
  - A feat's own extra die on an item's Strike (2026-10-26): Iron Cudgel's "With the Brutal Finish feat, one more weapon damage die on a hit and on a failure" is `strike.featDie`. On a hit it's a DamageDice rule with PF2e's roll option for the feat (`feat:brutal-finish`); on a failure it's one more of the weapon's dice when its user has that feat. "After the Strike, your turn ends" is a line of the card (`strike.turnEnds`).
  - An unarmed attack for one Strike (2026-10-26): Ring of the Tiger's claw is its Effect until the end of the turn, and the Strike with it is its user's own action, a line of the card. The Greater ring's "not cumulative with additional damage dice from striking runes" is `attack.strikingCap`: the dice its user's unarmed striking rune adds count toward the claw's extra die (`attackEffectSource`, `unarmedStriking`).
  - Swallow-Spike (2026-10-26): its attack takes the armor's item bonus to AC, meaning the armor's AC Bonus plus its potency rune (PF2e's Armor Potency and Plated Duster use the phrase that way), on the modifier and twice that on the damage. Before, it took the potency alone. "If the creature is swallowing or engulfing you, … an additional 1d6 untyped damage" is a second button on the ways card (`withExtraWay`, `src/compiler/instead-way.js`). Cutting free at the Rupture value is the ability module's own Rupture check, so it's a line of the card. tools/needs.mjs counts armor runes as etched.
  - Conditions at the end of the creature's next turn (2026-10-26, `scripts/later.js`): Nap Gas Dispenser's "At the end of their next turn, they fall Prone and fall Unconscious for 1 minute" is `plan.later` (at the plan: the core takes unknown row keys for gaps), no longer a note. Once the card is applied, the creature is marked, and at the end of its next turn (PF2e's pf2e.endTurn) it gets Prone and an Unconscious Effect for the minute. A mark made during the creature's own turn waits for the turn after. The critical failure's "three Interact actions to wake them" is in the Unconscious Effect's words.
  - A weakness triggered again (2026-10-26): for Freedom's Flame's Holy Retribution ("The target's holy weakness is triggered again or, if the target doesn't have a holy weakness, it takes 15 spirit damage"), `plan.ifTarget { weakness, weaknessDamage }` gives a creature with that weakness its value as untyped damage. Everyone else takes the card's 15 spirit damage (`withWeaknessAgain`, `planFor`).
  - Two Strikes on one roll (2026-10-26): Coldstar Pistols' "Make two Strikes against one target, taking the highest of the two attack rolls and applying it to both attacks" is `strike.volley.twin`. The readied Strike's attack is rolled twice and the higher result kept (PF2e's RollTwice), which matches two rolls at one modifier, and that result counts for both Strikes. On a hit, the second Strike's damage is rolled too (`secondStrikeDamage`, scripts/strikes.js). Its multiple attack penalty rises two steps after them, a line of the card.
  - A roll at each afflicted turn (2026-10-26, `src/compiler/afflicted-turns.js`, `scripts/afflicted-turns.js`): Blackfinger Blight's and Astringent Venom's "at the beginning of their turn … a flat DC 5 check or drop one random item they're holding". Once the save card hands the poison to Pseudo Afflictions, the creature is marked for the poison's maximum duration. At the start of each of its turns, while Pseudo Afflictions still has the poison on it, the flat check is rolled; on a failure, one random item it holds is dropped. Astringent Venom's sentence used to be dropped unread.
  - The weapon a talisman is fastened to, drawn (2026-10-26): Liquid Gold's "You draw the attached weapon as a free action" is the objects card's hand op for weapons only (`objects.weapon`): one of its user's weapons not in hand, picked on the card. The Greater one's "draw and load" also loads one round of its ammunition with PF2e's own attach (`objects.load`).
  - An illusion on its user's clothes (2026-10-26): Costume Change (Sihedron Ring, Raiment, Skirmisher's Coat) puts a note Effect on its user for the look. "Only a creature that's benefiting from truesight or a similar effect can attempt to disbelieve this illusion, with a DC of 20" is the GM's disbelief card, per the user's 2026-10-25 illusion ruling (`appearancePlan`, `readDisbelief`'s ONLY_WHO).
  - Speed penalties ended with conditions (2026-10-26, `readSpeedPenaltyEnds`, scripts/speed-penalties.js): Spleen Bloodstone of Arazni's Break Bonds ("as well as any Speed penalties affecting you") and Cayden's Tankard ("anything giving such targets a circumstance penalty to Speed", its targets' too). Each FlatModifier on a Speed selector below 0 is taken off the Effect that gives it, and an Effect left with no rules is deleted. Break Bonds' "unless the effect is magical and of 12th level or higher" spares such an Effect, and the conditions it grants (`unlessMagical`, PF2e's grantedBy).
  - A recovery's own damage and its lower-level case (2026-10-26, `withLowerLevelRecovery`): Searing Suture deals its 1d6 fire to the creature it's applied to, then rolls its flat check against persistent bleed. "When applied against a bleed effect that is lower level than the searing suture, the flat check is automatically successful" is the ways card's second button, which ends the bleed outright (the card can't see the level of what caused it).
  - A reward for felling the creature marked (2026-10-26, `withFelledReward`, scripts/felled.js): Doom Switch's Bragging Rights. Once its attack card is applied, the target is marked for the minute. Damage its user deals that leaves the target at 0 Hit Points gives its user temporary Hit Points equal to the target's level (twice that on a critical success) for 1 round.
  - Off-guard only to its user (2026-10-26): Mercurial Mantle's Cunning Dodge and Purloining Cloak. The creature's failed Perception check makes it off-guard only to its user's melee Strike (once). On a critical failure it's off-guard to all its user's attacks until the end of its next turn. Both use the core's effect scope, which the ability module plays as its mark on its user. The Step and the Strike are its user's own actions, a line of the card (`perceptionCheckPlan`).
  - An Effect behind its user's Recall Knowledge (2026-10-26, `checkGated` checkGate): Diplomat's Badge's and Uniter of Clans' "Attempt a DC 20 check to Recall Knowledge about people … If you succeed". Its user rolls PF2e's Recall Knowledge with the skill that fits (the GM says which). Apply takes the latest one in the chat and checks its total against the DC (PF2e's degrees, natural 20 and 1 included). The Effect is given only on a success; otherwise the card closes with nothing applied.
  - An item's own attack with splash (2026-10-26, `plan.splash`, scripts/own-splash.js): Lady's Chalice's "2 spirit splash damage (including to you, if you're evil)". Once its attack card is applied, a hit or a failure posts the bomb card's splash list: the creatures within 5 feet of the target, plus the target itself on a failure. Its user is listed only when it has the evil or unholy trait.
  - A thrown item's hit for one kind only (2026-10-26): Life Salt's "On a hit against an undead" is `plan.onlyAgainst { traits }`, and the card refuses a target without the trait. The save's failure note Effect now says it ends "once its user uses a hostile action against any undead", from the sentence after it.
  - A readied Strike (2026-10-26): Flashblade's Prepare Flash, "You Ready a melee Strike, increasing the flashblade's reach for that Strike to 15 feet. If the trigger doesn't occur, this doesn't count against the flashblade's frequency". It's the Strike card's readied Strike (`strike.readied`), with that reach, until the start of its user's next turn. If its Effect goes before the Strike is made, the activation's use is given back (`setUpReadiedRefund`, scripts/strikes.js).
  - A memory its user decides (2026-10-26): Unmemorable Mantle's "You decide whether or not the creature recalls the interaction" is a mind's, not a choice on the card (the needs rules: "choice" leaves it, "mind" reads "recalls the interaction"). Under the user's 2026-10-25 ruling each degree is a note Effect on the creature.
  - Truth Potion (2026-10-26, `withTruthRows`): the failure's "When you speak, you must tell the truth" is a note Effect on the drinker for the potion's 10 minutes. The critical failure adds the question: when asked, the drinker attempts a DC 19 Will save, an inline check on its note Effect, then answers truthfully on a failure or is temporarily immune to that question on a success. This follows the user's 2026-10-25 ruling on minds. The immunity reader no longer re-adds a sentence a row's note Effect already says.
  - Talk at will (2026-10-26): Communication Pendants' "for as long as your desire" isn't a time to keep (the needs rules), so its speech note Effect counts done under the user's ruling on speech.
  - An immunity with no end of its own (2026-10-26): Tteokguk of Time Advancement's "This lasts until morning of the next day, at which point you are temporarily immune until the next lunar new year". The immunity tracker gives it once the soup's Effect ends ("at which point" is read like "once its effects end"). It's unlimited (`duration.until`), and its words say it ends at the next lunar new year, for the GM to remove then.
  - Its allies' own moves (2026-10-26, `alliesMovePlan`): Guiding Cajon Drum's "Up to 2 allies that can hear the drum can use a reaction to Stride 5 feet" is a line of the card. Each ally's reaction is its own to take on the map.
  - Its user's own one move (2026-10-26, `ownMovePlan`): when an activation's whole effect is its user's Step, Stride or Leap (Viper Rapier's mocking Step, Little Love's Leap), that move is a line of the card.
  - Its user's own actions of another kind (2026-10-27, `ownMovePlan`'s OWN_ACTS): Swiftmount Saddle's Mount, Spangled Rider's Suit's dismount into an adjacent space, Maestro's Chair's Stride and Sustain of an active composition, and Sandals of the Stag's jump that doesn't automatically fail. PF2e has no rule for mounting or for a jump's Stride first, so each is a line of the card. Piereta's Retributive Strike isn't one of these: the ally's resistance is more than its own action. The Cricket's Stride up to three times its Speed (or another movement type it has), triggering no reactions, is one too. So is Clockwork Diving Suit's water jet, a 25-foot Swim in a straight line.
  - A condition its user avoids gaining (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/prevent-condition.js`, scripts/prevent-condition.js): Immortal Bastion's Doomed or Wounded, and Urn of Ashes' Doomed. Each is a readied Effect for the round, `prevents` the conditions. While it lasts, PF2e's creating that condition on the creature, or raising its value, is cancelled (preCreateItem or preUpdateItem, on the client making the change). The Effect is then used up and its owners and the GM are told. It works once, for the first of its conditions to come. The urn's own doom, which keeps it from being used again until a night's rest, is a line of the card. That part is out of combat, so the urn counts partial.
  - A creature its user picks, off-guard to its next attack this turn (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/off-guard-next.js`): Boots of the Dead's One of You. The undead the presser targets is Off-Guard until the end of its user's turn, only to its user's melee attacks and only once. This is the core's scope, as Mercurial Mantle's Cunning Dodge has it.
  - Spiritsight Crossbow's Ethereal Shot (2026-10-26): its Strike "ignore[s] cover from physical objects within the range of your Ethereal Vision", read as every cover level (`IGNORE_COVER`), PF2e's cover-suppressing Effect for the Strike.
  - A weapon that takes another shape (2026-10-26, objects op "reshape"). Anchor Spear's "extends into a +2 greater striking longspear or back to its original form" offers its other form. The Shifting rune's "takes the shape of another melee weapon that requires the same number of hands" offers PF2e's base melee weapons used in as many hands. The picked base weapon's die, damage type, group, category, hands, Bulk, reload and traits go on the item. Its other traits, its runes, name and material are kept (`reshapeChoices`, `reshapeWeapon`, scripts/objects.js).
  - A weapon of the user's choice that fades (2026-10-26, objects op "create" with `choose`): Armory Bracelet's charm. The card lists PF2e's common base weapons of the charm groups, and the one picked appears in hand with its runes, plus 20 pieces of its ammunition if it takes some. After 1 minute its runes drop to the lower ones (`made.downgradeAt`, scripts/expiring.js). It and its ammunition crumble at its user's next daily preparations (PF2e's pf2e.restForTheNight, `setUpMadeWeapons`).
  - An item's toggle until its user's next daily preparations (2026-10-26): Golden Blade of Mzali's Elemental Form turns its PF2e toggle on (its extra fire damage off), remembers it on the spear (`toggleUntilRest`), and turns it back off at its user's next Rest for the Night (PF2e's pf2e.restForTheNight, `togglesAtRest`). Before, this was a note for the user.
  - The user's answers of 2026-10-26 (docs/decisions-for-user.md, all A):
    - 1: an item whose words show no rule to play (`decided.js`, needs "no-rules") counts done. Its card says it has no rules to play, and the needs index lists those entries on a line of their own.
    - 4: a clause the text leaves to the GM's discretion becomes a line of the card ending "(the GM's call)", and the plan counts whole when nothing else is left (`withGmCalls`, its notes and its Strike's).
    - 5: a condition with no value takes 1 when another row gives a higher one (The Desert's Enfeebled 1), but not one the text rolls (Smirking Devil's Drained [[/r 1d4]]). A counteract check with no modifier uses GM Core's DC for the item's level less 10 (Flowing Water). Each says on the card that the GM may change it.
    - 2: a spell-cast card counts partial when one of its notes adds a rule the cast doesn't play (`withCastNotes`, honest.js): a shorter duration, another range, a bonus or penalty, a counteract modifier, damage beside the spell, the spell heightened by the user's level. A note that only restates the cast doesn't count: the spell's name, rank and DC, its spell attack, the spell's own duration and range (from PF2e's spell, `spellIndex`), "within range", what it does "as normal with the spell", an intelligent item's statistics. The needs index now counts every partial builtin card as partial, so 74 zone, teleport, readied-bonus, ways and table cards it had counted done are listed too.
    - 3: a note-only item counts done only once its text has been checked for a number, condition, Speed or other rule a card could play. The ones checked with nothing to play are in `notes-checked.js`, each with what its note holds; the rest stay listed until built or checked.
    - A spell-cast card's own duration (2026-10-26, `cast-duration.js`): where the item gives the spell another ("with a duration of 1 minute", "but the duration is only 10 minutes", "the effects of … Invisibility spell for 5 minutes", "the duration is unlimited", a longer one from a level on), the card says it and the Effects the spell module's cast makes are set to it. The spell module marks those Effects with the cast spell as their origin. Effects already made when the cast returns are set at once; ones made later, when the spell card is applied, are set from the cast message's mark (PF2e's createItem; scripts/cast-duration.js). Live-checked with Vanishing Wayfinder.
    - Not sustained, or sustained longer (2026-10-27): the Ethereal rune's "doesn't require concentration and lasts for 10 minutes" and Phantom Shroud's "You can Sustain the activation for up to 10 minutes" both give Ethereal Jaunt (sustained, 1 minute) 10 minutes. The shroud's is castDuration.sustained, so the card says "at most, while its user Sustains it". Its "When the activation ends, you return to material form" is the spell's own end.
    - A condition on its user with the cast (2026-10-27, cast-riders.js castSelf): Locket of Sealed Nightmares' "You become fatigued and cast one of the following spells …". Once the spell is cast (scripts/casts.js), PF2e's condition goes on the item's user. The card has a line of its own for it, shown beside several spells too.
    - A spell-cast card's own range (2026-10-26, `cast-range.js`): where the item gives the spell another ("with a range of 30 feet", "except the range is 500 feet", "within your melee range", "planetary"), the card says it, and a target beyond a range in feet or the user's melee reach is refused before the spell module casts (`beyondRange`, scripts/casts.js). Live-checked with Medusa's Scream.
    - A burst's range (2026-10-27): Dazzling Shortbow's "in a 10-foot burst within 60 feet" (Revealing Light's range is 120) and Cloud Pouch's "in a 20-foot burst within 10 feet" are castRange { feet, area }. The card refuses a creature farther than the range plus the burst's radius. A cast's words that say it again are no leftover: its DC as "with a DC of 34" or "The spell DC is 34", and its target as its trigger's creature ("on the creature you just dealt damage to", Talonstrike Blade). Greater Brilliant's "ignores the target's resistances to fire, spirit, and vitality" is PF2e's own rune (`counteract.js` RUNE_OWN).
    - One spell of several its words name (2026-10-27): Spark Dancer's "casts either Fireball or Lightning Bolt, depending on whether the current core would deal fire or electricity damage". Its linked cast was Fireball alone; the words' choices (cast-words.js) are now the card's, and its user picks by the core. "Depending on …" is that pick, not a leftover.
    - A cast's rank and DC from its caster (2026-10-27, `cast-owner.js`): The Inquisitor's "The rank of the spell is one-half your level, rounded down (minimum 5th level), and the save DC is your class DC or spell DC", and The Courtesan's rank. This is plan.castRank { round, min } and plan.castDC { from: "class-spell" }. At Apply, scripts/casts.js casts at its caster's level halved (at least the minimum) and at the higher of its class DC and spell DC. A casting time the activation already is ("but as a two-action spell rather than one that takes 1 minute to cast" on a 2-action activation) is no leftover either.
      Also "half your level" without "one-" (The Tyrant, The Tangled Briar, The Sickness) and "the save DC is equal to your class DC or spell DC". Another spell from a level on (The Sickness's "If you are at least 15th-level, you can instead choose to cast Spiritual Epidemic") is a choice with minLevel. The picker shows it as "(from level 15)", and the card refuses it for a caster below that level. Other wordings are read the same way: The Winged Serpent's "The level of this spell is one-half your level rounded down (minimum 9th)", and a class DC alone (The Betrayal, The Joke: castDC.from "class"). More targets than the spell's own, The Joke's "on up to 10 creatures in range of the spell", is castTargets { max }, and the card refuses more. And the same spell on another creature from a level on: The Winged Serpent's "you can cast foresight on another creature by touch" from 17th level is a choice that isn't on its user, by touch (within its caster's reach) and from that level.
    - Poison Fizz (2026-10-26): an item's own Effect linked in a paragraph of its own after its Secondary Effect is the use's when the use links none (`splitSecondary`). The candy's save bonus is now PF2e's Effect, and Crackling Bubble Gum's Failure Effect stays its pop's.
    - The needs index files a spell-cast card whose leftovers show no other need under "A spell cast with the item's changes", not under "Nothing to automate".
    - Golden Spur (2026-10-26): "You must have line of effect to the space" is `teleport.lineOfEffect`. The teleport card refuses a space past a wall that blocks movement (Foundry's move collision from the centre of its user's space; `wallBetween`, scripts/teleport.js) and says so on the card. Live-checked with a wall on the lab scene.
    - Resolute Shield's Stand Firm (2026-10-26): "You and the shield each take any remaining damage" is `bonus.shieldShare`. With its resistance 24 on, what still gets through to its user is dealt to the shield's Hit Points too (the core's damage listener; scripts/shield-share.js). Live-checked: 30 bludgeoning, 6 to each.
    - Enveloping Light (2026-10-26, `cast-riders.js`): the item's "For 5 rounds … with the effects of a 1st-rank Light spell" is the cast's own duration, and "At the end of each of your turns during this time, you regain 1d4 Hit Points" is `plan.turnHeal`. Its user gets an Effect for the 5 rounds that heals through PF2e at the end of each of its turns (PF2e's pf2e.endTurn; scripts/turn-heal.js). Live-checked.
    - A spell-cast card's note that says again what the item's own PF2e rules give (Elemental Wayfinder (Fire)'s resistance 5 to fire) isn't a leftover.
    - A spell cast with the item's changes (2026-10-27, `cast-changes.js`, innermost in both chains so `withCastNotes` reads what's left):
      - Lines of the cast card (`plan.castLines`, shown before its notes): what's only its user's, the map's or the GM's. Headwrap of Wisdom's Augury "from your own instincts" (flavor), Faerie Queen's Bower's protected area from its user's square and Helm of the Tides' bigger area at night (where its user lays it), Malefic Mirror's owner's domain and the viewer's familiarity (off the map; the card's DC as given), Perfection's First Step's 10 minutes of writing beforehand, Rime Foil's pairing, and Wildwood Ink's recharge by its user's own One with Plants (the activations card warns the GM of a second use, as of any).
      - A rank by the part used: Fan of Soothing Winds' beads are three choices of Heal (4th, 3rd and 2nd rank in the base fan), six uses a day ("once per day per bead"), at the undead's DC (28, 34 greater). Its 30-foot cone and its air trait stay notes, so the fan stays partial: the spell module's cast would have to take another area and trait.
      - Another DC on a condition its user knows: Rime Foil's "the DC is instead DC 24" after a shattered plan's damage is a second choice of Phantom Prison at DC 24. The picker shows each choice's own DC.
      - A kineticist's own numbers (`castImpulse`, each choice's `element`): Gate Attenuator's cast uses the better of the item's DC and attack and the caster's impulse ones, when PF2e's `flags.system.kineticist.elements` include the spell's element (as the kineticist module's own button does). Its "gains that element's trait" is a line where every spell has the trait already (the greater and major attenuators). Not the base one: Thunderstrike has no metal trait, so that sentence stays a note and the plan partial.
      - Elemental Wayfinder (Air)'s Gentle Landing once every 10 minutes, beside its once-a-day Lightning Bolt: a one-off (a second choice with a frequency of its own).
      - A weapon's section for some wielders that the activation before it took in (Blessed Reformer's Redeemer Wielder, `wielder-riders.js`) isn't the cast's. Its critical hit on an unholy creature (–10 feet to its Speeds and can't Step, until the end of its wielder's next turn) is the weapon's own rider. Live (`scripts/rune-strikes.js`), it plays only for a champion with the redemption class feature.
    - One of a kind of PF2e's spells, picked on the cast card (2026-10-27, `spell-picks.js`; the spells index now keeps each spell's rarity, book, save, target words and traditions, live too): Cantrip Deck (Full Pack) is the 25 common Player Core cantrips, and the 5-pack any common cantrip (the pack's own is its user's pick), each at 1st rank, DC 15, +5. Mage's Hat (Greater) is each arcane summon spell castable at 4th rank, Summon Animal and Summon Elemental first. Bleachguard Doll has Light at 3rd rank, Prestidigitation, and its other cantrip from the common ones with no attack roll or save. Its once-a-day spell, the creator's pick of hundreds, is a note, so it stays partial. A choice whose target is no creature (Light's object) needs no target first.
    - Calligraphic Illumination's "Attempt to Recall Knowledge about any one creature or object … with a +2 item bonus" is PF2e's Recall Knowledge on the item-action card (`item-actions.js`, 2026-10-27).
    - Alchemist's Haversack's versatile vial (2026-10-27, `focus.js`): the Focus Point card with PF2e's special resource `versatile-vials` one higher, up to its maximum, through PF2e's `updateResource`.
    - Detect Anathema Fulu (2026-10-27, `anathema-burn.js`): the direct-damage card of 4d6 fire at its wearer, and the brand a note Effect with no end. Its going off at an anathema act and the warning first are the GM's call; targeting the wearer is its user's.
    - One-offs (2026-10-27): Wandmender's wand cast again, Ruby Capacitor's temporary staff charges, Ring of Maniacal Devices' rune trap, Mind-Swap Potion, Stone Circle's ritual checks, Candle of Invocation's spell slots, Laurel of the Empath's month-long bonus and borrowed reactions, and the dragon talismans' set. The scutcheon's set sentence is a line of each way's card, since the ways card shows no notes of its own.
- **Ships and vehicles** (`src/compiler/vehicles.js`, 2026-10-27):
  - A vehicle an item becomes: PF2e's vehicle actor, linked, conjured by the ability module as a creature is (its token beside its user, gone at 0 Hit Points, dismissed from its card). This covers Marvelous Miniature (Boat)'s Cutter for a day, Folding Boat's Rowboat and the greater one's Cutter (no time: folding it back is the card's Dismiss), and Anglerfish Lantern (Submersible)'s Bathysphere for an hour. Their other sentences are lines of the card: where they can be used, their forms and what's stored, crew, ejection, broken. `conjured.js` leaves vehicles to this.
  - Feather Token (Swan Boat) and Wind-Up Cart have only their text's numbers, so each is a marker token: the swan boat with swim Speed 50 for a day, the cart with AC 15, 24 HP and 30 feet for a minute. Hardness and Break Threshold are an object's line, and the cart's moves are the map's.
  - A note Effect on the vehicle its user targets (targeting "1 vehicle", sent to the presser's targets by `route.js`; PF2e's vehicles take Effects): Feather Token (Anchor)'s day stopped, Kraken Figurehead's tentacles for 10 minutes (the Swim penalty in an aura of the ship's own length, which no zone reads). Feather Token (Fan) and Wind At Your Back are ways cards: the vessel's Speed bonus as a note (PF2e's vehicle Speeds are words no rule changes), or Gust of Wind at DC 20 on the cast card, or the group's longer Hustle on its user's note.
  - About Face!'s 180-degree turn is the map's: its token turned. Mariner's Mien's Set the Course is Quickened for its minute, with a note Effect beside it saying what the extra action is for and that it ends on leaving the vehicle.
  - The marvelous miniatures' shared flavor ("the camping set features the boat, …") no longer reads as a vehicle (`needs.js`), so the Campfire, Chest and Ladder are card lines.
  - One-offs: Kraken Figurehead (Wracking)'s Grab Them! (the save card stays), Hongbao of Many Things' twelve slips, and Violin of the Waves' crew penalty (its calmed weather is out of combat).
    - Its user's own Strike before or after a teleport or move, as PF2e makes it (2026-10-26: Gloaming Shard, Mercurial Mantle, Purloining Cloak, Skyrider Sword, Paws of the Grogrisant): a line of the card ending "(its own actions)", not a note (`OWN_STRIKE`, teleport.js; move.js). One the item changes stays a note: its damage, a bonus, a condition, staying undetected, a free action after it.
    - Psyche Salts (2026-10-26, `cast-riders.js` castBonus): "… with a +17 counteract check modifier and a +2 item bonus to saving throws against mental effects for 10 minutes". The cast plays the counteract modifier, and the bonus is an Effect of PF2e's FlatModifier rule on the creature the spell is cast on, for its time (`giveCastBonus`, scripts/casts.js). Live-checked.
    - Iris of the Sky's A Wish Granted With Inevitable Fire (2026-10-26): "If the attack is a failure, you take 5 persistent fire damage" is `rollTwice.onFailure`. A mark beside the roll-twice Effect, then that roll's failure (not a critical failure) deals it to its user through PF2e (scripts/fortune.js). Live-checked: PF2e gives a creature at 0 Hit Points no persistent damage.
    - Warrior's Training Ring (2026-10-26): "If you are an expert in any weapon, you instead gain a +4 circumstance bonus to the attack roll" is the modifier's `ifExpert`. Its user's attack proficiencies are looked at when the card is applied, and the card says it. Live-checked: +2 untrained, +4 expert.
    - Coronet of Stars (2026-10-27): "The triggering creature receives the effects of Breath of Life, except they recover 7d8 Hit Points" is worded as the creature regaining 7d8 Hit Points (item-data.js). Its card heals the creature its presser targets ("The triggering creature regains", the recipients' reader). The core's recovery from Dying keeps it from dying as the healing lands, and the spell's own 4d8 isn't cast.
    - Boots of the Blight's Swift Sidestep (2026-10-27): "If you succeed at the saving throw, you become Quickened for 1 minute, but can use the additional action only to Stride" is `bonus.onSuccess`. The save that spends the bonus gives its user the ability module's timed Quickened on a success, and the line says what the action is for (scripts/fortune.js). Live-checked.
    - Thrice-Fried Mudwings (2026-10-27): its reaction's "The first time … your fly Speed becomes 15 feet, or half your Speed, whichever is lower … After the second time, your mudwings are shattered entirely, ending the effect" is `bonus.speedSteps`. The item's fly Speed Effect on its user is lowered the first time and ended the second (`stepSpeed`, scripts/fortune.js). Before, the second sentence was dropped unread. Live-checked.
    - Readied bonuses' other sentences (2026-10-27, an audit of what the reader dropped): a move, a Hardness, a flat check, what ends it or can't be done now count as rule sentences. A resistance-and-bonus reading keeps them as notes too. Skitter Knot's roots and Pyrefeather Cloak's flat check were dropped before. "You can't use this ability to …" (Deflecting Branch) is `bonus.limits`, a line of the card. Pyrefeather Cloak's "If you are taking persistent fire damage, you can immediately attempt a flat check to end the persistent damage" is `bonus.recovery`: PF2e's own recovery check as the card is applied (`recover`, now in scripts/foundry.js).
    - Counteract cards' other rules (2026-10-27, the same audit): every rule sentence after the counteract is a note of its card, not only some. Draconal Mask's Truesight and Fu Water's Sickened were dropped before. The card counts partial while those notes leave a rule it doesn't play (`counteractLeaves`, `withCounteractLeaves` at the end of both chains). Card lines don't count: what to tick or give, the GM's numbers, a limit, a timing, a charm's spent spell, the GM's control of a summoned creature (the user's answer D of 2026-10-13). Nor does a wait its recharge holds. Before, a counteract card was never partial. Fu Water's "You're then Sickened 1" is `counteract.then`, given after the roll. Draconal Mask's "You then gain the effects of truesight for 1 minute" is `counteract.thenSpell`: the spell module casts it on the wearer and its Effects are set to the minute (scripts/counteract.js `castAfter`). A counteract beside another card keeps only its own notes. Moon Radish Soup's "you gain temporary immunity to its effects for 24 hours" is now an immunity the tracker holds. Live-checked: Fu Water, Draconal Mask (the spell module's caster-choices dialog applied), Pyrefeather Cloak.
    - Zones' saves checked for what they leave (2026-10-27, `withZoneUntraced`, honest.js): each save plan a zone holds gets the untraced-rule check builtins skipped before, with the zone's own fields in the trace. Its untraced sentences become the zone's notes, and the zone counts partial when any is a rule, or when a save's plan was already partial (Tar Rocket Snare, Deadweight Snare). Newly listed are Fire Box's cone, Puff Dragon's gas, Piercing Whistle Snare's 10-foot burst and its trigger's longer deafness, Spirit Trap's 10-by-10 area, Sanctified Beans' rows and Arctic Worm Chitin Shield's adjacent cold. An example sentence ("For example, …", Trip Snare) isn't a rule.
    - Bleeding Spines Snare (2026-10-27): "After the initial trigger, the spines … repeatedly for 1 minute, forcing any creature that enters the space or ends its turn in the space to take damage …" is the area the snare leaves (the ability module's `zone.then`): its square for 1 minute with the same basic Reflex save on entering or ending a turn there. Live-checked.
    - The zone check also takes a rule sentence with no number to trace it by (2026-10-27, `ZONE_RULE`): an Interact action, a flat check, a roll twice keeping the worse, damage to an item that ignores its Hardness. Fire-Douse Snare, Grease Snare, Rusting Snare, Dust Pods, Glittering Snare and Poison Barbs Snare now count partial with those sentences as notes.
    - Item-action cards' lines after the action (2026-10-27, `actionLeaves`): before, they were never counted. A line that says a rule the card doesn't play now makes it partial. A line its user keeps to doesn't: no hand free needed, no language penalty, a larger target, the action said again, its multiple attack penalty, its own movement (Onyx Panther's full-Speed Sneak, Blast Boots' longer jumps, Monkey Pin's Climb), The Bear's Strike limit. Nor does what its riders or damage play. Newly played: dice on a success, "double on a critical hit" (Bracers of Hammers' 10d6, Constricting Meteor's 1d10+7; `SUCCESS_DICE`); a degree the item changes, acted on and said (Ensnaring Disk, Eye of Enlightenment, Monkey Pin, Jug of Fond Remembrance; `action.degree`); a condition on its user after it (the Jug's Stupefied 1 for 3 rounds; `action.selfAfter`, the ability module's timed condition). Left partial: Binding Coil's Escape DC, Curled Cure Gel's condition lowered, Crying Angel Pendant's results, Ulfen Shieldbreaker's +2, Admonishing Band's immunity clause.
    - Conjured creatures as their item's text has them (2026-10-27, conjured.js + scripts/zones.js `finishConjured`). Once the ability module conjures it, the item module trades its creature type for the traits the item gives (Fossil Fragments' construct and earth) and adds its immunities (PF2e's immunity entries; by hand before). What its entry adds goes on the made creature too: the Onyx Dog's +4 to Track and darkvision (an Effect of PF2e's rules), the Petrified Wood tree's lost weaknesses and Immobilized. A conjure card now counts partial for a line left by hand, or for a rule of the creature's the made token doesn't have (the Amber Mosquito's Blood Drain, a figurine's week once slain, the Onyx Dog's scent). Before, a conjure card was never partial. Live-checked with the Petrified Wood tree.
    - Ending an item's effect early (2026-10-27, end-early.js + scripts/end-early.js): Fire-Douse Snare's flat checks (DC 10, then DC 5, then outright on the third Interact), Dust Pods' DC 5 flat check, Grease Snare's and Hoof Stakes Snare's three Interact actions. These are `plan.endEarly` on the zone's save. Once the ability module's save card applies, an end-early card lists each creature it affected. Pressed for one, its next try is made; ending deletes the item's Effects on it that grant the conditions it names (the ability module's timed conditions take their conditions with them). The zone check counts those sentences as played. Fire-Douse Snare and Dust Pods are now whole. Live-checked with Fire-Douse Snare (2026-10-27): a failed DC 10 try, then a DC 5 success, Blinded and Dazzled ended.
    - A save row's own way out (2026-10-27, end-early.js readRowEndEarly + strikes.js): Blindpepper Bolt's success row lets the dazzled creature spend one manipulate action wiping its eyes — the save plan's endEarly with degrees ["success"]; the end-early card after its save card lists only the creatures that rolled a success, and its try reads "1 manipulate action spent".
    - A snare's mark (2026-10-27, snares.js markedRows): Marking Snare's "marks the creature" rows (failure, and the critical failure beside its Blinded) each give a 24-hour note Effect, Marked: the +2 circumstance bonus to Track it until it's washed off — others' checks against it, words on the Effect.
    - Greased (2026-10-27, snares.js greasedRows): Grease Snare's failure and critical failure each give a note Effect for the Clumsy's time (1 minute, or 1 hour) with PF2e's RollTwice (keep lower) and AdjustDegreeOfSuccess (a failure one degree worse) on Thievery. On a critical failure, the end-early card's way to end it is a 1-minute activity (endEarly.byDegree `{ kind: "activity", minutes }`). Its squares become difficult terrain once it's set off: a 10-foot square left behind (zone.then) until wiped, the setter's choice of squares a line of its card. Live-checked, with the critical failure's 1-hour Clumsy a timed Effect the activity removes too (ability 1c792ce times conditions of hours and days; the next item release needs the ability release that has it).
    - Stakes in its feet (2026-10-27, snares.js stakedRows; scripts/foot-damage.js): Hoof Stakes Snare's failure and critical failure deal the snare's 2d6 in full. Before, the ability compiler had also read the foot Strike's 2d6 (4d6) as the row's own damage. The rows give the Speed penalty (–5, –10) for the minute and a note Effect with PF2e's RollOption `air-foot-damage:<dice>:<type>`. On the active GM's client, the core's applyDamage listener sees damage from a Strike of that creature's whose item is a foot (Foot, Hoof, Kick, Stomp, Trample). If the creature hit loses Hit Points, the stakes' dice are rolled and applied to the striker. Three Interact actions, its own or an ally's, end it (the end-early card). Live-checked.
    - While its persistent damage lasts (2026-10-27, snares.js staticRows): Static Snare's failure rows get PF2e's Effect: Static Snare. Its critical failure also gets a note Effect granting Hidden while the creature is Invisible (dust clinging to it). Both are `whilePersistent: "electricity"` (ability cf8d902), so they go with the creature's last persistent electricity damage, and a creature that took none gets neither. Live-checked. The next item release needs the ability release that has cf8d902.
    - A critical hit's extra cut (2026-10-27, readied-bonus.js onlyCritExtra + scripts/half-damage.js beforeCritical): Indomitable Keepsake's 10 off the triggering critical hit (its Greater's 20, its Major's 30, by their own sentences), never below a normal hit — bonus.reduceCrit { value }, not resistance (PF2e's all-damage resistance would come off each damage type); the core's damage listener takes the lower of the value and half the hit's total (its doubling's part) off its first instance once, nothing off a damage PF2e marks as no critical hit; the readied Effect is spent by the damage.
    - Swallowed, then spat out to fall (2026-10-27, spit-out.js, last before the user's rulings): Worm Vial's Eat — its critical failure's damage doubled ("doubled on a critical failure"), the 20-foot fall half its feet in bludgeoning and Prone on both failing rows (as the ability module's Fling), the spit a placed move within 30 feet (its user picks the space), its range the 15 feet its Eat says, not the Burrow's 80; the Burrow a card line (a tunnel on the map).
    - A snare's setter worse off (2026-10-27, snares.js setterPlan; ability a7db06e zone.burst.othersOnly): Piercing Whistle Snare's damage "to creatures within 10 feet" is a burst from its square for the others, and "The triggering creature is deafened for 1 minute on a failure, or 1 hour on a critical failure" its own save card with those times; the burst leaves it out. And honest.js withZoneUntraced now checks a burst's plan too, a sentence counting as left only when none of the zone's cards plays it (Sanctified Beans' two cards each play what the other leaves).
    - A trap set off by opening it (2026-10-27, opened.js; snares.js OPENED): Fire Box isn't a square stepped into — its basic Reflex save card for those its 15-foot cone reaches, used when someone opens it; its aim (the GM's way, at random, always through the opener) a line of the card.
    - What a row makes a creature drop of what it holds (2026-10-27, drop-held.js near the end of compileItem; src/live/drop-card.js; scripts/drop-held.js): Deadweight Snare's critical failure "drops any metallic items it's holding" — plan.dropHeld { degrees, metallic }; once the ability module's card applies, the drop card lists each creature of those degrees with what it holds, the metal-looking ones ticked (PF2e's material, else a name word like steel or wooden, else a weapon's group: swords, axes, knives, picks, hammers, flails, polearms, spears, darts, firearms), the GM's to change; Drop puts the ticked ones on the ground (carried "dropped").
    - A property rune's DC and dice (2026-10-27, strikes.js RUNE_DC; strike-card.js; scripts/strikes.js): Storm Arrow shot from a weapon with a shock rune — strike.runeDC, its save DC 27 when the Strike's weapon has that rune; strike.withoutRune, PF2e's DamageAlteration with the rune's slug (PF2e slugs a property rune's dice by the rune) setting those dice to none for that Strike, its readied Effect kept to the damage roll. And bombs.js bombSave keeps the save's own damage components when its rows deal a share of them (Storm Arrow's 3d12 electricity, Corpsecaller Round's 4d10 void had none before — the card dealt nothing).
    - Lights put out beside a Strike's extra damage (2026-10-27, strikes.js withLightsOut): Void Sickle's Light Harvest — its +1d6 void the readied Strike's; "All non-magical light sources within 30 feet are immediately extinguished" a line of its card (strike.lines), the map's lights to turn off, as a tunnel is drawn on it.
    - A new save against an affliction beside a flat check (2026-10-27, recovery.js afflictionSave; scripts/foundry.js afflictionSaves): Quenching Potion's "and attempt a new saving throw against any poison affecting you. Poison can't progress to a worse stage due to this saving throw" — each poison Pseudo Afflictions tracks on the drinker, its save at its DC: a success one stage lower, a critical success two (ended below stage 1), a failure no change (noWorse); Pseudo Afflictions gives the new stage's conditions.
    - An aura over PF2e's own aura Effect (2026-10-27, aura-zones.js negates form + AROUND_WIELDER; permanent.js nativeAura): Nightmare's Lament's Fuming Cloak — the zone on its wielder for the minute with PF2e's Aura: Fuming Cloak as its ownEffect (the concealment PF2e's), "A creature that begins its turn in the area becomes Sickened 2 (… negates) and is then temporarily immune …" its turn-start save with the immunity; its wielder seeing through the smoke words of the card; those holding their breath, not breathing or immune to poison the GM's call.
    - Pulling free with a skill check (2026-10-27, end-early.js readPullFree; strikes.js; scripts/end-early.js): Antler Arrow's bow critical specialization — the save's endEarly { how: { kind: "check", skill: "athletics", dc: 10, criticalDC: 15 } } for its immobilizing rows; the end-early card rolls PF2e's Athletics check at that DC (an Interact action), the harder DC a tick the card sets after a critical hit (the archer's specialization the card can't see, its user's to untick); pulled free, the Immobilized (a plain condition when it had no time) removed.
    - Pinned by an area's critical failure (2026-10-27, end-early.js withPinningWays): Enfilading Arrow's critical failure Immobilizes only when its archer has the bow critical specialization, which the card can't see. A ways card: the burst's save without it, and with it (that row's Immobilized and an end-early card for the Athletics DC 10 Interact to pull the missile free). Live-checked.
    - A penalty to the triggering action (2026-10-27, action-penalty.js): Stifling Shield's Silence Falls. Success and failure put a –1 / –2 status penalty on the creature's checks, spell DCs and class DCs (PF2e's `check`, `spell-dc` and `class-dc`) until the end of its turn, with a note Effect saying it counts only for the triggering action. On a critical failure, a note Effect says its action is disrupted and its result ignored, as the ability module's reaction cards say. Live-checked.
    - Spells it can't cast (2026-10-27, cast-block.js): Moritype's rows say which ranks the creature can't cast for 1 round: its highest, its 2 highest, or all but cantrips and focus spells. No PF2e rule stops a cast, so each is a note Effect for the round, and its target is "1 living creature".
    - Ammunition in the throat (2026-10-27, throat.js): Stonethroat Ammunition's success is its own −2 status to Athletics and to `jaws-attack` and `fangs-attack` for 1 round. PF2e's Effect: Stonethroat Ammunition (Success) has those selectors but no end, so it isn't used. Its failure (and the critical failure beside its Enfeebled 2) is a note Effect, "can't use its jaws or fangs Strikes or Swallow Whole", until the end of its next turn. Live-checked.
    - A bonus only against metal (2026-10-27, strikes.js ammoStrike; src/live/strike-card.js isMetalTarget): Lodestone Pellet gives +1 circumstance to ranged Strikes against creatures made of metal or wearing metal armor. Its Strike's attack has `against: "metal"`. When the Strike card is readied, the creature targeted then is checked: the metal trait, or worn armor of the chain, plate or composite group (not leather, wood or ceramic) or of a metal material. If it is, the bonus is whole. Otherwise PF2e's `target:trait:metal` is the only way it applies, since PF2e has no roll option for a target's armor. Live-checked against full plate.
    - Hiding in the open (2026-10-27, camouflage.js; scripts/ends-on-hostile.js): Camouflage Dye's "can Hide or Sneak without cover or concealment" has no PF2e rule, so it's words on its Effect for its time (10 minutes for the Lesser, 1 minute for the Greater), in place of the reminder of the whole text. The Lesser's end is two PF2e RollOptions on that Effect, `air-ends-on:hostile` and `air-ends-on:long-move`. On the active GM's client it ends after an attack roll, a damage roll or a spell cast by that creature, or a move of more than half its land Speed, and its Hidden and Undetected go with it. Live-checked, both ways.
    - A counteract check at the hit (2026-10-27, strike-counteract.js; scripts/strikes.js counteractOnHit; scripts/counteract.js postCounteractAt). Awakened Silver Shot's hit posts the counteract card at the creature hit, and only that creature can be picked. It looks for polymorph Effects (its spell's traits count too). A failure counts as a success against a devil or werecreature (`failToSuccess`, the traits of the creature hit). Counteracting Change Shape leaves a note Effect: it can't use Change Shape for 1 round, or 1 minute after a critical success. A devil or werecreature with no polymorph Effect gets Enfeebled 1 for 1 minute instead. Awakened Cold Iron Shot's card lists the creatures near for its user to name the one it believes is enchanted. On that creature it finds the Effects whose origin is the creature hit. A demon or fey with no Effect on any other creature in the scene is Stupefied 1 for 1 minute instead. Both live-checked (Silver: Change Shape countered, Enfeebled instead; Cold Iron: the demon's charm on an ally countered, Stupefied instead).
    - An item that fuses to its user once used (2026-10-27, fuses.js; scripts/becomes.js): Sluggish Bracelet's activation gives its −10 feet of Speed for 1 minute. Then `plan.item.becomes` changes the bracelet's own PF2e rules for good: its Acrobatics FlatModifier goes, a −5-foot status penalty to land Speed comes, and it's flagged fused. That happens once; a later activation changes nothing more. Live-checked (25 → 10 feet).
    - A condition's penalties ignored until it's given again (2026-10-27, fatigue-ignored.js): Ring of the Weary Traveler's 10 minutes of fatigue ignored were already `plan.item.suppress` (PF2e's AdjustModifier on the condition's own `fatigued` modifiers). Its "If something would cause you to become fatigued again … the suppression ends" is `endsOnAgain`: a RollOption `air-ends-on:fatigued` on that Effect. scripts/ends-on-hostile.js ends the Effect when PF2e creates that condition on the creature again, and the −1 comes back. PF2e doesn't add a condition the creature already has, so a new fatigue while it's still fatigued sets nothing off. Live-checked.
    - A readied bonus that climbs with each try (2026-10-27, readied-bonus.js withEscalatingBonus; scripts/fortune.js climb): Metronomic Hammer's +1 item bonus to Trip stays for the turn. After each Trip roll that isn't a success, critical success or critical failure, scripts/fortune.js raises its FlatModifier by 1, up to +3. One of those outcomes, or a Trip at another target than the last, puts it back to +1. The roll at a new target still gets the raised bonus, since it's set before the roll. Live-checked (+1 → +2 → +3 → +1).
    - Smoke where its caster was and where it arrives (2026-10-27, cast-smoke.js; scripts/cast-smoke.js): Charlatan's Cape's and Blink Blade's Translocate. Before the cast, the cast card watches for the next move of its user's token, for two minutes. That move (the spell's teleport, by hand in the spell module) starts the ability module's zone at the space it left and at the one it arrives in: Concealed inside, until the end of its user's next turn, the wind a line of its card. Live-checked.
    - A spell with no target (2026-10-27, cast-words.js fillCast): the ability compiler's casts of a spell PF2e gives neither a target nor an area (Translocate, Summon Animal, Dragon Wings…) get `targets: false`, so the cast card no longer asks for targets first (60 activations).
    - A worse save against the spell for a kind (2026-10-27, cast-riders.js withCastDegree; scripts/casts.js; src/live/card.js castDegreeEffectSource): Radiant Spark's Dominate makes celestials "get an outcome one degree of success worse than the result of their save". Before the cast, each target with that trait gets PF2e's AdjustDegreeOfSuccess on its Will save, flagged firstDegree so scripts/first-degree.js removes it after that save (a minute at most). Live-checked (success → failure, none on a creature that isn't a celestial).
    - Creatures of a kind immune to the cast, and others worse off (2026-10-27, cast-riders.js withCastTraits; scripts/casts.js; src/live/card.js castPenaltySource): Castrovel's Beacon's Starlight Burst (Vibrant Pattern). Before the cast, the cast card leaves out the targeted creatures with the elf trait, which are immune, and gives those with the demon trait PF2e's −2 item FlatModifier to Will saves for the spell's minute. For an area cast with no creatures targeted, its line says to target them. Live-checked.
    - A spell's effect around the creature hit (2026-10-27, strike-counteract.js withEffectAround; scripts/strikes.js): Blade of Fallen Stars' starlight shot "creates a Faerie Fire effect in a 5-foot emanation centered on the target". That's `strike.cast` with `around: 5`: the spell module's cast at the creature hit and every creature within 5 feet of it. Live-checked, with its plan readied on a shortbow (the gun sword's ranged use needs PF2e 8.6's loaded rounds).
    - A persistent bleed that grows with each failure (2026-10-27, growing-bleed.js; scripts/bleed-grows.js): Bloodgorger Scythe's Root in Blood now deals 1 persistent bleed. The ability compiler had read `@Damage[1[bleed]]` as plain bleed dealt once. A note Effect lasts while the bleed does (ability cf8d902 whilePersistent), with the RollOption `air-bleed-grows:bleed:1:3`. On the active GM's client the Effect is marked at the end of the creature's turn, once its persistent damage and recovery check come. At the start of its next turn, a marked Effect whose bleed is still there (the check failed) raises the bleed by 1, up to 3. Live-checked (1 → 2).
    - Terrain for that creature alone (2026-10-27, snares.js cautiousRows): on a success, Pit Illusion Snare's creature treats "the snare's square and all adjacent squares as difficult terrain until the end of its turn". No PF2e rule or zone makes terrain difficult for one creature, so it's words on its Effect until the end of its turn.
    - A teleport to a square fired at (2026-10-27, teleport.js orSquare; scripts/teleport.js): Dimension Shot teleports its user beside the creature it hits. With no creature targeted ("If you fire at a square"), the space is measured from its user instead: its held ranged weapon's farthest range (six range increments) and the square beyond. The card's lines say so. Live-checked (50 feet, no target).
    - A grab that works as the Grab creature ability (2026-10-27, grab-ability.js; item-data.js fillOwnerDCs; scripts/foundry.js ownerDCsOf): Scizore of the Crab's target is Grabbed until the end of its user's next turn. Its Escape (`escape: { ownerDC: "athletics" }`) is against its user's Athletics DC, filled in as the card is made, and the ability module ends the grab on a successful Escape. The grab's end when its user moves away is words on a note Effect for that time. Live-checked (Escape DC 10 = the wielder's Athletics DC, ended by an Escape).
    - A consumable's later activation, its own PF2e Effect (2026-10-27, later-own.js): Aurochs Jerky's Aurochs' Endurance, a free action "Trigger You begin your turn and are Fatigued". It's now `plan.again`, the eater's own card for PF2e's Effect: Aurochs' Endurance. The card is kept open for the jerky's hour and pressed each time its trigger comes. That replaces the old note "is linked too: the GM's". Live-checked.
    - Roots that carry and shield their dying user (2026-10-27, readied-bonus.js withRootsShield): Skitter Knot's readied Effect has its +1 circumstance AC plus the roots' Hardness (5, 10, 20) as resistance to physical damage. Both are spent by the first attack against it (`bonus.against`, "after which the roots crumble"). The roots carrying it 10, 15 or 20 feet is a line of its card. Unit-tested, not live-checked.
    - A way to end it for each row (2026-10-27, end-early.js readRowWays; snares.js): Glittering Snare's modifiers' "until it uses an Interact action to remove the glitter" / "until it spends a 2-action activity, which has the manipulate trait, …" / the critical failure's 3-action one — endEarly.byDegree, the end-early card's try for each creature by the degree it rolled (src/live/end-early-card.js howFor); its "or open the container" a line of the snare's card.
    - And on an item's own save card (2026-10-27, end-early.js withRowWays near the end of compileItem, each way of a ways card too): Crackling Bubble Gum's pop, "until it, or other creatures helping, spends a total of 3 Interact actions removing it" — its failing rows' endEarly, the try "3 Interact actions spent (by it or creatures helping, not all at once)".
    - A cast spell ended early by a save (2026-10-27, end-early.js readTugFree; strikes.js castOnHit; scripts/strikes.js; scripts/end-early.js postEndEarlyAt): Silencing Ammunition — its Silence the spell module's (castFromItem with saveFirst), then the end-early card for the creature it landed on, its try a DC 25 Will save (an Interact action), PF2e's "Spell Effect: Silence" the Effect it ends; its first save's -2 circumstance after a critical hit (saveFirst.criticalPenalty) an Effect on the creature for that save, gone once it's rolled.
    - A missile that reveals the creature it stays in (2026-10-27, strikes.js withBeacon; scripts/strikes.js): Beacon Shot's hit — a note Effect for the minute, PF2e's Hidden granted while the creature is Invisible, its other concealment gone in its words; pulled out (an Interact action and a DC 20 Athletics check) by the end-early card posted after the hit's riders, its Effect ended.
    - Barbs that hurt each long Stride (2026-10-27, snares.js barbedRows; scripts/move-damage.js): Poison Barbs Snare's failing rows give a note Effect for the minute carrying PF2e's RollOption air-move-damage:1d4:poison:half; after Foundry moves a token (its moveToken hook, the movement's passed distance), a creature with it that went farther than half its land Speed takes 1d4 poison through PF2e — a Stride split into several moves counts each by itself; its 3 Interact actions (its own or an ally's) the end-early card's.
    - A pause of the item's own extra damage (2026-10-27, permanent.js withPausedPassive, last): Skeletal Claw's "As long as any of these temporary Hit Points remain, the skeletal claw doesn't deal additional void damage" — that damage is no rule PF2e's data plays (the item has no DamageDice), its user's to add by hand, so the pause is a line of the card; the temporary Hit Points the card's.
    - Damage as the item's own Strike would deal it, of another type (2026-10-27, strike-as.js; src/live/strike-as.js; scripts/live.js postCardFor): Sinew-Song's Cutting Cadenza — plan.strikeDamage { as: "sonic", rows: { success 1, failure 1, critical failure 2 } }; as its card is made, PF2e's damage formula for its user's Strike with the item (StrikeData.damage({ getFormula: true })), its instances summed under the one type, the rows' shared damage beside their own bleed ("mixed"); no such Strike on its user, a whisper to roll it by hand.
    - A higher rank on a condition its user knows (2026-10-27, cast-owner.js withRankIf): Lion's Pelt's Contact Ally, "If you and the target share an insignia, the spell is heightened to 3rd rank" — a second choice of Message at rank 3, its condition on the cast card's picker, the user's to pick.
    - A Strike whose hit sets off one of several songs (2026-10-27, songs.js): Singing Shortbow's Harmonic Shot — the ways card, a way for each song; each the Strike card with strike.around, that song's card at every creature within 10 feet of the one hit (Song of Soothing's 3d10 healing and PF2e's Effect: Song of Soothing, an automatic card; Song of Suffering's basic Fortitude save); whether the creature hit is affected its user's (the card's Use my targets).
    - A toxin's or disease's stage lowered, one of its choices (2026-10-27, permanent.js chosenEffectsPlan STAGE_DOWN; scripts/foundry.js lowerAfflictionStage): Heartripper Blade's "reduce the stage of one toxin or disease you're suffering by one stage (this can't reduce the stage below stage 1 or cure the affliction)" — the ways card's third way, item.afflictionStage; the item card lowers the Pseudo Afflictions poison or disease at the highest stage above 1 by one, never below 1.
    - An opening sentence saying what the item is made of (2026-10-27, flavor.js flavorSentence): a consumable's first sentence after its Activate lines starting "A"/"An" as well as "This"/"These" — Alchemist's Damper's mercury tube — flavor when it has no link, number or rule word.
    - Countered outright when its source is far lower (2026-10-27, counteract.js OUTRIGHT_BELOW; counteract-card.js; scripts/counteract.js): The Teamster's "If the source of the condition is more than 4 levels lower than your current level, The Teamster automatically counters the condition" — counteract.outrightBelow; each condition row on its card gets a tick (the source's level its user's to know), and a ticked one ends with no DC, rank or roll.
    - A dragon's breath type and area, lines of the card (2026-10-27, type-choice.js readDragonType lines): Orb of Dragonkind's breath — its type the card's picker, its cone or line its user's to aim and target; neither a rule left, the plan whole.
    - Its user's own actions no card can take for them, as lines (2026-10-27): Dragonfly Fulu's jump as a single action without the Stride is a line of its card ending "(its own actions)", which counts as a card line. Leaper's Elixir (both grades), all of whose note is that jump, is checked as having nothing to play (`NOTES_CHECKED`).
    - Laurel of the Empath (2026-10-27): "You take half damage from the triggering attack" is `bonus.halfDamage`, a readied Effect. The core's damage listener halves the damage roll as PF2e applies it (PF2e's own `alter(0.5, 0)`; a number rounded down; scripts/half-damage.js), and the damage spends the Effect. Live-checked: 20 bludgeoning taken as 10.
    - Ended by its user's own doing (2026-10-27, bonus.endsOn, scripts/ends-on.js): Scale of Igroon's "This bonus ends if you Activate another ability or use the Shield Block reaction". The readied Effect ends at its user's next item activation through this module (scripts/activations.js) and at its next Shield Block. PF2e applies a Shield Block through applyDamage with shieldBlockRequest, which the core's damage listener sees.
    - Healing just after the damage (2026-10-27, bonus.healAfter, scripts/heal-after.js): Crown of the Fire Eater heals the lower of 15 (25, 35) and the triggering attack's fire damage before resistance, after taking that damage. The core's damage listener counts the damage roll's fire instances as PF2e applies it, then heals the creature through PF2e. The readied Effect is spent by the damage, as its fire resistance 15 is.
    - An affliction under a heading of its own after the activations (2026-10-27, permanent.js `headedAfflictions`): Devil's Trident's Sewer Haze, with its traits, Saving Throw, Onset and stages, goes to the activation that names it. It's given in the inline form the ability compiler reads, so its card hands the disease to Pseudo Afflictions under its own name and traits. The exposure's save is the activation's DC 25; the disease's DC 23 is its stages'. Before, that activation read nothing.
    - Soulpiercer's Extend (2026-10-27): "Your Strikes with the soulpiercer have a reach of 15 feet until the end of your next turn". PF2e has no rule that gives a character's weapon more reach than a trait's 10 feet: found live, it drops a reach-15 trait from a weapon, and AdjustStrike won't take it. So it's an Effect on its user saying it for its time, and its user measures by it (weapon adjustment property "reach", no rules). Live-checked.
    - Magnetite Scope's Expansion (2026-10-27): "The next Strike you make this round with the gun to which the scope is attached has its scatter radius increased by 5 feet" (10 for the Greater). PF2e has no rule for a scatter radius: its splash on creatures other than the target is its user's to apply. So it's a weapon Effect saying it until the end of its user's turn, and that Strike ends it. `endsOnStrike { trait: "scatter" }` (scripts/ends-on-strike.js) ends it only on a Strike whose options carry a scatter trait, not on one with another weapon.
- **Not a Strike** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/needs.js`, `src/compiler/decided.js`): "Strike" is the action only as PF2e writes it, capitalized. "Strike the chime" and "strike two links together" (Chime of Opening, Ouroboros Flail's Infinite Links) are the verb, and a feather token's "Activate Strike from a ranged weapon" is only how it's fired. Their objects are a line of the card. Where a shot lands, "within your first range increment" (Burrowing Bolt's tunnel, Scouting Arrow's Seek from where it lands), is no change to the Strike either, and "silently" isn't a sound. A check's own d20 and modifier ("a Thievery bonus of 1d20+13") isn't a result the reader misses. An activation with no Effect heading loses its Activate line on its card ("Activate (concentrate) Fate shot is …").
- **A hit's note Effect, all of it** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/hit-note-held.js`, last in an activation's chain): the sentences a hit's note holds aren't the Strike card's notes too (Magekiller Bullet's week of flat checks to cast). Its user's later use of what stays there goes on the note too (Wormhole Shot's "you can use it as the point of origin for a spell or ranged attack", and its end).
- **Hardness a Strike ignores** (2026-10-27, `strike.ignoresHardness`, `src/compiler/strike-damage.js`): "the damage the ammunition deals ignores the target's Hardness if it's 4 or lower" (Shatterpoint Ammunition). PF2e subtracts no creature's Hardness itself, only a shield's, so the Strike card says it.
- **A Strike card's lines** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/strike-lines.js`): notes that are only its user's to do or PF2e's own rule said again become `strike.lines`. Fairy Bullet's "You choose which corner of the target's space you want the burst to extend out from" is its user's pick on the map. Serpent's Lash's "Reposition the creature 5 feet (10 feet on a critical hit)" is PF2e's own Reposition's distances, the follow card's rows.
- **A hit that compels the creature's next action** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/compelled-hit.js`): Mindlock Shot's "you pick a Stride or Strike … it falls under a mental effect that makes it use its first action on its next turn to take the action you picked" is the ways card's pick of the action. Each way is the ammunition's next ranged Strike, and its hit puts a note Effect on the creature until the end of its next turn (two actions on a critical hit).
- **A critical hit's hold while the persistent damage lasts** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/persistent-hold.js`): Garrote Shot's "On a critical hit, it wraps around the target's throat if it has one, and the target can't breathe until the persistent damage ends". Its 2d12 persistent slashing is PF2e's own rule on the ammunition. The activation is now the Strike card, so its critical hit can put a note Effect on the creature that lasts while it takes persistent slashing damage (the ability module's `whilePersistent`).
- **What a Strike gains against a foe in a condition** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/strike-if.js`): Minotaur Chair's "If the foe is Prone, … +1 circumstance bonus to damage per weapon damage die, and the wounding rune deals 1d12 bleed on a hit (or 3d6 bleed on a critical hit)". These are PF2e's rules on the readied Strike's Effect, each predicated on `target:condition:prone`: a FlatModifier of `@weapon.system.damage.dice` (`strike.flat`), the wounding rune's own dice taken off (`strike.runeDice`), and its own dice on a hit and on a critical hit (`critical` false and true, which PF2e doesn't double).
- **A PF2e action while the effect lasts** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/granted-action.js`): Potion of Grounding's "You gain the Divert Lightning reaction while the effect lasts" is PF2e's own action, granted (GrantItem) by the potion's note Effect for its minute.
- **No range penalty out to an increment for a while** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/range-free.js`): Falconsight Eye's "reduces the penalty for firing into your weapon's second range increment from –2 to 0" for 1 minute is PF2e's `ignore-range-penalty:2` roll option on ranged attack rolls, on its note Effect.
- **Other creatures' own reactions** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/others-actions.js`): Singing Bowl of the Versatile Stance's "Anyone within 60 feet currently in a stance can spend a reaction to … change to a different stance" is the card's lines. Chainbreaker's Liberating Strike is the Strike card with its own weapon, and the grabbed or restrained ally's Escape after the hit is its line.
- **One of two effects by the actions spent, the 1-action one a Strike's** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/momentum-ways.js`): Horned Hand Rests' "Activate 1 or 2 … 1 … if your legchair Strides, it deals 2d6 additional force damage on its next Strike and, on a critical success, pushes the target away by 5 feet. 2 … a beam of force" is the ways card. The 1 action is the legchair's next Strike this turn, with those dice and the critical hit's push, and the 2 actions are the beam's save.
- **A Strike turned on another creature** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/redirected-strike.js`): Cauthooj Bagpipes' Throw Song, "the creature redirects the Strike to another creature of your choice within reach … If no other creatures are within range, … a –2 penalty to the Strike", is a note Effect on the failing creature for its turn. PF2e can't move a Strike to another target.
- **A bonus against the kind of creature its user selects** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/kind-ways.js`): Undead Compendium's "Select ghost, ghoul, graveknight, lich, … +2 item bonus on attack rolls and saving throws against that type of undead" is the ways card's pick. Each kind's bonus is PF2e's `target:trait:<kind>` on attack rolls and `origin:trait:<kind>` on saves. A kind with no trait of its own (a lich) gets PF2e's toggle, "Against a lich", on its note Effect, which the bonus needs.
- **A flat shadow's form** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/silhouette.js`): Silhouette Cloak's resistance is the card's own Effect (`plan.item.resist`), with "damage from effects with the light trait" among its exceptions (PF2e's `light`). The rest of the form is a note Effect on its user for the same time, with PF2e's BaseSpeed for "Your land and climb Speeds both become 40 feet". Its reaction gets the first activation's form until the end of the turn.
- **A Strike beyond its weapon's reach or range, free of penalties** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/anywhere-strike.js`): the Impossible rune's "Make a Strike with the etched weapon against one creature you can see, even if the target is beyond the weapon's reach or range. On this Strike, ignore any circumstance penalty, status penalty, and range increment penalty." is the Strike card with PF2e's `ignore-range-penalty` roll option. Its target and the penalties its user unticks in PF2e's roll dialog are the card's lines (PF2e has no rule that drops a kind of penalty).
- **An activation named for its item's PF2e toggle, flipped by each use** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/option-on.js`): Chaplain's Cudgel's Bow to a Higher Power, "This lasts until this activation is used again", flips PF2e's own `chaplains-cudgel:bow-to-a-higher-power` toggle, whose rules give its Strikes the holy or unholy trait. Its deity's weapon's shape and the toggle PF2e gives it after a divine spell are the card's lines.
- **A Strike with one of several benefits its user picks** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/benefit-ways.js`): Morphing Weapon's Reshape is the ways card. Reach is `strike.reach`, Shift is one way per damage type (DamageAlteration), Take is PF2e's Disarm card after the hit (Steal its line), and Weigh is a status bonus of the weapon's damage dice (`strike.flat`).
- **A creature impaled on a shield's blades** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/impaled.js`): Highhelm War Shield's critical hit grabs the creature until it Escapes, against its wielder's Athletics DC (`escape { ownerDC }`). It also gets PF2e's persistent bleed of the blades' damage dice (1 + their striking rune's) and a note Effect while it bleeds, saying the bleed can't end while it's impaled and giving the Escape DC once the shield is let go. The wielder's own part is the card's lines.
- **A spell cast "subjecting it to the effects of"** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/cast-words.js`): Decanter of Endless Water's geyser, "You can direct the stream at a creature, subjecting it to the effects of Hydraulic Push (spell attack roll +15)", is a cast of that spell at its own spell attack.
- **Ammunition that douses persistent damage** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/extinguish.js`): Extinguishing Ball does no damage (`strike.noDamage`). Its critical hit ends every persistent acid or fire damage, and its hit ends one (`strike.recover`, the live `recover`). Every degree but a critical failure puts a note Effect on the creature with PF2e's own rule for the lower recovery DC: ItemAlteration `pd-recovery-dc` on its persistent damage of those types, as PF2e's Toothy Knife raises it. That lasts 1 minute, or until the end of its next turn. The failure's row is played too (`strike.failRow`).
- **A maneuver's damage at the weapon's die with a feat** (2026-10-27, `follow.featDie`, `withFeatDie` in `src/live/strike-card.js`): Iron Cube's "If you have the Slam Down feat, … increase the damage dice if you knock the target prone to the damage die of your weapon if it's higher than d6". The Trip card's prone damage uses the Strike's weapon's die when its user has the feat. Its two-handed weapon's free hand is a line.
- **A light that lures the creatures beginning their turns in it** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/lure-light.js`): Mamlambo Scale's Luminous Lure is the ability module's zone on its wearer for the light's minute, with PF2e's Effect: Luminous Lure as its own. Each creature makes the Will save at the start of its turn in it, with the hour's temporary immunity. The failures' compelled walk and what follows it are a note Effect on the creature for that minute. The critical failure's Off-Guard is a note too, since it's only against that first Strike.
- **A ward against summoned creatures** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/warding-seal.js`): Portable Seal is a placed zone whose save is only for summoned creatures entering it (`only.traits`). A disrupted move is a note Effect, and the round's immunity is tracked. A summoned creature's melee Strike at a creature in it is no zone event, so that save stays the GM's and the plan is partial.
- **One attack roll at several creatures** (2026-10-27, `strike.volley.oneRoll`, `src/compiler/strike-lines.js`, `degreeAgainst` in `src/live/strike-card.js`): Hippopotamus Klar's Engulfing Jaws, "Make a melee Strike … targeting two creatures adjacent to each other. Roll the attack and damage once and apply it to each creature separately", readies one Strike at the two creatures targeted at Apply. Its attack roll is compared to the other creature's AC (PF2e's degrees, ±10 a step, natural 20 and 1), and the card says which it hits. Its one damage roll is applied to each creature hit.
- **A Strike whose hit splits into one more roll at several others** (2026-10-27, `src/compiler/split-arrow.js`, `strike.thenStrike`): Splithead Bow's Five-Headed Arrow is the bow's Strike card. Its hit posts the pieces' Strike card, one ranged Strike at up to four creatures within 30 feet of the first (`oneRoll` with `upTo`), its one roll compared to each one's AC. The bow's healing is off until its user's next daily preparations (`toggleOff.untilRest`: its Effect deleted at PF2e's Rest for the Night, the toggle back on).
