    - Its user's own moves as lines of the card (2026-10-27, own-moves.js, after the falls in both chains): Eagle Wing's and the Gliding rune's glide down ("5 feet down and 30 feet forward through the air", staying up while it glides each turn), Pocket Watch of Stethelos' three moves while time pauses, Gadget Skates' Sustained skating at twice its Speed with its DC 20 Acrobatics turn, and Emerald Grasshopper's long Leap beside its fall. What its user does on the map is the card's line (the user's ruling 2), one line ending "(its own move on the map)"; the whole activation those sentences and its flavor makes its own card, and beside another reading its move sentences become that line, partial no more when nothing else is left.
    - A harmless fall at a rate (2026-10-27, falls.js): Emerald Grasshopper's "you flutter in the air until the end of your turn, then fall harmlessly at a rate of 60 feet per round" is `fall { noDamage, rate: 60, flutter }` until the end of its user's next turn: the environment module's no-damage fall Effect, the flutter and rate its words.
    - A fall eased by the height it's deployed at (2026-10-27, falls.js heightWaysPlan; scripts/falls.js offers): Parachute Mail's Deploy Parachute is the ways card's two buttons, "Deployed higher than 100 feet: no falling damage" (`fall.noDamage`) and "Deployed lower than 100 feet: half the falling damage" (`fall.halved`, flagged for the environment module, which doesn't halve a fall yet — its words on the Effect). Its deploying within a round and its repacking (10 minutes, DC 15 Crafting) are each way's lines; the fall offer lists both ways.
    - A teleport beside the other wearer, and out of a fire (2026-10-27, teleport.js; src/live/teleport-card.js teleport.lines): Headbands of Translocation teleports its user adjacent to the creature targeted (the other invested wearer, within 1 mile — a line); Fire-Jump Ring's teleport is within 100 feet after its user's own Stride into a fire (lines: the Stride first, no damage from that fire, a space in or beside another fire, a fire it ends in affects it).
    - Difficult terrain ignored till its turn ends (2026-10-27, rows.js ruleEffectsOf): Feather Step Stone's "When you activate the stone, you ignore the effects of any difficult terrain and greater difficult terrain you move through until the end of your turn" — the "When you activate …" opening and the time at the end now read too; PF2e's terrain rules on its user's Effect.
    - Others along on a teleport, a creature brought, smoke (2026-10-27, teleport.js teleport.with / summon / smoke; src/live/teleport-card.js freeSpacesBeside; scripts/teleport.js bringAlong, smokeAt): Bring Me Near's "You and up to 4 willing creatures adjacent to you are instantly teleported to that spot" (within 5 miles), Blink Blade's critical hit "You and your target teleport to a space within 10 feet", Transposition Ammunition (Greater)'s "take along up to two willing adjacent creatures" are `teleport.with { count, target? }`: after its user's token lands, the creatures its presser targeted (those that stood beside it, or Blink Blade's target wherever it is), up to the count, each teleport into the free space beside it nearest to where they stood from it; with no room one stays. Blink Blade's "puffs of smoke, as noted with the activation above" are `teleport.smoke`: the ability module's Concealed zone at the space it left and the one it landed in, till the end of its user's next turn. Whistle of Calling's attuned creature is `teleport.summon`: the creature targeted is the token moved, to a space within 30 feet of its user (picked with its size).
    - What moves off the map, or moves only objects (2026-10-27, far-moves.js farMovesPlan; withTravelCast after the cast notes are judged): Homeward Wayfinder's lodge and Cube of Recall's attuned side (its user's token to that scene), the Paired runes' pockets trading places, Magnet Coin's coin and Predictable Silver Piece's toss are lines of the card; Cipher of Elemental Planes' keyhole view and the Compass's Track Teleportation are whispered to the GM and its user (the information ruling). Voyager's Pack's Group Voyage keeps the spell module's Interplanar Teleport or Teleport, its harnessed creatures, its DC 45 Survival check and where it arrives off target its cast card's lines.
    - A weapon that reshapes into one of a list (2026-10-27, objects.js RESHAPE_LIST, FORM_LINES): Twining Staff's "+1 striking staff, a +1 striking bo staff, or its disk form" — the objects card's reshape between its two weapon forms; its disk form, its grip and its expanding jump or Force Open with the staff's own +15 lines of the card.
    - A one-off beside a partial builtin (2026-10-27, decided.js): a plan whose builtin is partial for a one-off rule (Crown of the Companion's Share Life with its healing when the spell ends) gets its notes as the card's lines too, decided "one-off". The moving group's one-offs: Firefoot Popcorn's hot squares, Bola Shot's attack roll against Reflex DC, Dimensional Cleavestone's rifts, Tidal Fishhook's water-to-water Strike, Planar Tunnel, Crown of the Companion, Sargassum Phial's per-creature hallucinations, Colorful Coating (Red)'s trampoline.
    - A trail a Stride leaves (2026-10-27, move.js move.trail; src/live/teleport-card.js squaresAlong; scripts/teleport.js trailZone): Hellfire Boots' "Each square you move through during your Stride is scorched with hellish flames, becoming hazardous terrain for 1 minute. A creature that moves through one of these spaces takes 3d6 fire damage" — once the teleport card has moved the token, one ability module zone over the squares it crossed (made at the first, its shapes then all of them), hazardous terrain with that damage on entering, for the minute. Its maker isn't harmed by its own zone.
    - A ride (2026-10-27, teleport.js RIDE): Hippogriff in a Jar's effigy is its user's Fly up to 65 feet on the teleport card, the other rider along (`teleport.with`); those still on it when it dissolves falling Prone a line.
    - What holds a creature or a way in place (2026-10-27, moving-notes.js): Magnetizing's difficult terrain for moving apart, Immovable Arm's anchored arm (8,000 pounds or DC 40 Athletics to Force Open, DC 25 Seek to notice) and Warding Escutcheon's locked door (the shield's own Hardness, HP and BT from its text, DC 25 or 43 to open, an hour) are words on its user's note Effect, the GM's call and the door on the map lines; Stepping Stone Shot's line of stone discs is the card's line. Oil of Skating's Balance is PF2e's AdjustDegreeOfSuccess on its user's Effect for the hour (`action:balance`), its downhill, rough-ground and uphill words with it. Guangu of the Steppe's Hammer of Hooves is the ways card: its riders' PF2e Effect (10 minutes, not PF2e's unlimited) and their mounts' (Mount Speed), each at the creatures its presser targets.
    - A creature sent to another plane on a row (2026-10-27, far-moves.js withPlaneSent): Maw of Hungry Shadows' critical failure, beside its Blinded and Drained 2, "teleporting it to the Netherworld" — words on its note Effect (off this plane, its token leaves the scene), the GM's line gone.
    - A counteract near the square it's scattered in (2026-10-27, counteract.js SQUARE_NEAR, AFTER_FREE; scripts/counteract.js): Devil's Trap's powder counteracts a devil's translocation to within 30 feet of its square — the counteract card's creatures within 35 feet to pick the devil from, its line; after a success, the devil's note Effect "It can't use actions with the teleportation trait" for 1 minute (`counteract.afterFree`).
    - Drenched (2026-10-27, snares.js drenchedRows): Puddle Shove Snare's critical failure –1 circumstance penalty to saving throws against electricity effects for 1 minute is the row's modifier with PF2e's `item:trait:electricity` predicate; its crafter's direction for the push a line, as the rocket snare's.
    - Cast lines (2026-10-27, far-moves.js withTravelCast, after the cast notes): Bewitching Bloom (Purple Iris)'s Command picks its targets within the spell's range of the ally and commands Drop Prone — lines of the cast card. Starshot Arrow (Greater)'s "You can fire at any creature you can detect, regardless of range" and its instant, teleporting arrow are its Strike's lines (strike-damage.js requires).

## Triggered activations (2026-10-27)

- **Temporary Hit Points from a Strike's damage** (`DRAIN_HIT` in `src/compiler/strike-damage.js`, `strike.drain`; `scripts/strikes.js` `drainOnHit`, `scripts/drain.js` `markDrained`): Reaper's Toll's Reaper's Claim, "If your Strike hits, you gain temporary Hit Points equal to half the damage dealt, which last for 1 minute". On the hit, the creature struck gets the drain mark Exsanguinate uses; the Strike's damage roll as PF2e applies it then gives its user half of what the creature lost, after resistances, for the minute. "As though you were in the space of the triggering creature" is a line of the card: the reach is measured from there on the map.
- **A condition suppressed for a while** (`withSuppressed` in `src/compiler/triggered.js`): Lost Ember's Fix Focus, "You suppress the Off-Guard or Confused condition until the start of your next turn"; Aeon Stone (Olivine Pendeloque)'s Still Earth, "The stone suppresses the triggering effect for 1 minute" (its trigger "You become confused, frightened, or stupefied"); Moonlit Chain's Dim Sight, "suppress the Dazzled condition for 1 minute". Each condition is a note Effect on its user for that time, saying the condition has no effect, with PF2e's AdjustModifier suppressing the condition's own modifiers (Off-Guard's −2 to AC, which Confused grants too; Frightened's and Stupefied's status penalties; Dazzled has none). When the words name several conditions, the ways card has one way for each. Add a condition in `SUPPRESSES`: its condition item's FlatModifier selectors and slug, and what else suppressing it means in words.
- **An item's Hit Points: another creature's damaged, its user's mended** (`src/compiler/objects.js`; the objects card, `scripts/objects.js`): Rustbringer's Rusting Disarm, "The weapon takes 2d6 damage, ignoring the weapon's Hardness" (4d6 acid for the Greater), is op `damage`: Apply with the disarmed creature targeted lowers its weapon's Hit Points by the roll (its dropped one, or one picked when it has several), broken at its Broken Threshold and destroyed at 0; a weapon PF2e keeps no Hit Points for gets the damage said. Mending Lattice's "negates the damage and instantly and completely repairs the affixed item" and Fortifying Pebble's "the affixed object takes 10 less damage" are op `repair` of the item it's affixed to, picked once the damage lands (full Hit Points; 10 back). Sanguine Klar's "regains a number of Hit Points equal to the bleed damage, up to an amount equal to its Hardness" mends itself by the amount typed on the card, at most its Hardness.
- **Belt of Giant Strength's rock catch** is a one-off (`src/compiler/one-offs.js`): an Athletics check whose degree decides the triggering rock's damage. Its words are the card's lines.
- **A roll PF2e's own Effect replaces** (`withSubstituteRoll` in `src/compiler/triggered.js`): Ancestral Geometry's "Replace the roll with the d20 roll from your ancestor's visitation" is builtin `pf2e-rules`. Its linked Effect: Ancestral Geometry rolls the visitation's d20 as its badge and notes the save's type, and its SubstituteRoll offers that d20 in that save's roll dialog. There's no card; what its user picks there is a line.
- **A missed shot sent back** (`withRedirectedShot` in `src/compiler/triggered.js`; `src/live/strike-as.js` `triggeringMiss`, `formulaInstances`; `scripts/live.js` `triggeringMissFormula`): Turnabout Shield's "Make a ranged Strike using the ammunition with an attack modifier of +19, targeting a creature within 60 feet" is the item's own attack at +19 against the targeted creature's AC. On a hit, its damage is the triggering Strike's: as the card is made, the latest ranged Strike in the chat that missed its user gives its attacker's PF2e damage formula, each instance its own type, doubled on a critical hit (`plan.strikeDamage { from: "triggering-miss" }`).
- **A bleed made worse, and penalties after it** (`withBleedWorse` in `src/compiler/triggered.js`; `scripts/then-for.js`): Secret-Keeper's Mask (Father Skinsaw)'s mutilating wound is a note Effect on the targeted creature lasting while it takes persistent bleed damage, with PF2e's ItemAlteration `pd-recovery-dc` (+2, as PF2e's Toothy Knife raises it) and the −2 status FlatModifiers. Its RollOption `air-then-for:1:minutes:bleed` makes the active GM's client give the same penalties for 1 minute once the Effect goes and the creature no longer bleeds.
- **An attacker beguiled** (`withBeguiledAttacker` in `src/compiler/triggered.js`): Beguiling Crown's reaction is a DC 41 Will save card at the attacker. On a failure, a note Effect until the end of its turn says its attack is a failure (no damage) and it's friendly. On a critical failure it begs forgiveness for 1 minute.
- **A spell at its user counteracted** (`src/compiler/triggered.js`: `reflectedSpell`, `slashedSpell`, `withAbsorbedSpell`; `scripts/counteract.js`): the counteract card's one row for the triggering spell, its DC and rank the GM's. Reflecting Shard's Spell Riposte uses its user's Athletics modifier (`fromUser.modifier: "athletics"`, any skill's slug) at rank 7 (9 for the Greater); counteracted, the card says the spell goes on at its caster (`free.counteredWords`). Spellslasher's Spellslash uses half its user's level and its best Strike's attack modifier (`"weapon-attack"`, a line saying the GM may change it to the weapon's). Spell Duelist's Siphon gives no modifier, so its level's DC less 10, rank half its level; counteracted, a note Effect on its user says the grimoire holds the spell to cast from its own slot (`onCounter`).
- **Uzunjati Storytelling Amulet's Enamoring Story** (`EXTRA` in `src/compiler/item-actions.js`): Make an Impression or Request, picked on the card, rolled with Performance in place of Diplomacy (PF2e's own action).
- **An alert whispered to the wearers near** (`withAlertWearers` in `src/compiler/triggered.js`; `scripts/live.js` `postWhisper`): Name Pendant's Alert Superior Officer is the information ruling's whisper, to the GM, its user's player and the players of the creatures within 500 feet wearing a Name Pendant (`whisper.alsoTo`).
- **A Strike drawn to its user** (`withDrawnStrike` in `src/compiler/triggered.js`; `scripts/half-damage.js` `beforeShieldBlock`): "The triggering Strike targets you instead of its normal target" is a line of the card: the GM aims the attacker's Strike at its user before rolling it (Black Hole Armor). Arrow-Catching Shield's and Lodestone Shield's "If it hits, you gain the effects of the Shield Block reaction" is a readied Effect (`bonus.shieldBlock`, against what triggered it): the damage of the Strike it takes is applied as PF2e's Shield Block (applyDamage's `shieldBlockRequest`), and that damage spends the Effect.
- **Collar of the Shifting Spider** is a one-off: the mutagen loaded in it taking effect at half its time.
- **Rules PF2e can't play, on note Effects for their time** (`NOTE_RULES` in `src/compiler/triggered.js`): Helm of Zeal's Divine Fervor (an extra reaction only for its champion's reaction, until the start of its next turn), Whispering Veil's Unforeseen Alert (a reaction only to answer what it was warned of, this turn), Undead Scourge's Sever from the Void (on the undead it hit: no void healing for 1 minute unless that healing counteracts it, rank 4, DC 25), Victory Plate's recorded victory (until a Recall uses it). Add one with its sentences in order, whom it's for, its words and its time.
- **Death avoided at 1 Hit Point** (`withAvoidedDeath`): Locket of Love Left Behind's True Love's Power is `item.dropToOne` (its user's Hit Points to 1, its Dying ended). Its casing to replace before another use is a line of the card.
- **What it learns, whispered** (`withEchoedCall`): Lion's Call's Echo the Call is the information ruling's whisper (the GM tells where the creature is and what it looks like; an hour of telepathy within 1 mile).
- **Consumables pressed when their trigger comes** (`withTriggeredUse`): Fulu of the Drunken Monkey's two ways (its +2 against a poison; against alcohol, a note Effect with PF2e's AdjustDegreeOfSuccess for the triggering save and +4 to saves against PF2e's Alcohol for the minute). Emergency Eye's sight through the eye (Blinded suppressed until the end of its next turn). Inventor's Fulu's lines (the critical failure a failure, the innovation back in a minute). Murderer's Knot's Off-Guard is a note Effect lasting while the bleed does (`whilePersistent` and PF2e's GrantItem of Off-Guard); with its owner's Twist the Knife feat, the bleed is a rogue's sneak attack dice at its level. Exuviae Powder's two ways (8 hours of a chitin layer; Shed Chitin's Quickened, Doomed 1 and Restrained until it Escapes at DC 25). Persistent Lodestone's recalled round is the objects card's `reload`, `recalled`: the round it missed with given back and loaded, none of its user's spent.
- **A Strike of its own after the trigger** (`strikeCard`, `withTranslocatedStrike`, `withWeaponCast` in `src/compiler/triggered.js`): Bloodstride Blade's Call of the Bloodthirst is the teleport card beside it (`item.move`, a space within 5 feet of the creature targeted), then the strike card, its target Off-Guard to it (PF2e's EphemeralEffect). Vine of Roses' Holy Light is the strike card whose attack roll is the spell's: on a hit the spell module casts it at rank 3 at that degree (`strike.cast.attackFromHit`), with no weapon damage of its own (`strike.noDamage`).
- **Electricity drawn off a creature** (`withDivertedEnergy`): Storm Flash's Divert Lightning is a readied Effect on the creature targeted within 10 feet, immune to the triggering effect's electricity damage (spent by it). Its user's melee attack roll against the effect's DC comes first and is its own; the card says to Apply only after a success.
- **An armor that falls apart to shield its wearer** (`withCollapsedArmor`): Suit of Armoire's reaction is the ways card by what triggered it, the hit a failure or the Reflex save a success, and the suit off its wearer, as lines of the card.
- **Spellbook of Redundant Enchantment** is a one-off: a prepared spell kept by expending another.
- **Charges stored from a spell** (`withStoredCharges` in `src/compiler/triggered.js`; `src/live/charges.js`; the objects card's op `charge`): Conflagration Club's Absorb Arcana is the ways card, a way for each damage type its trigger names. Each stores the slot's rank, typed on the card, as charges on the club (its flag `charges`), refused while it still has some. Its Explosive Cone's die per charge, typed on its card and its type picked (`src/compiler/scaled.js`), is filled in from those charges as its card is made, and the charges are then spent. With none stored through a card, it's typed as before.
- **More note Effects for their time**: Auspicious Scepter's Fortunate Strike on the ally targeted (its next Strike bypasses resistance equal to the spell's rank, one type, the GM's call as the damage is applied; PF2e's resistance bypass belongs to runes, not to any rule an Effect can carry), and Key of Unwinding's Rewrite Time for its minute.
- **Weapons made for Strikes from where its hit landed** (`MATERIALIZE` in `src/compiler/objects.js`; `scripts/objects.js` `spendMadeAfterAttack`): Chain of Stars' three +1 striking shuriken of force are the objects card's `create` with `quantity` and `damageType`. They're PF2e's shuriken in its user's hand, its own Strikes with them. Each one goes after its attack (a miss's attack roll, a hit's damage roll). Whom they Strike (each a different creature, not the one hit), from that creature's space, and the multiple attack penalty held until the last are lines of the card. A talisman used up as they're made needs no holder.
- **Beguiling Crown's command** (`withBeguilingCommand`): the DC 41 Will save card at a living creature within 30 feet: helpful for 24 hours, friendly for 1 hour, or on a critical success immune for a year, each a note Effect (the user's ruling for attitudes).
- **Sustained, repeating and later effects** (2026-10-27, docs/needs.md "lasting", `test/lasting-2026-10-27.test.js`):
    - Read sentence by sentence (`src/compiler/lasting.js`): an activation or consumable whose words the readers before left whole is read by its own reading, every sentence of its effect one of the reading's, none dropped. It becomes a note Effect for the time its words give, the creatures' conditions, a cast or a whisper, and the card's lines for its user's own actions, the map, objects and what's out of combat. A Dismiss is its user removing the Effect. Hat of Many Minds' Patchwork Double (its 30 minutes), Twilight Tattoo's Inscribe, Charm of the Ordinary (One with Stone's merge for its 10 minutes, its 10d6 said), Clockwork Recorder, Pathfinder's Coin, Aeon Stone (Eastern Star)'s language and Spectacles of Understanding (Greater)'s Translate for every language seen are note Effects; Everlasting Adhesive, Librarian's Baton's attunement, Bivouac Targe's tent and Pathfinder's Pouch are card lines.
    - Lily Pad Platform's Plunge: engulfed is PF2e's Engulf, Grabbed and Slowed 1, for the creatures on the platform for the hour, each ended by an Escape (DC 19, the ability module's `duration.escape`). Faydhaan's Dallah's Brew is an 8-hour "water breathing" Effect on each drinker targeted (`item.recipient` "targets"; its name is the environment module's water breathing), its underwater temperatures in its words.
    - Casts: Horrific Effigy's Whispers of Leng is Nightmare (DC 30) at the named creature, cast again at each sleep (the GM's); Primeval Mistletoe (Greater)'s Cultivate is Field of Life, Sustained as the spell is. Silver Snake Cane's True Silver Snake is the conjured Giant Viper, rolling initiative, its Strikes silver (PF2e's AdjustStrike `materials`). Librarian's Baton's pendulum is whispered.
    - Tornado Trompo's tornado: a save card down its 120-foot line (DC 38 Reflex) in place of Whirlwind's cast, whose cylinder isn't the item's. It's Whirlwind's 8th-rank 5d10 bludgeoning, half on a success. On a failure the creature rises 10 feet and falls as the tornado ends, so PF2e's falling damage (half the distance, 5 more) and Prone; a critical failure doubles both (20 feet, 10). A creature that can fly doesn't fall, a line of the card.
    - A Sustain that gives again (`plan.again` with `repeat`, `time` and `when`): Blakenshipper's Be the Band keeps its 15 temporary Hit Points card, and after it a card for the Sustain's 5, kept open for the minute and pressed at each Sustain. The exposure card's follow-up now carries `repeat`, `until` and `when` (scripts/poisons.js), as the item card's does.
    - Healing a damage type pauses (`installed.deactivatedBy`, scripts/deactivated.js): Troll Hide's "If you take electricity or fire damage, the armor deactivates until the end of your next turn". The installed Effect's FastHealing is predicated off `self:effect:air-deactivated-<item>`. The core's damage listener gives its wearer that Effect to the end of its next turn when electricity or fire damage is applied, so the next turn's start heals none. Its organs, reattached parts, other trolls and the armor healed first are lines of its card.
    - Classified, not built: Atlas Arcane's "does not update afterward" is nothing later, so it's whispered (needs.js). "To sustain the horses" (Campaign Stable) is food, and "until you make your next daily preparations" (Mender's Soup) is out of combat, so both are card lines. Note-only and checked (`NOTES_CHECKED`): Bracelet of Buoyancy's Float, War Mage's Buckler's Spell Amp, Trackless (Greater), Kaiju Fulu, Instant Spy, Colorful Coating (Indigo), Banquet and Feast of Hungry Ghosts. One-offs (`ONE_OFFS`): Sea Retrieval Line, Kizidhar's Shield's Repair, Duchy Defender's Declare Judgment, Breech Ejectors, Shifter Prosthesis, Called (Lastwall).
- **Speeds, senses, forms and sizes** (2026-10-27, `test/senses-2026-10-27.test.js`):
    - Light spells a rank higher against darkness, and their flash (dark-light.js; src/live/cast-card.js darkLightCounteract; scripts/casts.js, scripts/counteract.js): Dawnlight's "any spell with the light trait is treated as though its counteract rank were 1 higher for counteracting darkness". Once the cast card has cast one of its light spells (Light; the Major's Holy Light too), it posts the counteract card against the magical darkness: the item's counteract modifier, the spell's rank and 1 more, the darkness's DC and rank the GM's. The Greater's and Major's flash, "Whenever you successfully counteract magical darkness while holding this symbol …", is the save card that card posts once it counteracts the darkness: Fortitude DC 23 (28), Dazzled for 1 round on a failure, at the creatures its presser targets. Only creatures with darkvision attempt it (`ifTarget` `lacks`, the rest immune); light blindness is a line of the card, the creature's own ability.
    - A growth its item's own Effect already is, and a Trample while it lasts (trample.js; src/live/strike-as.js formulaParts; scripts/poisons.js): Dinosaur Boots used to cast Enlarge, which left out the foot and its Trample. PF2e's Effect: Dinosaur Boots is Enlarge's size, reach, damage bonus and clumsy with the foot's Strike (d6, the Greater's d8 and Huge), so the item card gives that Effect. Its Trample is the save card after it (`plan.again`), kept for the minute: used once ("Once during the duration"), or each time for the Greater. It's a basic Reflex save (DC 27) at the creatures whose spaces its user entered, and its damage is the foot Strike's as PF2e rolls it when it's used (`strikeDamage.slug`, each damage instance its own type). The Greater's Huge is PF2e's; a user who wants to stay Large changes it by hand (a line of the card).
    - A watch whose set-off is the GM's note (snares.js alarmZone, sentry.js): Clockwork Chirper's square is set off once by a Small or larger creature. The note says its chirps are heard 500 feet away and that it follows that creature for up to an hour, with the bird's own numbers (an object). Sentry Fulu's guard watches a 20-foot burst for 8 hours. Each creature that enters sends the note of its alarm: audible, waking sleepers with a DC 15 Perception check, or mental, within 60 feet. The password, a slip past its Perception DC 20, the patrol and linked fulus are the user's and the GM's, as lines of the card. A note-only set-off is no longer given an untraced-sentence plan (honest.js withZoneUntraced), which would have made it a save card.
    - An object's own pace or a smell isn't its user's Speed or sense (needs.js): "Flies at a Speed of 45 feet" (the missives), "moves forward at a speed of 30 feet per round" (Wind-Up Cart), "a faintly fruity scent" (Ration Tonic) and "the scent of brimstone" (Fiendish Teleportation) no longer count as a Speed or a sense to build. What's left of Ration Tonic, the Messenger and Heartening Missives and Tracking Fulu is out of the fight or information, so the user's rulings make them the card's lines or a whispered card. Shortbread Spy (scout.js) is the whispered card too: the cookie's hour away, and the sights it records for whoever eats it, which the GM tells.
    - A failure made a success once (first-degree.js withOnceBetter): Heartening Missive (Butterfly)'s recipient, the creature its presser targets when the missive arrives, gets its user's Effect for 24 hours. It has PF2e's AdjustDegreeOfSuccess on Diplomacy and on Performance, a failure or critical failure one degree better, and the first roll it changes removes it (scripts/first-degree.js, now for several checks). Its 24 hours' immunity to heartening missives is the immunity tracker's, as the other missives'.
    - Floating off the ground (aloft.js): Hovering Potion gives a climb Speed equal to its drinker's Speed for 5 minutes (PF2e's BaseSpeed), and its floating, Strides up, down and along ceilings, and Arrest a Fall are words on that Effect. Feather Token (Balloon) is a note Effect for its 9 rounds on up to two creatures holding its handles, with the rise and fall round by round in its words; each token's elevation is its user's to set. Balloon Padding's Inflate is a note Effect with no end of its own (its user removes it on deflating): its height changes, the balloon's AC 10 and Hardness 0, and the fall when it's damaged. Boots of Free Running's wall-running during Strides has no PF2e rule (a climb Speed would let its user Climb, not Stride up walls), so its minute's note Effect is checked as all there is (notes-checked.js).
    - Unseen by undead (undead-ward.js; scripts/ends-on-hostile.js "air-ends-on:attack"): Corpseward Pendant's 10 minutes are words on its user's Effect, which PF2e can't play per observer. Its wearer's attack or damage roll ends it, but not a spell that harms no one; touching an undead is ended by hand. The undead's DC 18 Will save to ignore it is a card kept for the 10 minutes (`plan.again`), only for undead (`ifTarget` `lacks`). A success gives that undead its 24 hours' immunity to the pendant.
    - A rage whose creatures change while it lasts (cannibal-call.js): Horn of the Aoyin's Cannibal Call was a note. Now it's a DC 30 Will card for the creatures other than its user in its 60-foot emanation. A failure (1 round) or critical failure (1 minute) gives a note Effect with PF2e's rules: a jaws Strike (1d8 piercing), +2 damage on unarmed Strikes, +2 status to saves against mental effects, imprecise bloodsense 30 feet, +10 feet status to Speed, and –1 to AC. Whom they attack, no weapons, and no concentrate actions but Seek are its words. A critical success's 24 hours' immunity is the tracker's. Incapacitation (above level 13) and the horn turning on an owner who doesn't use it daily are lines of the card.
    - Words on an Effect for what PF2e can't play (rule-words.js, disguise.js, see-through.js): Infiltrator's Elixir's disguise, whose +4 is only against seeing through it and adds its level even if untrained, beside its +4 to Deception DC. Look-Me-Not's untrained Feint and its Strikes that trigger no reactions, beside its +2 to Feint. Sinister Knight's disguise until dismissed: lifesense precautions, mistaken for undead, and its counteract against alignment-revealing effects at its level's DC less 10 (+14, rank 4, the GM's to change). Scope of Truth's Reveal All: the GM's secret counteract (+20, rank 7) against illusions and polymorphs for 10 minutes. Animate Dreamer's Animate Object note only says what Telekinetic Hand does, so it isn't a rule left (honest.js).
    - Damage beside where its caster was and arrives (cast-arrival.js; scripts/cast-arrival.js; src/live/arrival.js): Fiendish Teleportation's brimstone. After the cast, the next move of its user's token sends the ability module's card of 2d6 spirit damage, with no roll against it. It goes to every other creature adjacent to the space it left or the one it arrived in, each once ("both spaces" read as each of them).
    - A snare's dousing (doused.js): Appetizing Flavor Snare's DC 15 Reflex save, which the ability compiler didn't read, is the snare's zone save. Its failures give a note Effect for the hour: predators smell it from twice as far and come to look (the GM's), and a minute's scrubbing washes it off.
    - A lamp that lights its room (lamp.js): Aquarium Lamp's activation puts 60 feet of bright light (and 60 more dim) at its user's square for 10 minutes (`placedLight`, as Flare Beacon's). Its prey refills and its eels' scuffles are lines of the card.
    - Forms (cryptid-shape.js, reshape.js, disguise.js, animal-brew.js): Cloak of the False Foe's Cryptid Shape is the cast card for Humanoid Form, its picker a Medium cryptid (rank 3, an hour) or a Large one (rank 5, 10 minutes), each choice's own time (`choice.castDuration`, scripts/casts.js). Bestiary of Metamorphosis's Shifting Form finds its user's polymorph Effect that has PF2e's choice of forms (a ChoiceSet beside its BattleForm) and makes it again, so PF2e asks for the new form, for half its time left (`plan.item.reshape`, scripts/reshape.js). The base Fanged rune's animal shape is words on its Effect until dismissed or the weapon is dropped; the Greater's and Major's were PF2e's own Effect already. The Bloodhammer Reserves give PF2e's Spell Effect for an Animal Form battle form: the Reserve's is rolled on the table card (its 1d10 with 10 rerolled, a d9 over the nine), and the Select's and Black Label's are picked on the ways card (rank 2 and rank 4). Their battle form's temporary Hit Points are set to 10 and 20 on each rank's BattleForm bracket (`application.battleTempHP`, src/live/card.js effectSource).
    - Conditions for a night's change (rebirth.js): Rebirth Potion's Clumsy 2, Enfeebled 2 and Stupefied 2 are granted by its 8-hour note Effect (GrantItem, gone with it), used as its drinker starts to rest. The new ancestry is its player's to make on the sheet, a line of the card.
    - A note beside a cast with its user's numbers (cast-note.js; scripts/casts.js): Enigma-Sight Potion's Truesight. The GM's secret counteract checks use its drinker's Perception modifier and half its level as their rank. Those numbers, filled in as it's cast, and a critical failure's Stupefied 1 for 1d4 rounds and Stunned 1, are its note Effect for Truesight's 10 minutes.
    - A shrink whose steps a save at each of its turns moves (implosion.js; scripts/implosion.js): Implosion Dust's failure and critical failure give a note Effect for its 6 rounds with PF2e's rules: one CreatureSize −1 for each step (never below Tiny, its reach PF2e's for the new size), −2 status to melee damage for each step, and a marker (RollOption `air-implosion:<save>:<dc>`). At the end of each of that creature's turns, the active GM's client rolls the save through PF2e and moves the steps: a critical success ends it, a success takes one off (none left ends it), a failure adds one and a critical failure two. The rules are made again for the new count, its damage penalty only for steps it really shrank. That only amorphous creatures are affected is a line of the card (the GM's call).
    - Penetrating Ammunition's 60-foot line Strike, one attack roll against each creature in it, stays partial (2026-10-27, at the merge). PF2e's own rule adds its 1d6 bleed, but PF2e's own rules have no card of their own, so its words can't be one's lines. A one-off on a partial builtin counts as card lines only when that builtin's card shows its notes (`decided.js`). It's work to build, as `volley.oneRoll`'s one attack roll at several creatures.
    - A weapon fighting on its own (self-fighting.js; src/live/self-fighting.js; scripts/poisons.js): Animated's Set Free and Vine Whip's rooted whip. The card keeps the weapon's Strike open while it fights (`plan.again`). Its attack is the text's modifier (+24, +22) plus the weapon's potency, and its damage is the weapon's own dice with its striking rune, of its type, with no Strength, plus 1d6 for each elemental property rune. All of it is filled in from the weapon as the card is pressed. After each use it rolls the DC 6 flat check. A failure closes the card, and the activation's 10 minutes' wait starts then (the activations' recharge flag), not when it was set free. Its flying, its actions and what it can't do (block, flank, react) are lines of the card.

## Rules for some situations, limits and immunities (2026-10-27)

These run last, after the user's rulings (`src/compiler/item-rules.js`, on an activation's plan and a consumable's).

- **Merged with a substance until expelled** (`src/compiler/merged.js`): Tideplate's Submerge, "You merge with the water for 10 minutes. While merged, you can't move …". It's a note Effect on its user for the time with PF2e's GrantItem of Immobilized, gone with it, as the spell module plays One with Stone's merge. What expels it (the water damaged or dried, a Control Water) is no event PF2e has: its words, with the 10d6, are on that Effect.
- **A bonus for a situation PF2e can't tell apart** (`src/compiler/situational.js`): a toggle on the item's note Effect (PF2e's RollOption, domain all, toggleable, as Red Rose's "Beloved within 10 feet") and the FlatModifier predicated on it, or on PF2e's own roll options where it has them. Eidetic Potion's +2 status to Crafting and Society for "representations of that memory" is removed after the first roll it helps (`removeAfterRoll: "if-enabled"`), else in a week. Fake Blood Pack's +2 item to Deception "when faking an injury", for 4 hours once punctured (how it's punctured is a line of its card). Empath's Cordial's +2 item to Perception to discern a creature's emotions (its toggle) and +1 to Sense Motive (`action:sense-motive`). Restful Sleep Fulu's +2 status to saves against mental effects in its dreams (its toggle and `item:trait:mental`); its doubled rest healing is words on that Effect.
- **Bonuses against the creatures its user picks, several** (`withToastedMarks` in `situational.js`, `item.mark.several`; `src/live/card.js` markEffectSource, `scripts/live.js`): Rhyton of the Radiant Ifrit's Toast!, "+2 item bonus to Deception, Diplomacy, or Intimidation checks against those creatures for 1 hour". Apply marks every creature its presser targets (one PF2e TokenMark at each token, the same slug), and the bonuses are predicated on `target:mark:<slug>`, as `marked.js`'s one creature's are.
- **An object the bonus is for, a line** (`withMechanismLine` in `situational.js`): Oozepick's "The bonus is only against the mechanism the ooze was first poured into" is the user's to keep at the map, a line of its card beside its Thievery bonus.
- **A time, then a temporary immunity to the item** (`src/compiler/immune-after.js`, held by the immunity tracker): Spiced Demonade's 24 hours from when its hour ends (25 hours from its use), its alcohol and lack of sleep words on its Effect. Shell of Easy Breathing's Fill the Shell gives each creature its user targets "Can breathe underwater" for the hour (8 for the Greater) and the immunity to its next daily preparations; filling and keeping the shell still are lines. Scholar's Drop's Secondary Effect is the ways card's second way: Fatigued's penalties suppressed for 10 minutes (as Ring of the Weary Traveler's) and the immunity for the 70 minutes from then, its first use's bonus ended by hand and its three uses a day lines. Journeybread (Power)'s week of meals is a line of its card.
- **Checked, nothing to play** (`NOTES_CHECKED`): Cat's Eye Elixir's targeting flat checks (no PF2e rule sets their DC, as PF2e's own Effect: Mummified Bat leaves it to words) and Journeybread's meals.
- **Ammunition that stays in the creature hit** (`src/compiler/intact-shot.js`): Treasure Vault's shots that "remain intact" (Lightning Rod Shot, Conduit Shot in its three grades, Spell Echo Shot). The Strike card's hit already put a note Effect on the creature for the shot's minute, with part of its rule. It now carries the whole rule: the creatures its user names, a spell that includes the shot in its area made larger or duplicated, the electricity riders. Lightning Rod's "–1 circumstance penalty to the save" is PF2e's FlatModifier on that Effect, for saves against electricity effects. Whether the shot stays with the creature (the GM's call) and where one that doesn't stick falls are lines of the card.
- **The sworn creature's critical hits** (`src/compiler/oath.js`): Oathbow's critical hits against the creature it's sworn against gain the bow's critical specialization. Beside the mark, an Effect for the oath's 7 days has PF2e's CriticalSpecialization for the bow's Strikes against the marked creature (`item:slug:oathbow`, `target:mark:oathbow-mark`), and a Note on those critical hits for the DC 20 when its user already has the bow's critical specialization.
- **Nothing read, the table's** (`src/compiler/table-lines.js`): Ghost Courier Fulu's message and its 2d10 hours on the way, and Obfuscation Oil's item warded against divination, are lines of their cards (a message, a journey, an object). Spiritsight Crossbow's Ethereal Vision is words on its user's Effect until the end of the turn, since no PF2e sense sees the Ethereal Plane. Holy Steam Ball's first "Activate 1 (manipulate)" is its description under a repeated Activate line; its card says the use is the activation after it, and that it's refilled before the next use.
- **A counteract on "the effect or item"** (`withNullifyLines` in `table-lines.js`; `src/compiler/counteract.js`): Cloth of Nullification and Rod of Cancellation get the counteract card's row for the magical effect or item. What follows the check is a line each, the GM's call: it's deactivated for the same hours as the item's own wait (its recharge), its magic is drained for good instead, most artifacts are beyond it, and the Rod's explosion against a Sphere of Annihilation. The Rod gives no modifier, so it's its level's DC less 10 (+30), as the user's answer 5 has it. That fallback is only for "the effect or item" and "the triggering spell" (Spell Duelist's Siphon's +20 since, still partial for its stored spell), not Splendid Floodlight's darkness beside its light.
- **A whetstone's cone at its wielder's kill** (`src/compiler/heartbeat.js`; `KILLED` in `src/live/poison-card.js`): Penultimate Heartbeat's save card is kept for later, open for its hour and pressed at each kill of a living creature with the weapon. Its failing rows' "can't reduce the value of the frightened condition below 1 (2) if it ends its turn where it can see you" is the core's conditionFloor for the hour at most. That it holds only at the end of turns it can see the wielder, and only for what's left of the hour, is words on that row's note Effect. The Reposition before it dies and the cone's aim are lines of the card.
- **A toggle for a situation on an activation's Effect** (`withActivationSituational` in `situational.js`): Magnetic Shield's raised shield is 1 better against metal weapons for its 3 rounds, as a +3 circumstance bonus to AC (`self:shield:raised` and its "Attacked with a metal weapon" toggle). Coral Aspect's Cohabitate gives resistance 5 to damage from a creature sharing its space while its toggle is on. Antimagic Oil (a consumable reading) gives PF2e's Immunity to magic for its minute to its user or a creature beside it, and the rest of what it holds off is words.
- **One-offs** (`src/compiler/one-offs.js`): Serrating's bigger die for one hit, Duchy Defender's To the Worthy!, Earthglide Cloak's expulsion from stone (beside its teleport card's Burrow, `withBuiltinOneOff` in `item-rules.js`), and Incense Bundle of Annual Blessings' monthly misfortune ended.
- **A zone with its user's own PF2e Effect** (`withZoneOwnEffect` in `src/compiler/zone-readings.js`; `scripts/live.js` giveUserConditions): Arctic Worm Chitin Shield's Misty Chill zone has the mist's save at the end of each adjacent creature's turn. PF2e's linked Effect: Misty Chill (Raise a Shield and resistance 5 to cold) now goes on its user as the card is posted (`plan.userEffects { kind: "native" }`, the ability module's native Effect). Before, its user had to apply it from the item.
- **A zone's save only the first time** (`withFirstEntrySave` in `zone-readings.js`): Candle of Truth's "when first entering the affected area, each creature (including you) must succeed at a DC 26 Will save". The save carries `trigger.immunity` for the candle's 10 minutes, so the ability module's cards leave out a creature that has saved already. A failure's "unable to tell any deliberate lies while within 10 feet" is a note Effect for that time, by the user's ruling for minds.
- **A snare set off beside it** (`withRidingSnare` in `zone-readings.js`): Clockwork Monkey is a snare's zone, gone once set off, like Alarm Snare. Its area is the 15-foot square the GM places centred on the monkey's square, so a creature moving next to it enters it. The note names what rides it and how it's rid of the monkey: an Escape (DC 18), or breaking it (AC 19, Hardness 2, HP 10, BT 5).
- **Bane Oil of the target's greatest weakness** (`src/compiler/fundamental.js`; `src/live/card.js` runeEffectSource, `rune.damageTypes`): Fundamental Oil is Bane Oil against elementals, with the damage type of the target's greatest weakness among acid, cold, electricity, fire and sonic, else the weapon's own type. PF2e has no roll option for a creature's weaknesses. So the weapon's Effect has a toggle that's always on, whose suboption is the type its user sets for the target, and a 1d6 DamageDice of each type against elementals.
- **An attitude by a save** (`src/compiler/attitude.js`): Beguiling Crown's once-a-day activation is a Will save (DC 41) at one creature within 30 feet. Helpful for 24 hours on a failure, friendly for an hour on a success, each a note Effect on it. A critical success's immunity for a year is the save's `trigger.immunity` (365 days).
- **A journey off the map** (`withBroomFlight` in `table-lines.js`): Flying Broomstick's Lift Off is lines of its card: its fly Speed of 40 feet to a destination, wandering without a good idea of it, its 4 hours, and its hour's wait after it lands (the GM's to keep, as the recharge starts at its use).
- **Hodag Toss** (a one-off): its Athletics check's Prone stays the card's. Its lines say all the rest: its user's Stride and Strike first, the throw's 10 feet in a direction its user picks with its collision's 1d6, or the toss straight up with its 1d6 (`withTossLines` in `table-lines.js`).

## Areas items make, and what their words leave (2026-10-27)

`src/compiler/area-readings.js` reads an activation or a consumable one sentence at a time. Each sentence of its effect has to be one of the reading's, in order, so none is dropped. The reading runs last in both chains, after the user's rulings. It builds a zone, a note Effect, a whisper or the card's lines.

- **A fall into water at half the damage** (`falls.js`, 2026-10-27): Diver's Gloves' "You take damage as if the fall were half the distance, to a minimum fall of 5 feet" is `fall { halved, minFeet: 5 }`, Parachute Mail's flag for the environment module, for the reaction's round. Its words are on the Effect. "You then Swim with the effects of a critical success" is its user's own move, a line of the card.
- **A readied bonus whose first sentence only pictures it**: Resonant Guitar's Chord of Protection, "putting up an invisible sound barrier between the target and the attacker". The readied-bonus card already plays the +2 to AC and the 3d10 sonic, so the sentence is no longer a note.
- **Smoke and vapors beside the weather's fog** (`weather.js` VAPORS): Goz Mask's "You ignore Concealment caused by fog, smoke, and other obscuring vapors" (1 minute, 10 for the Greater) is the environment module's `fog-mist`. Smoke and other vapors are on the Effect's words (`more`), its user's own to ignore.
- **A bonus only in some situations, PF2e's toggle for it**: Mantle of Far Horizons' Gaze upon the Horizon (+1 item to Perception to observe things over 500 feet away or in weather that penalizes Perception, for the hour), Echo Token's +1 status to the next Recall Knowledge on the GM's Lore within a year (removed after that roll). Each is a note Effect with a RollOption its user switches on and the FlatModifier predicated on it, as `situational.js` does.
- **Words on its user for its time**: Spectacles of Piercing Sight's minute of seeing through solid matter, and Effervescent Ampoule's walk on water until the end of its turn (no weight-sensitive pressure plates).
- **What it tells, whispered** to the GM and its user (the user's ruling): Detector Stone's undead within 60 feet (DC 23 for those hiding) and its evil undead auras, Earthsight Box's miniature of the stone around, and Sighting Shot's view along its course with its light.
- **Notes checked** (`notes-checked.js`): Prismatic Dust's colour (a no-effect plan now as well as one with no plan, `decided.js`), Cube of Recall's attuning, Ruby String, Colorful Coating (Blue), Preserving Shot, Explorer's Yurt, the Insistent Door Knockers' doors, Mutator Onyx, Private Workshop, Sandcastle, Blooming Lotus Seed Pod and Spider Chair's Web Swing.
- **Areas of light and darkness**: Sun Slayer's Sunset (dim light, a 60-foot emanation for a minute), Undertaker's Manifest (dim light, 30 feet around its user for 3 rounds, with its next shadow or void spell) and Bottled Night (a 15-foot burst thrown 20 feet for a minute, its light sources shedding only 5 feet of dim light) are the ability module's zone for their time. Their lighting is the zone's note, the scene's light the GM's to set, as Shadow Cloak Snare's darkness is.
- **A property rune for the creatures in a light**: Ghost Lantern gives ghost touch on every weapon and unarmed attack to each creature in its bright light (a hooded lantern's 30 feet) for 5 minutes. It's the zone's rule inside (`zone.inside`), PF2e's AdjustStrike adding the rune, given and taken as a creature comes and goes. Closing the shutters is the zone's End.
- **A placed light that counteracts darkness** (Inspiring Spotlight, both kinds): its burst's zone (`spotlightPlan`) has its light (`zone.light`, as Spark Wafer's), and its counteract against magical darkness (+17 or +25, its rank half the item's level) is the counteract card beside the zone (`plan.item.counteract`, `free: "the magical darkness"`).
- **Terrain an item makes**: Jar of Shifting Sands' Spill Sand is an adjacent square of difficult terrain, and its "can't use either of the jar's activations for 1 minute" is `trigger.recharge` shared with Avalanche of Sand. Fleshgem (Earthspeaker) is the ways card: one action makes the shards last 1 round, two actions 1 minute. Each way is a 10-foot burst around its user, difficult and hazardous terrain (2 piercing) for the others. Swamp Lily Quilt is a 10-foot square of mud for as long as the quilt stays. Colorful Coating (Yellow)'s square gives +2 circumstance to Climb (`action:climb`), and the terrain it eases is the GM's call.
- **A thrown burst's save, then hazardous terrain**: Flask of Elemental Earth is the zone placed within 60 feet for an hour. Its basic Reflex save falls on the creatures there as it's made, and its 1d6 persistent bleed on moving into it or ending a turn there (PF2e's persistent bleed doesn't stack, so it's once a turn, as its words say).
- **What those inside get**: Healing Vapor (each grade) is a 5-foot burst for 10 minutes with its item bonus to saves against diseases and poisons (PF2e's `item:trait:disease` and `item:trait:poison`). Its Hit Points every minute or two and its four creatures at a time are the GM's, out of combat. Lambent Perfume's mist posts a note at the end of each creature's turn in it: if that creature is sickened or afflicted, it may attempt a new save (its own roll). It has no time of its own. Candle of Revealing grants PF2e's Concealed to an invisible creature within 10 feet of it for its minute.
- **A held consumable's cloud** (Alchemical Atomizer): a square within 30 feet until the start of its user's next turn. Its note at a creature's entering says that a willing creature the consumable is valid for gains its effect (that consumable's own card).
- **A Speed penalty until healed** (Biting Snare's critical failure): a note Effect with PF2e's FlatModifier on all Speeds and no time. Its end, when the creature regains a Hit Point, is in its words.
- **Casts whose notes are the map's, its user's own or an object's**: Cloud Pouch's Mist moved 20 feet on a Sustain (the map), Dragon Pearl's Containment field with resistance 15 to a type its user picks (the field's numbers, an object's), Pipe of Dancing Smoke's Mist where its smoke bird bursts (Mist's own 120 feet), and Highhelm Stronghold Plate's Wall of Stone for up to 10 minutes (`castDuration`; the armor off its wearer meanwhile, its user's own on the sheet).
- **A spirit's choice of two casts** (Zuhra's Gloves' Zuhra's Stratagem): the ways card. Offense casts Weapon Storm at 6th rank, defense casts Wall of Metal, both at DC 30, and the zuhra's pick is the GM's call. The wall turns on the gloves' own PF2e toggle, "wall-of-metal", which takes away their +3 to Reflex DC against Disarm (`plan.toggleOn` in its rule's own domain, saving-throw; `scripts/toggle-off.js`). Its user switches it off when the wall ends.
- **Persistent damage for a few rounds at most** (Rusting Ammunition, both grades): a note Effect on the creature hit for those rounds (5 or 6), while it takes the persistent force damage (`whilePersistent`). It says that dropping to 0 Hit Points meanwhile crumbles it to powder. Its cube of a metal object is a line of the card.
- **Walls as zones**: Limestone Shield's Limestone Wall (a 60-foot line for a minute) and Rampart Shield (a 10-foot rampart for an hour) are the ability module's zone where they stand. Their AC, Hardness and Hit Points are its notes, an object's, as the ability module's own walls are, and the walls themselves are drawn on the map.
- **A snare that only its maker hears** (Warning Snare): Alarm Snare's square, set off by a Small or larger creature. Its note tells the GM that the sound reaches its maker within 1,000 feet, and that a creature searching nearby notices it if it beats its maker's Craft DC.
- **A week's ward and its counteract once set off** (Fulu of Flood Suppression, Fulu of Fire Suppression): the week's slowed water or fire is a line of the card (out of combat). Its counteract when it's submersed or catches fire (+7, rank 2, the magical water or fire effects there) is the counteract card beside it.
- **One-offs** (`one-offs.js`): Sidestepping's Phase Through, Golden Goose, Kimanéz Luminescent Toadstool, Doomsweeper's Clear the Way and Shaping Sweet.
- **A snare that leaves its caltrops** (Caltrop Snare): the snare's square, gone once set off, leaves PF2e's Caltrops in its square (`zone.then`). Their DC 14 Acrobatics check falls on the creature there as they land and on each creature moving in after. A failure deals 1d4 piercing and 1 persistent bleed and gives PF2e's Effect: Caltrops. The adjacent square its setter may have picked, and the caltrops ruined once they've hurt someone, are lines of the zone.
- **A snare that harms what's carried** (Rusting Snare): its Reflex save's rows deal the creature no damage now. Each row is a note Effect saying what its metal item takes: 2d6 ignoring Hardness, with 1d6 or 2d6 persistent on a failure or a critical failure. The item's Hit Points are an object's, and PF2e has no card for them. Scraping the chemicals off is in those words.
- **Ground softened where the next shot lands** (Thundercrasher): a placed zone as wide as the weapon's scatter (10 feet) of difficult terrain. Which surfaces are earth or stone is the GM's call.
- **Concealed until its user's next Strike** (Sawtooth Reaper's Bloodmist Whirl; `strike.userRules`, `src/live/strike-card.js`): the readied Strike's Effect on its user also grants PF2e's Concealed, so it ends when the Strike is made or at the start of its user's next turn. Its next Strike's off-guard target is the strike card's as before. The free Strike after Ensnaring Disarm is its user's own.
- **A whetstone's light, and on what it hits** (Limning Gem): the weapon's dim light (10 feet) for the gem's minute (`plan.item.light`, on its wielder). Each hit with the picked weapon gives the creature hit a note Effect (`attackRiders` `rider.note`, `scripts/attack-riders.js`): outlined in bright light, and PF2e's Concealed granted while it's invisible. It lasts until the end of its user's next turn, or after a critical hit for as long as the gem lasts.
- **A camp's thorns and its Heal** (Kizidhar's Shield's Secure Site): the cast card's Heal at 4th rank from the shield, at every creature in the campsite. Its three-action version is picked on PF2e's own spell card. The thorns ringing the campsite as Wall of Thorns for 8 hours are a line of the card (out of combat, the map's).
## Who sees whom, and several creatures at once (2026-10-27)

PF2e keeps Hidden, Undetected, Concealed and Invisible on one creature, not between two, so what an item changes of them is words on a note Effect for its time, on the creature it's about (`src/compiler/visibility.js`, read sentence by sentence as `lasting.js` reads its own).

- **Hiding anywhere** (`HIDE_ANYWHERE`): the three Chameleon Suits and both Camouflaging Chromatophores' Background Adaptation (Hiding with no cover or concealment for a while) are a "Blending in" note Effect on the user for its time. It still can't Sneak without ending its movement in cover or concealment. The suit falling apart afterwards is a line.
- **Each creature in the area**: Glimmering Missive's dust (10-foot burst, 1 hour) and the Revealing Mists' cone (15 or 30 feet, 1 minute) put a note Effect on each creature the presser targets (`item.recipient` "targets", the area on the plan's targeting). An Invisible creature that walks into the mist later is the map's.
- **On whom it's for**: Olfactory Obfuscator's masked scent on the drinker, Peachwood Talisman's sensed undead on its user, Powder's pinpointed creature until the end of its thrower's turn, Mirror Robe's diverted foe (Off-Guard to the wearer's allies alone) until the start of the wearer's next turn, Lady's Knife's link (Status each way, for a day) on the ally and its user, Shadowmist Cape's Hidden on the creature that missed, Guiding Starknife's outlined target.
- **A bonus only in a situation**: Phantom Roll's Stealth while Avoiding Notice (PF2e's own `action:avoid-notice`, or the Effect's toggle) and Aroma Concealer's Stealth against smell (its toggle, as PF2e's Aromatic Ammunition has one), each a FlatModifier on the note Effect.
- **Cover**: Siege Shield's Plant Cover is the ways card's two buttons, standard cover for a Medium or smaller creature, lesser for a Large one. Each gives the creature in its square PF2e's own Effect: Cover rules at that degree, until it's removed.
- **Lines of the card**: what a creature's sight changes once is the GM's to keep. Hunter's Bane's attacker becomes Observed (or Hidden), Goblin-Eye Orb's Strike takes no flat check, Ghostshot Wrapping's shot leaves its user unseen, and Lion Claw's Strike follows its Stride (the move card).
- **Whispers**: Detector Stone's two activations and Singing Stone's Stone's Sight tell what's there (the information ruling).
- **A glow on the first creature its weapon damages** (`rider.note`, `rider.once` in the attack riders; `scripts/attack-riders.js` `weaponDamageDealt`): Oil of Revelation's card picks the weapon it coats, and its watch Effect lasts the oil's hour. The first time that weapon deals Hit Point damage, the creature gets a "Glowing wound" note Effect for 1 minute (while Invisible it's only Hidden, and lighting doesn't make it Concealed), and the watch Effect is removed.
- **A held elixir, oil or potion used through it** (`item.patch.categories`, Potion Patch's way; `scripts/live.js`): both Alchemical Atomizer activations list its user's elixirs, oils and potions. Apply uses the one picked, and that item's own card follows, where the creature it's for is chosen (an adjacent willing ally, or for the cloud the first willing creature to enter its square). Its 10 uses of reagents are a line.
- **A creature moved within a reach** (`placed`, `src/compiler/placed.js`'s teleport card after the save card): Octopus Bottle's octopus grasps a creature within 15 feet, a DC 24 Fortitude save card at it. On a failure its user moves it to another space within the octopus's reach. Its ink cloud under water (Undetected inside, no smell, for 1 minute) is a line, the map's.
- **Berries**: Feather Token (Holly Bush), fired or not, puts its 2d4 berries in its user's inventory, each eaten for 1 Hit Point (`item.creates`, as Verdant Branch's fruit).
- **Casts**: Rhyton of the Radiant Ifrit's Ifrit's Command is the spell module's 6th-rank Command at every hostile creature targeted; its Fatigued for those that fail is a one-off's line. Fulu of Concealment's Shared Invisibility lasts the fulus' hour (`castDuration`).
- **A rune step in other words** (`PLUS_STEP` in `src/compiler/rune-steps.js`): Guiding Starknife's darts (4 piercing beside 1d4 spirit, 6 with greater striking and 8 with major) take the weapon's own striking rune.
- **Add an item:** a reading in `READINGS` (`src/compiler/visibility.js`) with its source name, one pattern for each sentence of its effect, and its card.
## Afflictions, persistent damage and dying (2026-10-27)

- **An addiction held off for a day** (`src/compiler/addiction.js`): Addiction Suppressant's grade bonus (+1 to +4) is an item bonus to saves for the day, predicated on a toggle, "Against its addiction", on its note Effect. PF2e has no addiction trait to aim it by, so its user ticks it for the addiction's own save. The note holds the suppression (as though a dose had been taken, the addiction DC not raised). Its crafting is a line of the card: one drug's own suppressant, of a higher level than that drug.
- **A save against one affliction, now** (`src/compiler/affliction-saves.js`, `counteract.save`): the counteract card in a save mode. Step 1 finds the drinker's curses, diseases and poisons of its kinds (PF2e's affliction items, and the Effects Pseudo Afflictions tracks them with) at or below the level the item names. Step 2 rolls the creature's own save (the affliction's save type) at its DC. A success ends it, or holds it off when the item says so. Affliction Suppressant (Major) holds it off for 10 minutes, beside the item card's bonus. Antidote (Major) and Antiplague (Major) end it, beside PF2e's Effect. Mantle of Amazing Health's once-an-hour activation ends it and adds a note Effect naming the 24 hours of immunity (`counteract.immunity`).
- **Held off rather than removed** (`counteract.suppress { value, unit, stageDown?, words }`): on a counteract or save success, the affliction stays and a note Effect on the creature says, for that time, what's held off and what follows. With `stageDown` its stage is lowered for now first (Pseudo Afflictions' badge, never below 1). Razmiri Wayfinder halts a poison or disease for a day (+17, rank 5: no modifier given, so its level's DC less 10). Razmiri Mask's Call Upon Razmir's Mercy is Cleanse Affliction at half its user's level (`fromUser.rank`), its kinds by that rank (`counteract.afflictionsByRank`: a disease or poison from rank 3, a curse too from rank 4, nothing below). On a success it lowers the stage by 1 and suppresses it for 24 hours. Neither PF2e nor Pseudo Afflictions can pause an affliction, so the note Effect tells the GM.
- **Its user's own counteract numbers when better** (`src/compiler/own-counteract.js`, `fromUser { better, needs }`): the card takes its user's numbers only when they have the features named, and each number only where it's higher than the item's, with a line saying so. Flame Navette with the Determination feat uses its user's Will save (the feat's counteract check). Contagion Metabolizer, for a chirurgeon with Powerful Alchemy on one made by Quick Alchemy (PF2e's infused trait), uses half its user's level as its rank and their class DC − 10 as its modifier.
- **What its user only learns** (`src/compiler/needs.js`, the afflictions need): a poison or affliction named only in what its user comes to know ("you also know any afflictions … affecting them", "You know whether the object is poison", "You can check if the two share an origin") isn't an affliction to play. The user's rulings then make it information, whispered to the GM and its user (Gaze of the Mantis's Target Sense, Secret-Keeper's Mask (Blackfingers)), or a line of its card (Origin Unguent's test of two objects).
- **A symptom hidden for a while** (`src/compiler/hidden-symptom.js`): Snake Oil is a note Effect for the hour on the creature it's put on. A creature that Seeks to look closer can see through it: the GM's disbelief card for that hour (Perception DC 17), as for an item's illusion.
- **A poison's stages written as a range** (`src/compiler/poison-reading.js`): Unending Itch's "Stages 1–5" is written out a stage each and the item compiled again, so Pseudo Afflictions plays it like any other poison. A poison's own words that no reading plays become lines of the poison card for the GM: how its victim notices it, its identification DCs, its secret saves (Unending Itch); Taster's Folly's Recall Knowledge DC, its two compounds and its Sickened that can't be ended while it lasts. Taster's Folly's onset is read too, where the stat block's field ran on into the next.
- **A spell's weakness added to a curse's** (`src/compiler/curse-weakness.js`): PF2e keeps only a type's highest weakness, so Arboreal's Revenge's Oaken Resilience (weakness 3 beside its curse's 5) is the ways card. On its wielder it's PF2e's own Spell Effect: Oaken Resilience (Arboreal's Revenge), with weakness 8 to fire. On another creature it's the cast as read.
- **A vaccine's strain** (`src/compiler/vaccine.js`): its +2 to saves against diseases is the item card's for the vaccine's duration (the user's answer of 2026-10-17). Its immunity to the one strain it was made for is a note Effect beside it for the same time, as no PF2e rule can aim at one strain.
- **Clean air for a round** (`src/compiler/breathe-clean.js`): Gas Mask of Clean Air's Breathe Clean is a note Effect with PF2e's Immunity to inhaled and olfactory effects (inhaled poisons and diseases are PF2e's inhaled ones). Its breathing in unbreathable air, underwater or in a vacuum is in its words.
- **A swarm shooed onto an ally** (`src/compiler/shooed-swarm.js`): Wasp Guard's persistent poison stays the card's. The enemy's way to be rid of it is a note Effect on that enemy for as long as the persistent poison lasts (`whilePersistent`): its action ends that damage and moves the swarm to an ally within 30 feet, who takes it instead.
- **A weapon on a force tripod** (`src/compiler/tripod.js`): Silver Tripod is PF2e's Tripod for a minute, a note Effect with the Tripod's own rule, its kickback penalty suppressed. The weapon can't be moved; letting go or Dismissing it ends the Effect.
- **More from a successful Administer First Aid** (`src/compiler/first-aid.js`, `action.onSuccess`): Crying Angel Pendant's PF2e action is the card's, its variant by the target's state. On a success to stabilize, the target is healed 1 Hit Point while it's still dying, so the core ends Dying and wakes it. On a success to stop bleeding, its persistent bleed ends outright.
- **A dying ally lifted back up** (`src/compiler/dying-lift.js`): Banner of the Rising Star's Rise Up heals 30 with Wounded kept and a day's immunity on the item card. That it's only for a Dying ally in the aura, and the ally's free Stand, are lines of the card.
- **What follows a condition that goes on** (`src/compiler/sleep-notes.js`): Dreaming Round's escalating Slowed and its saves are the ability module's; its deep sleep once Slowed takes all its actions is a note Effect on the creature on a failure or worse. Urn of Ashes' own doom is a note Effect on its user that says the urn can't be used again until a night's rest lowers the urn's doom.
- **A dead creature brought back to life** (`src/compiler/revive.js`, `item.revive`; `scripts/revive.js`): the item card given to a dead creature (PF2e's dead status, or no Hit Points and not dying) takes its dead status off, ends Dying and Unconscious, sets its Hit Points and spends its Focus Points. A note Effect says what else it comes back without. A debilitation is an Effect for its days with PF2e's conditions granted (so they can't be removed or reduced), given first, as the spell module's Raise Dead does. Elixir of Rejuvenation is the ways card: drunk, its restore; given to a creature dead a week or less, 1 Hit Point. Gift of the Poisoned Heart is a successful Resurrect (1 Hit Point; Clumsy 1, Drained 1 and Enfeebled 1 for a week), and its curse, the revived creature's paranoia, is a note Effect on it. Whether it died recently enough is the GM's, a line of the card.
- **A body made young** (`src/compiler/revive.js`): Sun Orchid Elixir is Elixir of Rejuvenation's restore (full Hit Points, curses, diseases and poisons of level 20 or lower removed). Its youth, age, memories and month of brewing are lines of the card.
- **Curses that bind a mind** (`src/compiler/cursed-gifts.js`): Rose of Loves Lost is the save card (Will DC 27) at the creature given it. A failure is Charm with no end, a note Effect granting PF2e's Friendly condition, its daily saves and its wasting Doomed in its words (out of combat, the GM's). Tablet of Chained Souls' Spirit Carving puts a Geas on its user as a note Effect with no end (the ritual's Sickened while kept from its task; the ghost it becomes if it dies first); the clue it gives is the GM's, a line of the card.
- **Only one of its kind a day** (`src/compiler/one-a-day.js`): Flame Navette's "only one flame navette per day" is the immunity tracker's day on its user, so the cards refuse another while it lasts.
## Actions and feats for the user, choices and objects (2026-10-27)

These read an activation's or a consumable's own sentences whole (`src/compiler/user-actions.js`), after the triggered readers and before the user's rulings (`decideActivation` in `permanent.js`, `decide` in `index.js`), or extend the readers they sit beside.

- **An armor trait's action no longer needed** (`withEntrenched`): Bastion of the Inheritor's red cloak gives the entrench melee bonus without its action for the minute. Its user's Effect has PF2e's FlatModifier, +1 circumstance to AC with `item:melee` (as Tempest Cloak's `item:ranged`), and an AdjustModifier that suppresses the white cloak's +2 to Make an Impression (the item's own FlatModifier, slugged by PF2e from the item's name).
- **A rule PF2e can't play, on a note Effect for its time** (`NOTE_RULES`): Clockwork Shield's extra reaction each turn for a minute, only to Shield Block. Vital Earth's 24 hours without air or water is labelled "Doesn't need to breathe", the words the environment module's breath tracker reads on a creature's items (its `noBreath`). The +1 to others' Medicine checks on its user stays words, as no rule on its user's Effect can give it.
- **Checked: its user's own action, an object's or the map's** (`NOTES_CHECKED`): Rope of Climbing's crawling rope and its commands, Immovable Rod's anchor (Force Open DC 40 to move it), Philosopher's Stone's quicksilver and downtime, Watchful Portrait's scrying, Accolade Robe's Review (an item from its storage, then its user's own Recall Knowledge). A GM's no-effect plan is now read as nothing for this too, and its card-line plan keeps its own source and activation where it has them (`decided.js` ownParts: before, an undefined source wiped the base's, as 24 card-line plans had).
- **One-offs**: Remote Trigger's bombs set off from afar, and Gunner's Bandolier's runes moved onto the weapon drawn.
- **An instrument pulled into its user's hands** (`objects.js`, op "hand"): Instrument Harness's Ready to Play is the objects card's pick of its user's items not in hand. That the Interact draws no reactions is a line of the card (`read.line`).
- **Used within its user's own action** (`readWithinActivity` in `item-actions.js`; `scripts/item-actions.js` withinResults): a consumable that is "Activated as part of the same activity you use to" Administer First Aid, Treat Wounds or Treat Poison. Every sentence after its flavor must be read, or it isn't this. Lady's Blessing Oil's stabilize uses the recovery roll DC (5 lower, `action.dc.less`) and turns a failure into a critical failure. Yarrow-Root Bandage's one check at 5 more than the stabilize DC (`action.dc.more`, the bleed's DC the GM's when higher: a line) also stops the bleed as PF2e's stop bleeding does: a success's assisted recovery is the flat check at DC 10 (`recover`), and a critical failure's bleed damage lands at once. Curled Cure Gel's success lowers the highest of Clumsy, Enfeebled or Stupefied by 1 (its user may pick another: a line). Its 24 hours' immunity goes on the creature treated (`action.immunity`, `scripts/immunity.js`: an item action aimed at a creature gives it the immunity, not its user). Artevil Suspension's success lowers the creature's ingested poison one stage, never below 1 (`lowerAfflictionStage` with `only`: not one whose traits say another vector), and then it's Sickened 2. The forced variant (`action.variant`) is used only when PF2e's action has it.
- **The item's own Escape DC on a Grapple** (`ESCAPE_DC`): Binding Coil's Grabbed and Restrained get `duration.escape` with the coil's DC 20 (30 for the Greater), as the ability module ends a condition by a successful Escape. Its user moving without freeing the creature is a line.
- **A Demoralize immunity its user keeps** (`AFTER_LINES`): Admonishing Band's "with the Warning Shot feat, the target doesn't become temporarily immune" is a line, since no card keeps that immunity.
- **One of several actions, then damage at the one it worked on** (`readUsedSkillTo`): Venomed Tongue's Create a Diversion, Feint or Lie, picked on the card, and its 2d6 persistent poison on a success at the creature targeted. It reads the whole Effect, so it replaces the ability compiler's partial check (`action.whole`, `itemActionOver`).
- **A result as though by another action's** (`withAsThough`, beside the skill actions in `permanent.js`): Self-Emptying Pocket's Beguiling Presence gets the ability module's compiled Create a Diversion success row on its success and critical success. The contract entity's influence (the item's own +2 or −2) is a line, the GM's call.
- **Its user's own feat, its immunity changed** (`withFeatImmunity`): Battle Medic's Baton's Battle Medicine is its user's own action (a line). The creature targeted gets a note Effect saying it's immune to that user's Battle Medicine for 1 hour.
- **A mode picked on the card** (the ways card, `magneticSuit` and `captivatingScore` in `user-actions.js`): Magnetic Suit's attract or repel. Each is its user's Effect for 10 minutes with PF2e's rules: toggles for an attack with a metal weapon and for a metal object climbed (PF2e can't tell either, as with Magnetic Shield), and `target:trait:metal` for Grapple and Shove. Attracting, the allies within its emanation get a card after it (`plan.again`, an ally-area plan at the presser's targets) with their own Effect: +N status to AC against metal weapons, its own toggle. Captivating Score's positive or negative emotion is each a Will save (DC 28) at the creatures that hear it, and a failure's attitude toward the score's subject is a note Effect for the hour (the user's ruling for minds). The ways card shows no notes of its own, so each way's card carries them.
- **A skill picked, for one action only** (`listedSkillPick` in `skill-pick.js`; `withSkimmed`): Possibility Tome's Skim is the card's skill picker of the seven skills it lists and "lore" (PF2e's `lore-skill-check`, every Lore: a line says it's the one Lore). The +3 item bonus and its critical failure made a failure are for Recall Knowledge only (`skillPick.predicate`, on every rule), for 24 hours. The Interact perusing the book is its user's own, a line.
- **A longer range for a place of one kind** (`cast-range.js`): Conch of Otherworldly Seas' Sounds of the Deep has Clairaudience's range as 1 mile to a location underwater (words, the place its user's pick).
- **A cast over the GM's no-effect** (`permanent.js`): an activation the ability compiler couldn't read is now read for cast words too, unless it counteracts (the counteract card's: Draconal Mask, Pale Lavender Ellipsoid). Lion's Call's Find the Pride casts Locate at rank 5, and what it may look for is a line (`withLocateChoices`).
- **A note that burns its holder** (`burningNote`): Self-Immolating Note's 1 fire damage is the damage card at the creature its user targets. When it catches fire (5 minutes after it's opened) is a line.
- **An oil that casts on a corpse** (`anointedCorpse`): Anointing Oil is the spell module's cast of Peaceful Rest at the corpse. The undead's Enfeebled 1 on touching it is a one-off line.
- **A spell's effects changed past its cast** (`withSeenLanguages`): Spectacles of Understanding's Translate of every written language for the hour is a note Effect on its user instead of the cast, the user's ruling for languages.
- **Its user's own arm made solid** (`withSolidArm`): Aether Appendage's corporeal arm until the end of the turn. On its user's Effect, PF2e's AdjustStrike adds the magical trait to unarmed attacks that aren't magical and the ghost touch rune to those that are. That PF2e can't tell the arm's attacks apart is a line.
- **Objects, checked and one-offs**: Deteriorating Dust's three grades and Demolition Fulu's three are checked (an object's Hardness and damage over hours, the GM's, as Colorful Coating (Indigo) and Kaiju Fulu are). Splatrope Extruder, Violet Ray's returning condition, Inexplicable Patch's 10 minutes, Rusting Carapace's item branch, Bottled Roc and Anointing Oil's undead are one-offs.
## Small needs: removed creatures, retaliation, information, numbers, use limits (2026-10-27)

- **A wait said at a paragraph's end** (`withCooldown` in `src/compiler/cooldown.js`): Madcap Top's "The top can't be activated again for 1d4 hours" stands before its table with no full stop. A paragraph's end now ends the sentence, so it's the activation's `trigger.recharge`, as Rod of Wonder's is.
- **Damage to whoever touches its user while an activation lasts** (`src/compiler/touch-retaliation.js`, `plan.alsoRetaliation`): Armorskin Robes of Fire's Elemental Sheath says its retaliation in a sentence of its own ("While activated, any creature that touches you or damages you with an unarmed attack or melee weapon without the reach trait takes 1d6 fire damage"), apart from the activation's "For 10 minutes". It's Thunder Mail's retaliation card, kept open for the activation's time beside its own card (`scripts/live.js`). A weapon without reach hits from an adjacent square, so that's `adjacentMelee`.
- **Resistance ignored in part** (`withAwakenedShot` in `src/compiler/small-needs.js`; `scripts/strikes.js`): Awakened Adamantine Shot's "ignores the first 20 resistance a creature has to physical damage, all damage, or piercing or bludgeoning damage". It's the Strike card with the shot loaded and `strike.ignoreResistance` { value, types }. When the card is applied, that's set on the shot as the resolution core's `ignoreResistance` flag (core 321af5b), and the core stamps it onto a Strike's damage roll with the shot loaded. Its Off-Guard for 1 minute "instead" at a construct, or at a creature adamantine gets past (a weakness to it, or a resistance it's an exception to), is `plan.ifTarget`, as for Moonlit Spellgun's silver. The Strike card now applies a Strike plan's own `ifTarget` to the creature hit, once even when two of its kinds fit.
- **A bonus to one Recall Knowledge check** (`withRecallBonus` in `src/compiler/small-needs.js`): Twig of Knowledge and Memory's +1 status bonus for the creature it's pressed to. It's the fortune card's readied bonus for the creature targeted, a FlatModifier to skill checks predicated on `action:recall-knowledge` until the end of the turn and spent by that check. The check is PF2e's own Recall Knowledge. Its three uses (PF2e's item has one) are a line of the card.
