- **A reaction for both drinkers** (`withSharedReaction` in `src/compiler/small-needs.js`): Potion of Shared Life's "You both gain the Share Life reaction". The note Effect for the minute grants PF2e's own action (GrantItem), given to each creature its user targets (`item.recipient` "targets"): its user and the creature that drinks the rest.
- **A readied bonus's own setup** (`withSetupLines` in `src/compiler/small-needs.js`): Psychic Warding Bracelet's 10 minutes to tie on, its falling off after the save that spends it, and untying it with an Interact are its user's own time and hands. They're said again as the fortune card's lines.
- **One-offs of this group** (`src/compiler/one-offs.js`): Ivory Baton (both grades), Instant Fortress, Eldritch Flare, Ooze Skin, Flurrying, Time Shield Potion, Deck of Illusions, Lucky Draw Bandolier (both grades), Madcap Top, Jabali's Dice, Rod of Wonder, Prismatic Plate, Krasovnatype, Chronicler Wayfinder, Cunning, Demilich Eye Gem, Feather Token (Whip), Prankster's Perpetual Pieplate, Neophyte's Fipple, Kraken Figurehead's Grab Them!, Animate Dreamer's Unleash Spell, Iron Equalizer, Octopus Potion (three grades), Reactive Flash, Chimera Flail's Pouncing Whirl, Wildwood Ink (Greater, Major), Ulfen Shieldbreaker's Battering Blow, Electromuscular Stimulator and Sanguine Ammunition. Each card still plays what it can (the cast, the table and its rows' own cards, the Stride), and its lines say the rest. Checked note-only items (`src/compiler/notes-checked.js`): Collar of the Eternal Bond's Eidolon's Freedom, Ghostly Portal Paint and Ghost Ink, whose words are the map's or out of combat.

## Unique items and artifacts, built in full: the first group (2026-10-27)

The user: "unique effects that are on unique rarity items should be automated … They are special and should feel special to players." `src/compiler/artifact-readings.js` reads each activation sentence by sentence, as `lasting.js` does (every sentence of its effect one of the reading's, none dropped), outermost on the activations' chain (`artifactsOf` in `permanent.js`), over whatever was read before. A reading can keep the plan read before and add to it (`keep`).

- **Its user's own check against a set DC** (`plan.ownSave`): the ability module's save card at its user's own token, the check a skill (`scripts/poisons.js`). Essence Prism's "secret DC 30 skill check with a skill associated with a magical tradition that uses the essence you chose" is the card's pick of eight (each essence and its traditions' skills: arcane material and mental, divine spiritual and vital, occult mental and spiritual, primal material and vital), `check.secret` set for a blind roll. A success puts the quality on its user as a note Effect, a failure another quality (the GM's). The save card now says "check" for a skill (`saveText`). The core's save family doesn't resolve a skill against a set DC yet (a request), so this one counts as gaps.
- **Essence Prism's refractions**: the encased creature (or both) Paralyzed for the hour with a note Effect saying what breaks free at its end and at which levels (2 lower; 1 and 3 or more for a biased one; 1 or 2 higher for an amalgam). Placing the new creatures, which the GM builds, is a line of the card.
- **Mirror of Sorshen**: Second Glance is the DC 35 Will save from the item's own words (incapacitation, emotion, visual), Fascinated with a note Effect for the mirror's hold (hostile actions don't end it; a DC 30 Will save does). Irresistible Desire is Controlled for 30 days with Dominate's critical failure on a note Effect (the DC 35 Will save to break free on an order against its nature; seeing the mirror again resets it).
- **Radiant Spark**: Binding Circle's shorter casting is a note Effect on its user for the hour. Its consumption is the DC 49 Will save: Drained 2 on a success, death on a failure (the core's terminal death) with a note Effect on who can bring it back, and 24 hours' immunity.
- **Shadowpiercer**: Absorb Shadows keeps its temporary Hit Points typed on the card (four times the counteract rank) and adds a note Effect for the darkness it counteracts within 15 feet while they last. Rebuff Gloom is the ways card's two buttons, each the free counteract card (+46, rank 10): a Mythic Point spent, or Drained 2 given as it's used.
- **Graymist**: its reaction has Shadowmist Cape's words, so it takes that reading (`visibility.js`).
- **Cayden's Tankard**: the conditions it ends now end the Effects that grant them (`reduce` entries' `granter: "ends"`, `scripts/live.js`, PF2e's grantedBy), with a line when that Effect's item is below the tankard's level (a week before it can make it again). The fear reaction ends Frightened and its Effects on its user and the targets; the effect's save at its creator is the GM's until a card in the chat can be aimed again (a request).
- **Deck of Harrowed Tales** casts Gate (rank 10). **Phoenix Fighting Fan** casts Quandary at DC 34 within 30 feet for a minute at most; the Sustain by Strikes is the spell module's to add (a request). **Scale of Igroon**'s reflection is the same open request as the Tankard's.
- **Terrain Adaptation** (Slithermaw's Bane (Heroic)): the ways card's eighteen (nine terrains, heat or cold). Each is an Effect with PF2e's own ignored difficult terrain for that environment (`system.movement.terrain.difficult.ignored`, which the environment module's movement reads) and +2 circumstance to saves with the environment module's or an environmental origin's option where the scene is of that terrain. The temperature protection is the environment module's. Both last until its user's next daily preparations and replace the last use's (`untilRest`, `replaces`: `scripts/until-rest.js`).
- **The Beating** casts Pernicious Poltergeist at its user's rank and DC (read before). Its 10-foot burst is difficult terrain too: `plan.castZone`, the ability module's zone with that terrain, placed once the spell is cast (`scripts/casts.js`) for the spell's minute.
- **One object a creature has, taken or dropped** (`dropHeld.one`, `take`, `attended`, `maxBulk`; `listedItems` in `src/live/drop-card.js`, `scripts/drop-held.js`): The Crows' Thievery against the creature's Reflex DC lists what that creature holds, wears or carries outside a container, 2 Bulk or less, and its user ticks the one. On a critical success it's moved into its user's hand (PF2e's own transfer, then held), and on a success it's dropped. A failure's faltering grip is a note Effect on the creature to the end of its turn. The Juggler's caught item is the same card from an automatic row at the creature it came from (1 Bulk or less, into its user's hand); a row whose only work is that is aimed at the creatures targeted (`targetedRow`).
- **Hit Points lost by its user's level** (`item.hpLoss`, `loseHitPoints` in `scripts/live.js`): The Rakshasa's pledged follower, the one creature targeted, loses Hit Points equal to its user's level as PF2e's final damage, which nothing lowers. If that kills it (a creature other than a character at 0 Hit Points, or a character at its Dying maximum), its user gains temporary Hit Points equal to its level, the higher kept. Its 24 hours' immunity goes on the follower (`immunity.on` "targets", `scripts/immunity.js`). Its user's healing was read before.
- **Any item of PF2e's in its user's hand** (`objects.choose.any`, `anyItemChoices` in `src/live/objects-card.js`): The Wanderer's object is any common permanent item (weapon, armor, shield, equipment or container) of Light Bulk or less and at most two levels below its user's, picked on the objects card from PF2e's equipment. It's made in its user's hand with its own runes, for its minute (`expiresAt`, `scripts/expiring.js`), and it's gone once dropped (`whileAttended`).
- **Blood-Drinker's Drink Blood**: temporary Hit Points twice the felled creature's level (read before), three times while its user holds Fleshrender too (`levelTemp` `ifHeld`: the card looks at what its user holds).
- **A building where its user clicks** (`plan.structure`; `src/live/structure-card.js`, `scripts/structure.js`): the Guiding Chisel's "up to ten contiguous 10-foot cubes". The structure card offers its square shapes of that size (30 feet across one story high, 20 across two, 10 across ten). On Place it, its presser clicks the middle, and the active GM's client has the environment module place it (`api.placeStructure`: hewn stone walls with a stone door, the creatures inside pushed out). The last building the chisel made is removed first (`api.removeStructure`, its id kept on the item). Any other shape is drawn by hand.
- **Guiding Star Orb's Embed Location** is an Effect on its user saying the orb holds that place, in place of the last one (`replaces`). **The soulhearts'** Bolster Maiserene Phantom gives each PC targeted +N maximum Hit Points (PF2e's FlatModifier on `hp`) while in cursed Bastardhall. The phantom's new rank is the GM's to set, and the soulheart is destroyed (`item.destroyed`, `destroyUsedItem` in `scripts/foundry.js`). Phoenix Fighting Fan's Raise Dead consumes the fan the same way (`scripts/casts.js`).
- **Flame of the Radiant Spark**: the ways card, kept open. Claiming the flame gives its user an Effect with a candle's blue light (PF2e's TokenLight), one flame at a time (`replaces` "any": from any item). Spending it casts 6th-rank Raise Dead and deletes the flame's Effect (`plan.castEndsRule`). Its Destruction and the adventure's hint are lore, read and left off the card.
- **Whispering Remnant's Hear My Words**: Enthrall at DC 30 (read before). What the enthralled see is a note Effect on its user (`castNote`). The DC 30 Will save on its next waking (Stupefied 1 for 24 hours) is its own save card for later, posted with the cast (`plan.castAgain`). The week's lock if it doesn't guide them is a line. **Liver Bloodstone's Arazni's Fervor**: Bless for its minute; the free action growing it 10 feet at each turn's start is its user's own, a line.
- **Starless Scope's look**: its user's +2 to Recall Knowledge is an Effect of PF2e's rule to the end of the turn (`userEffects` kind "rules"). The DC 27 Will save's starlight is a note Effect with its rules: −1 status to saves against divine effects (−2 and a minute on a critical failure), and from a failure PF2e's ActorTraits taking away incorporeal or a granted Slowed 1 for any other creature (granted only when it wasn't incorporeal). A critical success's shattered lens is a line. Its divine-focus check is the spell module's (a request).
- **Horn of Rust**: its four spells are the cast card's pick at its user's spell DC, charges spent as a staff's (a line, since it casts them with none left too). Its aura's tetanus: a creature the shards damage (a success, failure or critical failure) gets its own DC 19 Fortitude save card after the zone's (`afterDamage` on the zone's save, `scripts/after-damage.js`, on the ability module's applied hook, at that creature: `struckToken`). Its disease (onset 10 days; Clumsy 1, Clumsy 2 and can't speak, Paralyzed, death) is handed on as the poisons' are (`afflictionHandoff`).
